auvi. cu. turicāmip piḷḷi eḻutiya maṇimēkalic curukkam, urik kuṟippukkaḷuṭaṉ - pākam 1

6. cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam uritta kāti

ivvaṇṇam tōṉṟiya maṇimēkalā teyvam cutamatiyiyum maṇimēkaliyiyum kaṇṭatu. piṉpu, at
teyvam, cutamatiyip pārttu, " iṅkē vāṭi niṟkiṉṟa nīvir eṉuṟṟīr?" eṉṟu viṉava,
cutamati utaya kumaraṉ kūṟiya vaṉittiyum oḻiyātu urit tāḷ. appōtu at teyvam,
"araciḷaṅkumaraṉ āyiḻi taṉmēl
[1] taṇiyā nōkkam tavirvila ṉāki
aṟattōr vaṉam eṉṟu akaṉṟaṉaṉ; āyiṉum,
[2] puṟattōr vītiyil poruntutal oḻiyāṉ;
ātalāl, nīṅkaḷ perunteruviṟ celvati viṭuttu, iv vuvvaṉat tic cūḻntuḷḷa matiliṉ
mēṟṟicic ciṟuvāyil vaḻiyākac cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭattiṟkuc celka. aṅkē iravuppōtu
kaḻiyiṉum uṅkaṭkuc ciṟu tīṅkum eytātu, "eṉṟu colliṟṟu, cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam eṉṟu
teyvam urippak kēṭṭatum cutamati viyap puṟṟu, ataṉi nōkki, "ak kōṭṭatti iṅkuḷḷōr
aṉi varum cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭam eṉkiṉṟaṉar. eṉṉi iṅkē koṇarnta vañca viñciyaṉāṉa
mārutavēkaṉ itaṉic cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭam eṉṟu collak kēṭṭuḷēṉ; atuvēpōla, nīyum iṉṟu
collak kēṭṭēṉ. itaṉic cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭ ṭam eṉṉātu, nīṅkaḷ cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam eṉṟu
colluva taṉ karutteṉṉi?" eṉṟu viṉaviṉaḷ.
maṇimēkalā teyvam, ak karuttiyarikkap pukuntu, " itaṉi yāvarum cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭam
eṉpatu vaḻuvaṉṟu. in naṉkāṭu, campāpati eṉṉum innakar tōṉṟiya kālattē uṭaṉ tōṉṟiya
mutukāṭē yākum. itaṉkaṇ,
cuṭuvōr, iṭuvōr, toṭukuḻip paṭuppōr,
[3] tāḻvayiṉ aṭippōr, tāḻiyiṟ kavippōr,
iravum pakalum [4] iḷivuṭaṉ tariyātu
varuvōr peyarvōr māṟāc cummiyum,
eñciyōr maruṅkiṉ īmam cāṟṟi
neñcu naṭukkuṟūum neyta lōciyum,
tuṟavōr iṟanta [5]toḻuviḷip pūcalum,
piṟavōr iṟanta aḻuviḷip pūcalum,
nīḷmuka nariyiṉ [6] tīviḷik kūvum,
[7] cāvōrp payirum kūkiyiṉ kuralum,
pulavūṇ poruntiya [8]kurāliṉ kuralum,
[9] ūṇ tali tuṟṟiya āṇ talik kuralum,
nal nīrp [10] puṇari naḷi kaṭa lōtiyiṉ
iṉṉā iciyoli eṉṟum niṉ ṟaṟātu.
iṉṉa iyalpiṟṟāya cuṭukāṭu iruppak kaṇṭum, makkaḷ nilitta vāḻvaṭiyār pōl
mayaṅkukiṉṟaṉar" eṉṟu viritturikkat toṭaṅki, at teyvam,
"tavattuṟi mākkaḷ, mikapperuñ celvar,
[11]1 īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir, āṟṟāp pālakar,
mutiyōr eṉṉāṉ, iḷiyōr eṉṉāṉ,
koṭuntoḻilāḷaṉ koṉṟaṉaṉ kuvippa, iv
aḻalvāyc cuṭali tiṉṉak kaṇṭum,
kaḻiperuñ [12]celvak kaḷḷāṭ ṭayarntu
mikka nalla ṟam virumpātu vāḻum
makkaḷiṟ ciṟanta maṭavō ruṇṭō ?
itu niṟka, orunāḷ iravu oru pēymakaḷ peṇ piṇa moṉ ṟiṉ karuntaliyik kiyil ēnti,
puyalō kuḻalō? kayalō kaṇṇō ?
kumiḻō mūkkō? itaḻō kavirō?
pallō muttō eṉṉātu, iraṅkātu,
kaṇ [13] toṭṭuṇṭu [14] kaviyaṭi peyarttut
taṇṭāk kaḷippiṉ āṭum kūttu
oṉṟi, avvaḻiyē aṟiyātu ceṉṟa, cārṅkalaṉ eṉṉum pārp paṉac ciṟuvaṉ oruvaṉ kaṇṭu
añci naṭunaṭuṅki, taṉ vīṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu, taṉ tāyiṭam naṭantatu colli uyir viṭṭāṉ.
avaṉ peṟṟōr mikka mutiyōrāvar. avaruḷ, avaṉ tanti kaṇṇiḻantavaṉ. kō tami yeṉṉum
avaṉatu tāy vayatu mutirntavaḷ.
makaṉ pirivi āṟṟāta kōtami, avaṉ uṭalic cumantu īmappuṟaṅ kāṭṭiṉ matil vāyilil
niṉṟu, campāpatiyi nōkki muṟiyiṭṭaḻutāḷ. avaḷ kaṇmuṉ ac campāpati tōṉṟi, 'nī
eṉṉi yaḻitta kāraṇam eṉṉi?' eṉṟāḷ. kōtami taṉ makaṉ iṟantu kiṭappatu kāṭṭi,
‘eṉ uyir koṇṭu ivaṉ uyir tantaruḷil, eṉ,
kaṇṇil kaṇavaṉi ivaṉ kāttu ōmpiṭum;
ivaṉ uyir tantu eṉ uyir vāṅku'
eṉṟu irantaṉaḷ. atukēṭṭa campāpati,
[15]'aiya muṇṭō, āruyir pōṉāl,
ceyviṉi maruṅkil ceṉṟu piṟappeytutal;
āṅkatu koṇarntu niṉ [16] ār iṭar nīkkutal
īṅku eṉakku āvatoṉ ṟaṉṟu; nī iraṅkal.
koli aṟamām eṉum koṭuntoḻil mākkaḷ,
[17] avalap paṭiṟṟuri āṅkatu; maṭavāy!
ulaka maṉṉavarkku uyirkku uyir īvōr
ilarō; inta īmap puṟaṅkāṭṭu
aracarkku amintaṉa āyiram kōṭṭam
ataṉāl, nī niṉ karutti viṭuka ' eṉṟaṉaḷ. itaṉālum maṉam teḷiyāta kōtami, ' tēvarkaḷ
ittakiya varam taruvar eṉa nāṉmaṟi yantaṇar navilva tuṇṭu; ataṉāl, nī aruḷāviṭiṉ, yāṉ
ippoḻutē uyir viṭuvēṉ' eṉṟu colla, campāpati, teyva vaki palavum viṭātu
varavaḻittu, avaḷ muṉ niṟutti, 'nī kēṭkum varatti ivar taravallarō, kāṇ' eṉṟāḷ. at
teyvaṅkaḷ aṉittum, campāpati kūṟiyavāṟē, ' iṟanta uyiri mīṭṭum taruvittal
muṭiyātu' eṉṟu colla, kōtami taṉ makaṉic cuṭukāṭṭil iṭṭut tāṉum uyir tuṟantāḷ.
campāpati taṉ āṟṟal tōṉṟa
eṅkuvāḻ tēvarum kūṭiya iṭantaṉil,
cūḻkaṭal vaḷi iya [18]āḻiyaṅ kuṉṟattu,
naṭuvu niṉṟa mēruk kuṉṟamum,
puṭiyil niṉṟa eḻuvakik kuṉṟamum
[19] nālvaki marapiṉ māperun tīvum,
ōrirāyiram ciṟṟiṭit tīvum,
piṟavum āṅkataṉ iṭavaki uriyaṉa,
peṟumuṟi marapiṉ aṟivu varak kāṭṭi,
āṅkuvāḻ uyirkaḷum avvuyir iṭaṅkaḷum,
pāṅkuṟa [20]maṇṇīṭṭil paṇpuṟa vakuttu
mikka mayaṉāl iḻikkappaṭṭa
cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭam īṅku itukāṇ;
iṭupiṇak kōṭṭattu [21]eyiṟpuṟa mātaliṉ,
cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭam eṉṟalatu uriyār,
ituvē itaṉ varalāṟu " eṉa am maṇimēkalā teyvam kūṟi muṭittatu. ataṉpiṉ cutamati
uṟaṅkiviṭṭāḷ. at teyvam maṇi mēkaliyit taṉ mantirattāl mayakkit taḻuvi
yeṭuttukkoṇṭu vāṉ vaḻiyāka, teṉ ticiyil muppatu yōcaṉit tolivil kaṭal naṭuvaṇ
irunta maṇipallavam eṉṉum oru tīvil kaṭaṟkari yil maṇaṟparappil avaḷik kiṭattiviṭṭu
nīṅkiyatu.
----
[1]. taṇiyā nōkkam - nīṅkāta kātal.
[2]. puṟattōr vītiyil - veḷiyē ēṉi makkaḷ cellum teruvil.
[3]. tāḻvayiṉ aṭippōr -tōṇṭiya paḷḷaṅkaḷil putippavar.
[4]. iḷivuṭaṉ - aruvaruppōṭu. [5]. toḻuviḷippūcal - tuti muḻakkam.
[6]. tiviḷik kūvum - ūḷiyiṭum ōciyum.
[7]. cāvōrp payirum- iṟappavari aḻikkum. kūki - pērānti.
[8]. kurāl - kōṭṭāṉ.
[9]. ūṉ tali tuṟṟiya āṇ tali - tali mūḷiyi uṇavāka uṇṭa āṇ ṭalip paṟavi. āṇ makaṉatu
tali pōṉṟa vaṭivamuṭiyatu ippaṟavi; ataṉāl itaṉi āṇṭalippuḷ eṉṟaṉar.
[10]. puṇari - alikaḷ.
[11]. īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir - makavīṉṟa iḷiya maṭantiyar.
[12]. celvak kaḷḷāṭṭayarntu - celva mikutiyāl cerukkuṟṟu mayaṅki veṟuppavikaḷic
ceytu.
[13]. toṭṭu - tāṉ.
[14]. kaviya?. peyarttu - piḷavupaṭṭa aṭikaḷi iyakki. taṇṭāk kaḷippu - kuṟiyāta
makiḻcci.
[15]. āruyir pōṉāl atu ceyta viṉikkēṟpa vēṟē piṟappu eytum eṉṟa poruḷil
aiyamuṇṭō, eṉṟatu: cettaniṉ makaṉatu uyir vēṟu piṟappi eytiyirukkum; ataṉi mīṭṭum
taruvippatu eṉpatu muṭiyātu eṉṟavāṟu. |
[16]. ār iṭar - poṟuppataṟ karitākiya tuṉpam.
[17]. avalappaṭiṟṟuri - kēṭu viḷivikkum poymmoḻi.
[18]. āḻiyaṅkuṉṟam - cakkaravāḷakiri.
[19]. nālvaki māperun tīvukaḷ, " mahāt vīpam nāṉku; ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu jamputvīpam' eṉpar
putta nūlār.
[20]. maṇṇīṭu - veṇ cutiyāl ceyta vaṭivukaḷ.
[21]. eyil - matil.
-------

7. tuyileḻuppiya kāti

maṇimēkaliyik kippaṟṟa muṭiyātu, kiyaṟṟut taṉ araṇ maṉikku mīṇṭuvanta utayakumāraṉ,
"ik kaṅkul kaḻintatum avaḷi eṉ kiyakappaṭuttuvēṉ" eṉṟu eṇṇamiṭṭuk koṇṭu, aṉ
ṟiravu kaṇṇuṟakkam peṟātu, taṉ paṭukkiyil puraṇṭu koṇ ṭirun tāṉ. avaṉ kaṇmuṉṉē
maṇimēkalā teyvam tōṉṟi, "maṉ ṉavaṉ makaṉē,
kōlnili tirintiṭiṉ, kōḷnili tiriyum;
kōḷ nili tirintiṭiṉ, [1]māri vaṟaṅkūrum;
mārivaṟaṅ kūriṉ, maṉ uyir illi;
maṉṉuyir ellām maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ
taṉṉuyir eṉṉum takuti iṉṟākum
[2] tavattiṟam pūṇṭōḷ taṉ mēl vitta
avattiṟam oḻika"
eṉṟu urittuviṭṭu nīṅki, uvavaṉam pukuntu aṅkē uṟaṅkik koṇṭirunta cutamatiyik
kaṇṭu avaḷatu tuyili nīkkiṟṟu. avaḷ teḷinta kāli, avaḷ muṉṉē niṉṟu, " cutamati,
añcēl, yāṉ maṇimēkalā teyvam. ivvūril naṭipeṟum intiraviḻāvik kāṇa vanta yāṉ, eṉ
mantirattāl maṇimēkaliyi mayakkik koṇṭupōy maṇipallavattil vittuḷḷēṉ. avaḷ iṉṟiya
ēḻām nāḷ, taṉ paḻam piṟappi aṟintukoṇṭu iṅku varuvaḷ. avaḷukkup puttaṉatu
nallaṟattip piṉpaṟṟum nallūḻ vantiruk kiṟatu. avaḷ ivvūril taṉ urukkarantu
ulaviyakāliyum niṉ kaṇṇukkut taṉ uṇmi yuruvi oḷiyāḷ. itaṉi avaḷ tāy mātavikkut
terivikka.
tiri [3] irum puvattut teyvamoṉ ṟuṇṭeṉak
kōvalaṉ kūṟi ik koṭiyiṭi taṉṉi,eṉ
[4]nāmam ceyta nal nāḷ naḷḷiruḷ
kāmaṉ [5]kiyaṟak [6]kaṭunavi yaṟukkum
[7` māperun tavakkoṭi īṉ ṟaṉi eṉṟē
naṉavē pōlak kaṉavakattu urittēṉ;
īṅku ivvaṇṇam āṅku avaṭku uri "
eṉṟu colliviṭṭu maṟintēkiyatu. cutamati tāṉ uṟaṅkik kiṭanta iṭattiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu
cuṟṟumuṟṟum nōkkiṉāḷ. eṅkum ev vayirum uṟaṅkiṉa. iṭiyiṭiyē,
[8] yāmam koḷpavar ēttoli yaravamum,
# # #
[9] niṟiyaḻi yāṉi neṭuṅ kūviḷiyum,
# # #
ūr kāppāḷar eṟi [10] tuṭi yōtiyum,
putalvarip payanta [11]puṉiṟu tīr kayakkam
tīrviṉi makaḷir kuḷaṉāṭu aravamum,
[12]valitta neñciṉ āṭavar iṉṟiyum,
[13]pulikkaṇat taṉṉōr pūta catukkattuk
koṭittēr vēntaṉ koṟṟam koḷ keṉa
iṭikkural muḻakkattu [14]iṭumpali yōtiyum
īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir, āṟṟāp pālakar
kaṭuñcūl makaḷir [15] neṭumpuṇ uṟṟōr
tamtuyar keṭukkum mantira mākkaḷ
maṉṟap pēymakaḷ vantu kikkoḷka eṉa
niṉṟeṟi paliyiṉ neṭuṅkural ōtiyum
piṟavum eḻuntu icittaṉa. ivaṟṟāl uḷḷam kalaṅki naṭukkamuṟṟa cutamati, av vaṉattiṉ
mēṟṟiciyil uḷḷa ciṟu vaḻiyiṉūṭu, ayalatākiya cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭatti aṭintāḷ.
avviṭattē palarumpukumāṟu tiṟantavāyiliyuṭiya [16]ulakavaṟavi iruntatu. aṅkē ceṉṟa
cutamati ataṉ orupuṭiyē iruntaṉaḷ. avviṭattē niṉṟa tūṇil amintirunta pāvi yoṉṟi
uṭalākakkoṇṭu oru teyvam, avaḷi aḻittu, " cutamati, nī muṉ piṟappil, acōtara
nakarattu aracaṉāṉa iravivaṉmaṉ makaḷ. nī tuccayaṉ eṉṉum vēntaṟku maṉivi. niṉakku
muṟpiṟappil vīri eṉpatu peyar. niṉakkut tamakki yorutti yuṇṭu. avaḷ tāri
eṉappaṭuvāḷ. avaḷ iṟantatu kēṭṭu nī uyirviṭṭāy. ippōtu nī niṉ muṉ piṟantāḷākiya
tāriyoṭu kūṭiyirukkiṉṟāy. iṉṟiya ēḻām nāḷ niṉ paḻam piṟappiṉ taṅki ilakkumi
eṉpāḷ niṉ piṟappum taṉ piṟappum aṟintu varuvaḷ. nī añcaṟka" eṉṟu colliṟṟu. ic
coṟkaḷāl cutamati tikippuṇṭu añci maruṇṭirukkiyil,
[17]kāvalāḷar kaṇtuyil koḷḷa,
tūmeṉ cēkkit tuyil kaṇ viḻippa,
[18] valampuric caṅkam vaṟitu eḻuntārppa,
pulampuric caṅkam poruḷoṭu muḻaṅka,
[19] pukarmuka vāraṇam neṭuṅ kū viḷippa,
poṟimayir vāraṇam kuṟuṅ kū viḷippa,
[20] paṇi nilap puravi paleḻun tālap
[21] paṇi nilip puḷḷum pala eḻuntāla,
[22] pūmpoḻi lārkip puḷḷoḷi ciṟappa,
pūṅkoṭi yārkip puḷḷoḷi ciṟappa,
[23] kaṭavuṭ pīṭikip pūppali kaṭikoḷa,
kalampakar pīṭikip pūppali kaṭikoḷa,
[24] kuyiluvar kaṭitoṟum paṇṇiyam paranteḻa
koṭuppōr kaṭitoṟum paṇṇiyam paranteḻa
ūrtuyil eṭuppa, [25] uravu nīr aḻuvattuk
[26]kār iruḷ cīttuk katiravaṉ muḷittalum,
cutamatikku accam nīṅkiṟṟu. avaḷ mayileṉa naṭantu mānakariṉ teruvaḻiyē ceṉṟu mā
taviyi yaṭintu nikaḻntatu muṟṟum vaḻu viṉṟik kūṟiṉāḷ. mātavi, atu kēṭṭu,
maṇiyiḻanta nākampōl varuttam koṇṭaṉaḷ; cutamatiyō, maṇimēkaliyiṉ pirivāṟṟātu
uyiriḻanta uṭalpōlat tuyar uḻappāḷāyiṉaḷ.
------
[1]. māri vaṟam kūrum - māri vaṟṟiviṭum; maḻi illiyāy viṭum.
[2]. tavattiṟam pūṇṭōḷ - tavattiṉ takutikoṇṭa maṇimēkali .
[3]. irumpuvam - periya kaṭal.
[4]. nāmam ceyta nalnāḷ - peyar vitta naṉṉāḷ,
[5]. kiyaṟā - ceyal aṟa. [6]. kaṭukavi - piṟavi nōy.
[7]. māperuk tavakkoṭi - mikka periya tavattiṟam pūṇutaṟkuriya koṭi pōlum makaḷ.
[8]. yāmam koḷpavar - nāḻikik kaṇakkeṭuppōr.
[9]. niṟiyaḻiyāṉi neṭuṅkūviḷi - pōkkuvara viṉ ṟik kaṭṭuṇṭu kiṭattalāl uṇavu
koḷḷātu varunti, pākar niṟuttum niṟi kaṭanta yāṉi kaḷ neṭitāy icikkum piḷiṟal.
[10]. tuṭi - pampi pōlum oruvaki icikkaruvi.
[11]. puṉiṟu tīr makaḷir - makavu peṟṟu vālāmi nīṅkum makaḷir; kayak kam tīr makaḷir -
makavu peṟṟu varuttam nīṅkum makaḷir.
kuṟippu:-makavu peṟṟa makaḷir pattām nāḷ iravil makappayanta mutiya . makaḷir puṭicūḻac
ceṉṟu māliyil nīrāṭal paṇṭiyōr marapu.
[12]. valitta neñciṉ āṭavar - pakimi yiruttalāl atu kuṟitta neñciṉi yuṭiya vīrar;
valiya pakivarkaḷ.
[13]. pulikkaṇattu aṉṉōr - pulikkūṭṭam pōṉṟa vīrarkaḷ.
[14]. iṭumpa.li yōti - paliyiṭum ōti - paliyiṭuvōr ceyyum perumuḻakkam.
[15]. neṭum puṇ uṟṟōr - pōril periya puṇpaṭṭavar.
[16]. ulaka aṟavi - ellāvaki makkaḷum vantu taṅku taṟkuriya aṟac cāli. |
[17]. kāvalāḷar kaṇ tuyil koḷḷa - iravu muṟṟum kāval purinta vīrar viṭiyalil
kaṇṇuṟakkam koḷḷa
tūmeṉ cēkkit tuyil kaṇ viḻippa - tūya melliya paḷḷiyil tuyiṉṟa āṭavar makaḷir eṉṟa
irupālār kaṇkaḷum viḻittukkoḷḷa.
[18]3. valampuric caṅkam - valampuri ' eṉṉum caṅku. varitu eḻuntu ārppa - vaṟitē
muḻaṅka. pulampuric caṅkam- pulavar kūṭṭam. poruḷoṭu muḻaṅka - poruḷ nirampiya
moḻikaḷic colli ōci ceyya.
[19]. pukarmuka vāraṇam - puḷḷi poruntiya paṭṭamaṇinta yāṉi, poṟimayir vāraṇam -
poṟikaḷ poruntiya mayiriṉi yuṭiya kōḻi.
[20]. paṇi nilippuravi - kutirip pantiyil niṟkum kutirikaḷ. āla - āṭa:
[21]. paṇi nilip puḷ - marakkiḷiyil taṅkum paṟavikaḷ. āla -muḻaṅka.
[22]. pūmpoḻil - pūñcōlikaḷil. ārki - nirampiya. puḷ - paṟavi kaḷ.
pūṅkoṭiyār - pūṅkoṭi pōlum makaḷir. kippuḷ - kiyil aṇinta vaḷikaḷ.
[23]. kaṭavuṭ pīṭiki . teyvaṅkaṭkuriya pīṭaṅkaḷ. pūppali - aḻakiya paṭiccalkaḷ.
kaṭikoḷa - muṟṟuppeṟa.
kalam pakar pīṭiki - aṇikalam viṟkum kaṭitteru. kaṭiyiṉ vāyi. lukkup pūccorintu
poṭṭiṭṭup pūci ceytal.
[24]. kuyiluvar kaṭi - tōṟkaruvi vācippōruṭiya vīṭṭu muṟṟam. paṇ iyam - paṇṇaminta
ici,
koṭuppōrkaṭi - koṭivaḷḷal vīṭṭu muṟṟam. paṇṇiyam - pala paṇṭaṅkaḷ.
[25]. uravu nīr aḻuvam - virinta nīrmikka kaṭal.
[26]. kār iruḷ cīttu - mikka iruḷik keṭuttu.
--------------------

8. maṇipallavattut tuyaruṟṟa kāti

maṇimēkalā teyvattāl taṉittu viṭappeṟṟa maṇimēkali maṇipallavat tīviṉ kaṭaṟkariyil,
tuyileḻuntu cuṟṟumuṟṟum pārkkaluṟṟāḷ. taṉṉō ṭirunta cutamatiyiyum avaḷ kaṇṭilaḷ.
avaḷ kaṇṇetirē,
[1]ñāḻal ōṅkiya [2]tāḻkaṇ acumpiṉ
āmpalum kuvaḷiyum tāmpuṇarntu mayaṅki,
vaṇṭuṇa malarnta [3] kuṇṭu nīr ilañci
[4] muṭakkāl puṉṉiyum maṭaṟpūn tāḻiyum
[5`veyil varavu oḻitta payilpūm pantar
tōṉṟiṟṟu. aṅkē tōṉṟiya aṉittum avaṭkup putumiyā kavē tōṉṟiṉa. ciṟitu kālattē
viṭiyaliṉ veḷḷiruḷ nīṅka, ñāyiṟu kiḻakkē eḻuntatu. maṇimēkali, avviṭatti viyantu
nōkki,
"uvavaṉa maruṅkiṉil ōriṭam kol itu !
cutamati! oḷittāy; tuyaram ceytaṉi;
naṉavō, kaṉavō eṉpati aṟiyēṉ;
maṉam [6]naṭuk kuṟū um; māṟṟam tārāy,
val iruḷ kaḻintatu; mātavi mayaṅkum;
[7] elvaḷi vārāy; viṭṭakaṉ ṟaṉiyō?
[8] viñciyil tōṉṟiya viḷaṅkiḻi maṭavāḷ,
vañcam ceytaṉaḷ kollō ! aṟiyēṉ
orutaṉi añcuveṉ, tiruvē vā"
eṉac collikkoṇṭu, cutamatiyit tēṭit tiriyalāṉāḷ. nīrt tuṟikaḷ, maṇaṟ kuṉṟaṅkaḷ,
perum poḻilkaḷ ākiya ivviṭam tōṟum tēṭiṉāḷ. eṅkum avaḷik kāṇāmiyāl, maṇimēkali
peritum accamum avalamum koṇṭu,
9 kural talik kūntal kulintu piṉ vīḻa
araṟṟiṉaḷ kūuy aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki
[10] vīḻtuyar eytiya [11]viḻumak kiḷaviyiṉ,
tāḻtuyar uṟu vōḷ [12]tantiyi uḷḷi
“em itiṟ paṭuttum [13]vevviṉi uruppak
[14]kōṟṟoṭi mātaroṭu vēṟṟunā ṭaṭintu
vivāḷ [15]uḻanta maṇippūṇ akalattu
aiyāvō"
eṉṟu aḻalāṉāḷ. avvāṟu aḻupavaḷ muṉṉar, puttaṉākiya peruntava muṉivaṉ iruntu aṟam
urikkum taruma pīṭikiyoṉṟu tōṉ ṟuvatāyiṟṟu. iḵtu,
aṟavōṟku aminta ācaṉa meṉṟē
[16] naṟumalar allatu piṟamaram coriyātu;
paṟaviyum mutirciṟi pāṅku ceṉṟu atirātu;
tēvarkōṉ iṭṭa māmaṇip pīṭiki,
[17]piṟappu viḷaṅku aviroḷi aṟattaki ācaṉam
oru kālattē, nākavēntar iruvar ip pīṭikiyik kaṇṭu, ovvoruvarum tamakkē uriyateṉak
karutikkoṇṭu eṭukkaluṟṟu āṟṟārāyiṉar. āyiṉum, ataṉpāṟ koṇṭa āciyāl iruva rum
oruvarōṭoruvar peruntāṉiyuṭaṉ neruṅkip pōr cey taṉar. atupōḻtu, avar muṉ puttar
perumāṉ tōṉṟi, [18]"iruñ ceru oḻimiṉ; ītu ematu," eṉṟu colli, ataṉmītu
iruntu aṟaṅ kūṟiṉaṉ. itu putta pīṭikiyiṉ varalāṟu.
---
[1].'ñāḻal - oruvakik koṉṟi; pulinakakkoṉṟi .
[2]. tāḻkaṇ acumpu - āḻnta nīr nilic cēṟṟil.
[3]. kuṇṭu nīr ilañci - āḻamāṉa nīriyuṭiya poykik kari.
[4]. muṭakkāl puṉṉi - vaḷinta aṭimarattiyuṭiya puṉṉi.
[5]. veyil varavu oḻitta-veyil nuḻiyātapaṭi neruṅkat taḻitta.
[6]. naṭukku ṟū um - naṭukkam koḷḷum.
[7]. elvaḷi - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷiyiṉi yuṭiyavaḷē.
[8`. viñciyil tōṉṟiya - vittiyāl tōṉṟiṉa.
[9]. kural talik kūntal - pūṅkottukkaḷic cūṭiya kūntal.
[10]. vīḻtuyar - mikka tuyaram. [11]. viḻumakkiḷavi - tuyarattip pulappaṭukkum col.
[12]. tanti - kōvalaṉ. [13]. vevviṉi uruppa - tīviṉi varutta.
[14]. kōṟṟoṭi- mātar - tiraṇṭavaḷi yaṇinta kātaliyākiya kaṇṇaki
[15]. uḻanta - veṭṭuṇṭu varuntiya.
[16]. naṟumalar allatu maram piṟacoriyātu - 5ṟiya pūkkaḷit tavira, ēṉi evaṟṟiyum
maraṅkaḷ utirppatilli.
[17]. piṟappu viḷaṅku aviroḷi - aṟattaki ācaṉam- aṟavōr kāṇ pārkkup paḻampiṟappu
viḷaṅkutaṟku ētuvākiya oḷi poruntiya vīṟ virukkum aḻakuṭiya ācaṉam.
[18]. iruñ ceru - mikka pōr.
--------

9. pīṭiki kaṇṭu piṟappu uṇarnta kāti

ivvaṇṇam tōṉṟiya putta pīṭikiyik kaṇṭatum maṇi mēkali taṉṉimaṟantu, ataṉ arukē ceṉṟu,
talimēṟ kikuvittu uvakiyāṟ kaṇṇīr corintu, vaṇaṅki ataṉi mummuṟi valam vantu
vaḻipaṭṭu niṉṟāḷ. anniliyē avaṭkum paḻam piṟappiṉ ceytikaḷ niṉiviṟku vantaṉa.
uṭaṉē, avaḷ,
[1]" toḻutaki mātava, [2] tuṇiporuḷ uṇarntōy!
[3] kāyaṅ kariyil nī urittati yellām,
[4] vāyē ākutal mayakkaṟa uṇarntēṉ.
kāntāra nāṭṭup pūruvatēya maṉṉaṉāṉa mattipati eṉṉum aracaṉ taṉṉāṭṭiṟku nikaḻavirunta
perun tīṅkiṉip piramatarumaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉāl uṇarntu, taṉṉāṭṭu makkaḷuṭaṉ iṭavayam
eṉṉum taṉ nakariṉ nīṅki, avanti nōkkic ceṉṟu koṇṭiruk tāṉ. orukāl avaṉ kāyaṅkari
eṉṉum yāṟṟiṉ kariyil pāṭi yamittut taṅkiyirukkiyil, am muṉivaṉ kūṟiyavāṟē
nāṭu [5]nila naṭukkattāl keṭṭaḻiyak kēṭṭaṟintu varuntiṉaṉ. appōḻtu ammuṉivaṉ
mīṇṭum vantu avvaracaṉukkum, kuṭimakkaṭkum nal laṟam urittaṉaṉ. annāḷil,
aravak kaṭaloli acōtaram, āḷum
iravi vaṉmaṉ oruperun tēvi,
[6] alattakac cīṟaṭi amutapati vayiṟṟu
ilakkumi eṉṉum peyarpeṟṟup piṟantēṉ."
attipatiyeṉum aracaṉ peruntēvi
cittipuram āḷum cītaraṉ tirumakaḷ,
nīlapati yeṉum nēriḻi vayiṟṟil,
kāli ñāyiṟṟuk katirpōl tōṉṟiya
irākulaṉ [7]taṉakkup pukkēṉ; avaṉoṭu
[8] parāvaru marapiṉ niṉ pātam paṇitalum
eṭṭiru nāḷil ivvirākulaṉ taṉṉit
[9] tiṭṭi viṭam uṇum; cel uyir pōṉāl,
tīyaḻal avaṉoṭu cēyiḻi mūḻkuvi;
[10] ētu nikaḻcci īṅkiṉṟu; ātaliṉ,
[11] kavēra kaṉṉip peyaroṭu viḷaṅkiya
[12] tavākkaḷi mūtūrc ceṉṟu piṟappeytuti;
aṇiyiḻi, niṉakku ōr [13]aruntuyar varum nāḷ,
maṇimēkalā teyvam vantu tōṉṟi,
aṉṟu appatiyil āriruḷ eṭuttut
teṉ tici maruṅkil ōr tīviṭi vittalum,
[14] vēka ventiṟal nākanāṭ ṭaracar
[15]ciṉamācu oḻittu [16]maṉamācu tīrttu āṅku
[17]aṟaccevi tiṟantu [18]maṟaccevi aṭittup
[19] piṟavippiṇi maruttuvaṉ iruntaṟam urikkuma
[20] tiruntoḷi yācaṉam ceṉṟu ki toḻuti;
aṉṟip pakalē uṉ piṟap puṇarntīṅku
iṉṟu yāṉ uritta uriteḷivāy eṉa
urittaṉi; atukēṭṭu, eṉ kātalaṉ piṟappum kāṭṭāyō eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉēṉ. atupōtu, at
tīviṭi niṉṉikkoṇṭu ceṉṟa teyvam tōṉṟi,avaṉik kāṭṭi, ' ivaṉē niṉ kaṇavaṉ' eṉṟu
collumeṉṟu moḻintaṉi. at teyvam iṉṉum vantilatē" eṉṟu urittu niṉṟu aḻutāḷ.
------
[1]. " toḻutaki mātava '- itu mutal maṇimēkali muṉṉip piṟavi yil taṉ muṉtōṉṟi
nallavi kūṟiya piramatarumaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉi ap pīṭikiyil etirpeytu koṇṭu kūṟukiṉ
ṟāḷ. toḻutaki mātava - yāvarum toḻattakka periya tavattiyuṭiya piramataruma muṉiva.
[2]. tuṇiporuḷ - meypporuḷ. [3]. kāyaṅkari - oru yāṟu.
[4]. vāy - uṇmi . [5]. nilanaṭukkam - pūkampam.
[6]. alattakaccīṟaṭi amutapati - cempañciyūṭṭiya ciṟiya aṭikaḷi yuṭiya amutapati eṉka.
[7]. taṉakkuppukkēṉ - taṉakku maṉi viyāyiṉēṉ.
[8]. parāvarum marapu - vaḻipaṭum muṟimi.
[9]. tiṭṭiviṭam - kaṇṇil viṭam uṭiyatoru pāmpu.
[10]. ētu nikaḻcci īṅku iṉṟu - ñāṉampiṟattaṟkuriya takuti ippiṟappil illi.
[11]. kavērakaṉṉi - kāviri.
[12]. tavākkaḷimū tūr - keṭāta celvak kaḷippiṉi yuṭiya paḻiya vūrākiya
kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam.
[13]. aruntuyar - utayakumaraṉāl maṇimēkalik kuṇṭāvatu pōṉ ṟirukkum tuṉpam.
[14]. vēkaventiṟal - ciṉamum mikka tiṟalumuṭiya,
[15]. ciṉa mācu - ciṉa mākiya kuṟṟam.
[16]. maṉamācu - maṉa vaḻukkākiya kāmamum mayakkamum.
[17]. aṟaccevi - aṟattik kēṭṭaṟ kuriyakātu.
[18]. maṟaccevi - pāvattik kēṭkum kātu.
[19]. piṟavippiṇi maruttuvaṉ - piṟavi nōykku maruntākiya ñāṉa maḷikkum puttaṉ.
[20]. tiruntoḷi ācaṉam - aḻakiya oḷi viḷaṅkum pīṭiki.
---------------

10. mantiram koṭutta kāti

maṇimēkali taṉ paḻam piṟappuṇarntu niṟkiyil, avaḷi at tīvil viṭṭucceṉṟa maṇimēkali
teyvam, ap putta pīṭikiyip puttaṉākavē karuti,
uyirka ḷellām [1]uṇarvu pāḻākip
poruḷ vaḻaṅku [2]cevittuḷi tūrntu aṟiviḻanta
[3]vaṟantali ulakattu [4]aṟampāṭu ciṟakkac
[5]cuṭarvaḻakkaṟṟut taṭumāṟu kāliyōr
iḷavaḷa ñāyiṟu tōṉṟiyatu eṉṉa,
nīyō tōṉṟiṉi; niṉ aṭi paṇintēṉ;
nīyē āki, niṟku aminta ivvācaṉam
[6]nāmici vittēṉ; talimicik koṇṭēṉ,
eṉṟu colli vaṇaṅki niṉṟāḷ. avaḷik kaṇṭatum, maṇi mēkali, avaḷ aṭiyiṟ paṇintu, "
niṉ tiruvaruḷāl eṉ paḻam piṟappi uṇarntēṉ; eṉ peruṅ kaṇavaṉ yāṅku uḷaṉ?" eṉṟu
kēṭṭaṉaḷ. maṇimēkalā teyvam, avaṭku avaḷatu vara lāṟu kūṟattoṭaṅki, " nī muṟpiṟappil
ilakkumi yeṉṉum peya ruṭaṉiruntāy. niṉakkuk kaṇavaṉ irākulaṉ eṉpavaṉ. oru kāl avaṉ
niṉṉoṭukūṭi oru poḻiliṭattē taṅkiyirukkiyil, avaṉōṭu nī ūṭiṉi. avaṉ niṉṉip
pulavi tīrttaṟku aṭivīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉaṉ. appōḻtil, cātu cakkaraṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉ vāṉ
vaḻiyāka vantu niṟka, avaṉik kaṇṭu nī vaṇaṅkiṉi; irākulaṉ uṇmiyuṇarātu niṉṉi
vekuṇṭāṉ. avaṉukku nī muṉi vaṉa tuciṟappuk kūṟi vaṇaṅkuvittaṉi. piṉpu am muṉivaṉi
mīṭṭum vaṇaṅki,
amara,kēḷ: [7]niṉ tamar alam āyiṉum
antīntaṇṇīr amutoṭu koṇarkēṉ;
uṇṭi; yām [8]uṉ kuṟippiṉam
eṉṟu urittu uṇavu koṭuttu ōmpiṉi; aṉṟu nī ceyta aṟa viṉi niṉṉip piriyātu niṉ
piṟappi aṟuppataṟkut tuṇi yāyiṟṟu.
uvavaṉa maruṅkil uṉpāl tōṉṟiya
utayakumaraṉ avaṉ, uṉ irākulaṉ;
āṅku avaṉ aṉṟiyum avaṉ pāl uḷḷa
[9]nīṅkāt taṉmi niṉakkum uṇṭākaliṉ,
[10] kanta cāliyiṉ kaḻiperu vittu ōr .
[11] ventuku veṇkaḷar [12]vīḻvatu pōṉm eṉa
aṟattiṉ vittu āṅku ākiya uṉṉi ōr
[13] tiṟappaṭaṟku ētuvāc cēyiḻi! ceytēṉ;
iṉṉum kēḷ : niṉakku muṉ piṟanta tāri, vīri eṉṟa iru maṭantiyarum tattam kaṇavaṉmāruṭaṉ
kaṅkippēryāṟṟaṅ kari yil viḷiyāṭikkoṇṭirukkiyil, aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ vantu cērnta ṉar.
avari im maṭantiyar vaṇaṅki vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. avar kaṭku, aṭikaḷ, " maṅkimīr, yāṉ puttaṉ
iruntu aṟam uritta kuṉ ṟiṉ arukil uḷḷa pātapaṅkaya maliyit toḻutu valamkoḷḷa vantēṉ;
nīvirum ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭumiṉ " eṉṟu urittēkiṉar. avarum avvāṟē ceytu ip piṟappil
mā taviyum cutamatiyumāyiṉar," eṉṟu ivvāṟu kūṟiya at teyvam mēlum kūṟattoṭaṅki,
[14] " aṟipiṟap puṟṟaṉi, [15]aṟampā ṭaṟintaṉi;
[16] piṟavaṟam urippōr peṟṟiyum kēṭkuvi;
palvēṟu camayap [17]paṭiṟṟuri ellām
[18] alliyaṅ kōti kēṭkum annāḷ,
iḷiyaḷ, vaḷi yōḷ eṉṟu uṉakku yāvarum
viḷi poruḷ uriyār; vēṟṟuru eytavum
[19]antaram tiriyavum ākkum [20] ivvaruntiṟal
mantiram koḷka" eṉa vāymiyiṉ ōti,
muṭivil, " puttaṉaruḷiya tiruvaṟam eytutal orutali " iṉi, nī . ip putta pīṭikiyi
vaṇaṅki niṉ ūrkkuc celka" eṉṟu moḻintu antarattē cellat toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
at teyvam ciṟitu tolivu ceṉṟu, caṭṭeṉat tirumpi vantu, " niṉakku oṉṟu colla
maṟantēṉ, kēḷ," eṉṟu colli,
ciṟanta [21]koḷkic cēyiḻi, kēḷāy:
makkaḷ yākki [22] uṇaviṉ piṇṭam;
ipperum mantiram irumpaci aṟukkum
eṉṟu oru mantiratti vaḻaṅki, vicumpu vaḻiyāka mēṟ celvatā yiṟṟu.
-----
[1]. uṇarvu pāḻāki - aṟivu keṭṭu.
[2]. cevit tuḷi - ceviyil naṟporuḷik kēṭkum tiṟam.
[3]. vaṟantaliyulakam - vaṟumiyuṟṟa ulakil (aṟivu vaṟumi).
[4]. aṟam pāṭuciṟakka - aṟam tōṉṟi viḷaṅka.
[5]. cuṭar vaḻakku aṟṟu taṭumāṟukāli - veyilavaṉ tōṉṟuvatiṉṟi eppōtum iruḷēyāki
yāvum mayaṅkum kālattu.
[6]. nāmici vittēṉ - nāvāl paravukiṉṟēṉ.
[7]. niṉ tamar alam - yām uṉakkuc ciṟanta aṉpuṭiyavarkaḷallēm.
[8]. uṉ kuṟippiṉam - uṉ kuṟippiṉpaṭiyē niṉṟu oḻukuvēm.
[9]. nīṅkāttaṉmi - eppōtum niṉittuk koṇṭirukkum ' taṉmi .
[10]. kantacāli - mikka maṇamuḷḷa oruvaki tel.
[11]. ventuku veṇkaḷar - ventu karintu utirum veṇmiyāṉa kaḷar nilam.
[12]. vīḻvatupōṉm - vitikkappaṭuvatu pōlum; nī utayakumaraṉic cērvatu, kantacāliyiṉ
vitiyik kaḷar nilattē vitippatu pōlap payaṉaṟṟuppōm.
[13]. tiṟappaṭaṟku ētuvā - nalla neṟiyiṟ celvataṟku ētuvāka.
[14]. aṟipiṟappu - aṟitaṟku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟi aṟiyum piṟappi.
[15]. aṟampāṭu - aṟattiṉ tōṟṟam.
[16]: piṟavaṟam urippōr peṟṟi - piṟacamaya vātikaḷiṉ camaya aṟaṅkaḷ.
[17]. paṭiṟṟuri - poyyuri. [18]. alliyaṅkōti - maṇimēkaliyē.
[19]. antaram - vāṉam. [20]. aruntiṟal - mikka vaṉmi .
[21]. koḷki - oḻukkam; viratamumām.
[22]. uṇaviṉ piṇṭam - uṇaviṉālākiya tacippiṇṭam.
-------------

11. pāttiram peṟṟa kāti

maṇimēkalā teyvam nīṅkiya piṉ maṇimēkali eḻuntu maṇi pallavattuḷ ceṟiyat tōṉṟiya
veṇmaṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷ, viripūñ cōlikaḷ mutaliya kāṭcikku iṉiya iṭaṅkaḷik kaṇṭuvantaṉaḷ.
ivvāṟu oru kāvatat tolivu ivaḷ tirintuvarukiyil, ava ḷetirē tīvatilaki eṉpavaḷ
teyvak kōlattuṭaṉ tōṉṟi ṉaḷ. tīvatilaki maṇimēkaliyiṉ taṉimikaṇṭu, [1]" ilaṅku toṭi
nallāy, nī yār?" eṉṟu viṉaviṉaḷ. avaṭku maṇimēkali,
eppiṟap pakattuḷ yār nī eṉṟatu?
[2]poṟkoṭi yaṉṉāy, poruntik kēḷāy :
pōya piṟaviyil [3]pūmiyaṅ kiḻavaṉ
irākulaṉ maṉiyāṉ; ilakkumi [4]eṉpēṉ;
[5]āya piṟaviyil āṭalaṅ kaṇiki
mātavi īṉṟa maṇimēkali yāṉ;
eṉ peyart teyvam īṅku eṉik koṇara, im
[6] maṉ perum pīṭiki eṉ piṟap puṇarntēṉ;
īṅku eṉ varavu itu; īṅku eytiya payaṉ itu,
eṉṟu taṉ varalāṟukūṟi, at tīvatilakiyiṉ varalāṟṟi viṉa viṉaḷ. atukēṭṭa tīvatilaki,
"ittīviṟku ayalē irattiṉat tīvu eṉpatoṉṟuṇṭu, ataṉkaṇ camantam eṉṉum maliyoṉ ṟuḷatu.
ataṉ ucciyil puttar perumāṉatu tiruvaṭi amin tiruk kiṟatu. ataṉit toḻutu
valaṅkoṇṭu vanta yāṉ, iṅkuḷḷa putta pīṭikikku vantēṉ. eṉṉi intiraṉ iṅkē irutti ita
ṉik kāvalpurintu varumāṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ. yāṉum atu mutal iṅkē itaṉik kāttu
varukiṉṟēṉ; eṉ peyar tīvatilaki eṉ patu ” eṉṟāḷ.
tīvatilakiyiṉ varalāṟukēṭṭa maṇimēkali maṉam makiḻ kiyil, at tīvatilaki mēlum
kūṟaluṟṟup, " puttar perumāṉ uritta nallaṟatti yuṇarntu, avaṉ vaḻaṅkiya nōṉpiṉi mēṟ
koṇṭavar ip pīṭikiyik kaṇṭu toḻutu tam paṇṭip piṟappi aṟintu koḷvar," eṉṟu
urittāḷ. ataṉ piṉ, ip pīṭikiyiṉ muṉṉē kōmukiyeṉṉum nīrnili yoṉṟu uḷatu.
ataṉkaṇ, āṇṭutōṟum vikācit tiṅkaḷ patiṉāṉkām nāḷā kiya vicākattaṉṟu, amutacurapi
eṉṉum uṇkalamoṉṟu tōṉ ṟuvatuṇṭu. innāḷ annāḷākum. nī celvāyāyiṉ, ak kalam
niṉkiyiṭattēvantu poruntum, atu muṉṉāḷil āputtiraṉ kiyil iruntatu.
āṅku atiṟpeyta [7]āruyir maruntu
vāṅkunar kiyakam varuttuta lallatu,
tāṉ [8]tolivillāt takimiyatākum;
naṟumalark kōti ! niṉ [9] ūrāṅkaṇ
aṟavaṇaṉ taṉ pāl kēṭkuvi itaṉ tiṟam
eṉṟu moḻintāḷ. maṇimēkaliyum uṭaṉē eḻuntu ceṉṟu pāta pīṭikiyit toḻutu
valaṅkoṇṭu, kōmukiyiyum av vāṟē vaṇaṅkittoḻutu niṉṟāḷ. ciṟitu pōḻtil,
tīvatilaki moḻinta vaṇṇamē, amuta curapiyeṉṉum uṇkalam tōṉṟi, avaḷ kiyakattēvantu
poruntiṟṟu.
pāttiram peṟṟa pintoṭi maṭavāḷ
[10] māttiri yiṉṟi maṉamakiḻ veyti,
[11] māraṉi vellum vīra, niṉṉaṭi,
[12] tīneṟik kaṭumpaki kaṭintōy, niṉṉaṭi,
piṟarkku aṟam muyalum periyōy, niṉṉaṭi,
[13] tuṟakkam vēṇṭāt tollōy, niṉṉaṭi,
[14] eṇ piṟakkoḻiya iṟantōy, niṉṉaṭi,
[15 kaṇ piṟarkku aḷikkum kaṇṇōy, niṉṉaṭi,
tīmoḻik kaṭitta ceviyōy, niṉṉaṭi,
[16] vāymoḻi ciṟanta nāvōy, niṉṉaṭi.
[17] narakar tuyar keṭa naṭappōy, niṉṉaṭi
[18] urakar tuyaram oḻippōy, niṉṉaṭi
vaṇaṅkuta lallatu vāḻttal, eṉ nāviṟku
aṭaṅkātu
eṉṟu ivvāṟu maṇimēkali paraviṉāḷ. tīvatilakiyum ap pāta pīṭikiyip paṇintu pōṟṟi,
maṇimēkalikku avvuṇkalattiṉ uyarvu collat toṭaṅki,
"kuṭippiṟap paḻikkum, [19]viḻuppam kollum,
piṭitta kalvip perumpuṇi [20] viṭūum,
nāṇ aṇi kaḷiyum, āṇ eḻil citikkum,
pūṇ muli mātaroṭu [21]puṟaṅkaṭi niṟuttum
pacippiṇi eṉṉum pāvi; atu tīrttōr,
[22] icic col aḷavikku eṉ nā nimirātu "
eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ. piṉpu, orukālattē peru vaṟam ulakil tōṉṟi, varutta, evvuyirum
pacippiṇiyāl peruntuyar uḻappa, kōcika muṉivaṉ, pacimikutiyāl nāyūṉit tiṉṉa
muyaṉṟu, ataṉippeṟṟu, muṟimippaṭi toṭakkattē intiraṉukkup paṭittup piṉ tāṉ
uṇṇaluṟṟāṉ. uṭaṉē intiraṉ tōṉṟi maḻivaḷam curantāṉ. ataṉ piṉpē ulakil vaciyum
vaḷamum perukiṉa. at tēvaṉum nalla ciṟappip peṟṟāṉ.
[23]āṟṟunarkku aḷippōr, [24] aṟavili pakarvōr,
[25]āṟṟā mākkaḷ arumpaci kaḷi vōr,
mēṟṟē ulakil [26] meynneṟi vāḻkki;
[27] maṇ tiṇi ñālattu vāḻvōrk kellām,
uṇṭi koṭuttōr uyirkoṭut tōrē,
ivvāṟu tīvatilaki pacittōrkku uṇavu nalkum aṟattuṟi yiṉ pāṅkiṉi urikka,
maṇimēkali, "muṟpiṟappil, yāṉ cātu cakkara muṉivaṉukku uṇavūṭṭiya aṟappayaṉāl ip
piṟappil ic curapiyiṉiyaṭivēṉāyiṉēṉ. niṟka, in nāvalantīvil veyileṉa muṉiyātu
puyaleṉa maṭiyātu paci nōyāl varunti, piṟar piṟar illantōṟum ceṉṟu irappōr palar
uḷar. avarkku uṇavu tantu avar mukam kuḷirvatik kāṇa vēṭkiṉṟēṉ" eṉṟu urit tāḷ.
avaṭku at tīvatilakiyum, " aṉṉāy, ic curapitāṉum, aruḷ mikavuṭiya
ciṟantōrkkallatu, cevvaṉam uṇavu curavātu; iḵtu ataṉ iyalpu; itaṉi muṉpē colla
maṟantēṉ" eṉṟāḷ.
ciṟitu ivvāṟu pēciyiruntapiṉ maṇimēkali tīvatilakipāl viṭi peṟṟukkoṇṭu, putta
pīṭikiyi valam vantu vaṇaṅki, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiṟku vāṉvaḻiyāka vantu cērntaṉaḷ.
ivaḷ varutaṟkuḷ, nāṭkaḷ ēḻu ceṉṟaṉa. " maṇimēkalā tey vam urittavāṟu iṉṉum
maṇimēkali vantilaḷē" eṉṟu mātavi yum cutamatiyum maṉam mayaṅkiyiruntaṉar. caṭēreṉa
maṇimēkali avar muṉṉē vantu tōṉṟi niṉṟaṉaḷ. avviruvarum avaḷit taḻuvi makiḻcci
mikkaṉar. avarkaṭku maṇimēkali, nikaḻntatu muṟṟum kūṟi, avar tam paḻam piṟappiyum
eṭuttōtaluṟṟu,
iravi vaṉmaṉ oru perumakaḷē,
[28]turakat tāṉit tuccayaṉ tēvi,
amutapati vayiṟṟu [29]aritiṉ tōṉṟit
[30] tavviya rākiya tāriyum vīriyum
[31]avviya rāyiṉīr nummaṭi toḻutēṉ;
vāyvatāka māṉiṭa yākkiyiṉ
tīviṉi yaṟukkum ceytavam numakku īṅku
aṟavaṇa vaṭikaḷ tampāl peṟumiṉ
ceṟitoṭi nallīr umpiṟappu īṅkiḵtu
eṉṟu colliviṭṭu, " iḵtō, eṉ kiyiluḷḷa amuta curapi paṇṭu āputtiraṉ kiyakat
tiruntatākum; itaṉi nīvirum toḻu miṉ " eṉṟu moḻintāḷ. avarkaḷum avvaṇṇamē
ceytaṉar. piṟaku mūvarum aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkaṟkuc ceṉṟaṉar.
-----
[1]. ilaṅku toṭi - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷiyiṉiyaṇinta.
[2]. poṟkoṭi - kāmavallikkoṭi. [3]. pūmiyaṅ kiḻavaṉ - aracaṉ.
[4]. eṉpēṉ - eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭuvēṉ.
[5]. āya piṟaviyil - ippōtu ākiya ip piṟappil.
[6]. maṉ perum pīṭiki - mikka perumiyāṉa pāta pīṭikiyāl.
[7]. āruyir maruntu - cōṟu. [8]. tolivu - kuṟital.
[9]. ūr āṅkaṇ - ūriṭattē .
[10]. māttiri - aḷavu .
[11]. māraṉ - aṟattiṟkumāṟāṉa ācikaḷi maṉattil eḻuppum oru tēvaṉ. ivaṉ maṉmataṉ
allaṉ.
[12]. tīneṟik kaṭumpaki - pāva vaḻikaḷākiya mikka paki. pāvaneṟi oruvaṉip pakivaṉpōlat
tuṉpattukkuḷḷākkutaliṉ " kaṭumpaki ' yeṉṟār,
[13]. aḻivillāta niruvāṇatti virumputalāl, aḻiyum iyalpiṉa tāṉa tuṟakkam
vēṇṭappaṭātāyiṟṟu.
[14]. eṇ piṟakku oḻiya iṟantōy - māntar eṇṇamuṭiyāta uyar nili yaṭintōy. piṟakku
oḻiya - piṟpaṭa.
[15]. kaṇ - ñāṉakkaṇ . kaṇṇōy - ñāṉiyē. intiraṉukkuk kaṇ ṇiraṇṭum koṭutta varalāṟu
uṇmiyiṉ, kaṇṇik koṭuttōy eṉ ṟumām.
[16]. vāymoḻi - meymmi . [17]. narakar tuyar - narakar eṉpavarkkuṟṟa tuṉpam.
[18]. urakar - nākar. ivar paṇṭiyil pāmpu vaṇakkam purinta āriyar kūṭṭattu oru
piriviṉar eṉṟum kūṟupa.
[19]. viḻuppam - perumi. [20]. viṭū um - nīkkum.
[21]. puṟaṅkaṭi - vāyiṟpuṟattē. [22]. ici - pukaḻ.
[23]. āṟṟunarkku aḷippōr - piṟar ceyyum mikiyiṉip poṟuk kum vaṉmiyākiya
celvarkku utavi ceypavar.
[24]. aṟavili pakarvōr - payaṉ nōkki utavi ceypavarāvar.
[25]. āṟṟāmākkaḷ - mikka vaṟiyar.
[26]. meynneṟi vāḻkki - meyyākiya aṟaneṟikkaṭ celuttap paṭum vāḻkki .
[27]. maṇ tiṇi ñālattu - aṇukkaḷ ceṟinta nilavulakattil.
[28]. turakattāṉi - kutirippaṭi. [29]. aritiṉ tōṉṟi - arumiyākap piṟantu.
[30]. tavviyar - tamakkiyar. [31]. avviyar - tāymār.
[31]. peṟumiṉ - aṟintukoṇmiṉ.
-----------

12. aṟavaṇart toḻuta kāti

maṇipallavattiliruntu tirumpivantu kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉatti yaṭinta maṇimēkali, "
aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ eṅkē uḷar?" eṉṟu viṉavicceṉṟu avari aṭintaṉaḷ. avar narittu mutirnta
yākkiyuṭiyar; [1]naṭuṅkāta nāviṉiyuṭiyar. aṟam urikkum avvaṭikaḷārik kaṇṭa
maṇimēkali avari valamvantu vaṇaṅkit toḻutu niṉṟu, tāṉ cutamatiyuṭaṉ malarvaṉam
pukuntatu, utaya kumaraṉ taṉṉi nōkki vantatu eṉpaṉa mutalāka, maṇipallavattiṟ putta
pīṭikiyik kaṇṭu paḻam piṟappuṇarntatu, amuta curapipeṟ ṟatu īṟāka nikaḻntavi
aṉittiyum oṉṟum viṭātu urittāḷ.
ivaṟṟik kēṭṭatum aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ mikka makiḻccikoṇṭu " poṟṟoṭi mātarāy, [2] naṟṟiṟam
ciṟakka" eṉa vāḻtti, maṇimē kalikkup paḻam piṟappiṉ varalāṟṟip paṟṟit tāṉum cila col
lal toṭaṅkiṉar. 'yāṉ āti mutalvaṉ aṭiyiṇiyip paravu taṟkup pātapaṅkaya malikkuc
ceṉṟēṉ. vaḻiyil oru poḻilil kaccaya nāṭṭaracaṉ tuccayaṉik kaṇṭu, "maṉṉa, niṉṉōṭu niṉ
tēviyarkkut tītiṉṟō ?" eṉa nalam ucāviṉēṉ. avaṉ maṉam aḻuṅki, kaṇkaluḻntu, '
kaḷimayakkattāl vīri yāṉi muṉ vīḻntu iṟantaṉaḷ. attuyar poṟāta tāri, aramiya
muṟṟattiṉmēl ēṟi, āṅku niṉṟum vīḻntu uyir tuṟantāḷ,' eṉṟaṉaṉ. atu kēṭṭu, yāṉ,
aracē, itu paḻaviṉippayaṉ; nī [3] pariyal eṉṟēṉ. iṉṟu āṭum kūttiyar pōla mātaviyum
cutamatiyum maṇimēkaliyumāka nīvir vēṟṟurukkoṇṭu niṟpatik kāṇkiṉṟēṉ " eṉṟu
kūṟi, mēlum kūṟattoṭaṅki,
"piṟaviyum [4] aṟaviyum peṟṟiyiṉ uṇarnta
naṟumalark kōtāy! nalkiṉi kēḷāy :
[5] taruma talivaṉ talimiyiṉ uritta
perumicāl nalla ṟam perukātāki
iṟutiyil naṟkaṟi cellum peruvaḻi
aṟu kiyum neṟiñciyum aṭarntu [6]kaṇṇaṭit tāṅkuc
[7]ceyir vaḻaṅku tīkkati tiṟantu kalleṉṟu
uyirvaḻaṅku peruneṟi orutiṟampaṭṭatu :
[8] taṇpaṉi viḻuṅkiya ceṅkatir maṇṭilam
uṇṭeṉa vuṇarta lallatu yāvatum
kaṇṭu iṉitu viḷaṅkāk kāṭci pōṉṟatu.
" itaṉāl makkaḷ aṟavuṇarviṉṟi melintu tīmikaṉṟic ceytu uḻaluvārāyiṉar. itaṉik kaṇṭa
cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭat tut tēvarellām, tuṭitalōkam ceṉṟu, āṅku uṟiyum pirapā pālaṉ
eṉṉum periyōṉ tiruvaṭiyil vīḻntu kuṟiyirantu niṉṟaṉar. avar vēṇṭukōṭku icintu,
"iruḷ parantu kiṭanta [9]malartali yulakattu
[10] virikatirc celvaṉ tōṉṟiṉaṉ eṉṉa;
[11] īreṇṇūṟṟōṭu īreṭṭāṇṭil
pēraṟivāḷaṉ tōṉṟum;
"ataṉpiṉ avaṉāl, ivvulakil,
"peruṅkuḷa maruṅkil [12]curuṅkic ciṟuvaḻi
irumperu nīttam pukuvatu pōla
[13] aḷavāc ciṟucevi aḷapparu nallaṟam
uḷamali yuvakiyōṭu uyirkoḷap pukūum,
"mēlum, aṟam urikkum tiṟalōṉākiya antap periyōṉ, ivvulakil tōṉṟuṅkāl,
“tiṅkaḷum ñāyiṟum [14] tīṅkuṟā viḷaṅkat
taṅkā nāṇmīṉ takimiyiṉ naṭakkum;
vāṉam poyyātu; mānilam vaḷampaṭum;
ūṉuṭi yuyirkaḷ uṟutuyar kāṇā;
[15] vaḷivalam koṭkum mātiram vaḷampaṭum;
[16] naḷiyiru munnīr nalampala tarūum;
[17]kaṟavi kaṉṟārttik kalaniṟi poḻiyum;
paṟavi [18]payaṉ tuyttu uṟipati nīṅkā;
vilaṅkumakkaḷum [19]verūup paki nīṅkum
“akkālattē, avaṉ urikkum aruḷaṟattik kēṭṭu oḻukuvōr piṟavittuṉpam nīṅkuvar.
apperumāṉatu aṭiyiṇi kaḷip piṟavitōṟum yāṉ maṟavātu ēttivarukiṉṟēṉ.
" iṉi, niṉṉāl ivvūriṉkaṇ etu nikaḻccikaḷ pala nikaḻa virukkiṉṟaṉa. avi nikaḻntapiṉ,
nī nallaṟam pala peṟuvāy. mātaviyum cutamatiyum, muṉ piṟaviyil pātapaṅkaya maliyip
paraciyava rātalāl, iṉi avarkaḷ apputtaṉatu tiruvaṭi toḻutu viṉi nīṅki naṉṉeṟip
paṭarvar. nī āruyir maruntākiya amutacurapi peṟṟuḷāy . atukoṇṭu pacippiṇi tīrkkum
pēraṟattip paṇṇuvāyāka” eṉṟu ivvāṟu aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ maṇimēkalikku aṟam urittār. itaṉi
maṉamkoḷḷak kēṭṭa maṇimēkali, pacippiṇi tīrkkum perumpaṇiyi mēṟkoṇṭu āṟṟa eḻuntaṉaḷ.
-----
[1`. naṭuṅkāta - piḻipaṭāta. [2]. naṟṟiṟam - 5lla kūṟupāṭukaḷ.
[3]. pariyal - varuntātē.
[4]. aṟavi - aṟattoṭu kūṭiya vaḻi. peṟṟiyiṉ - muṟippaṭi, nalkiṉi - uvantu.
[5]. taruma talivaṉ - puttaṉ.
[6]. kaṇ aṭittāṅku - iṭamellām nirappi aṭittuviṭṭāṟ. pōla.
[7]. ceyir vaḻaṅku tīkkati - kuṟṟam nilavum tuṉpakkati. uyir vaḻaṅku peruneṟi -
uyirkaḷellām cellum vaḻi.
[8]. taṇpaṉi...maṇṭilam - kuḷirnta paṉiyip pōkkiya civanta katir kaḷiyuṭiya ñāyiṟu.
[9]. malar tali yulakam - iṭam virinta ulakam. [10]. viri...celvaṉ - ñāyiṟu.
[11]. āyirattu aṟu nūṟṟup patiṉāṟām yāṇṭu. pēraṟivāḷaṉ - puttaṉ.
[12]. curuṅkic ciṟuvaḻi - curuṅkiyākiya ciṟiyavaḻi, curuṅki - mataku.
[13]. aḷavā-pōtiya aḷaviṟṟākāta. nallaṟam pukūum eṉa iyikka.
[14]. tīṅkuṟā - māṟupāṭiṉṟi. [15]. vaḷi...koṭkum - kāṟṟu valamākac cuḻalum.
[16]. naḷiyirumunnīr - nīrceṟinta periya kaṭal.
[17]. kaṉṟu ārtti - kaṉṟi uṇpittu. kalaniṟi poḻiyum - kaṟa vikkalam niṟintu
vaḻiyumpaṭip poḻiyum.
[18]. payaṉ - paḻam. " paḻantēr vāḻkkip paṟavi " eṉṟār piṟa rum. uṟipati -
vāḻumiṭam.
[19]. verūuppaki - añcutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa paki.
-------------

13. āputtiraṉ tiṟam aṟivitta kāti

vaṭa nāṭṭil uḷḷa kāci nakarattukku vāraṇāci eṉṟum peya ruṇṭu. ataṉkaṇ apañcikaṉ
eṉṉum ōr pārppāṉ vāḻntu vantāṉ. avaṉ maṟi yōtuvikkum toḻiliṉaṉ. avaṉuṭiya
maṉivi cāliyeṉṉum peyaruṭiyavaḷ. avaḷ orukāl taṉ kaṟpu nili yiḻantu karuppamuṟṟāḷ.
kaṇavaṉi vañcitta ik kuṟṟattukkuk kaḻuvāy nāṭi, teṉkumariyil nīrāṭak karutit
teṉṉāṭu nōkki vantu koṇṭiruntāḷ. pāṇṭi nāṭṭuk koṟki nakarukkut teṟkē
orukāvatat tolivil kōvalar 'vāḻum oru ciṟṟūril, oru cōliyil vayāvum
varuttamumuṟṟu āṇ kuḻanti yoṉṟik karuvuyirttāḷ.
piṟanta kuḻantiyik kaṇṭum, ap pārppaṉi maṉam ciṟitum teḷiyātu, ataṉpāl aṉpō
aruḷō iṟiyum koḷḷātu, ac cōliyilē taṉikkaviṭṭuc ceṉṟoḻintāḷ. aṅkē taṉit
tukkiṭanta kuḻaviyip pacuvoṉṟu kaṇṭu, ataṉpāl irakkamuṟ ṟut taṉ nāvāl nakkit taṉ
maṭicurantu pāli ūṭṭi eḻunāḷ vari yil kāttu ōmpivantatu. kaṟpuc ciṟappillā
avvaṉṉeñcap pārppaṉiyiṉ ceyal orupuṟamirukka, āviṉ aruṭceyal vēṟoru puṟattē
ciṟakka, avvaḻiyē vanta iḷampūti eṉṉum antaṇaṉ kaṇṭu, ak kuḻavipāl aṉpumikakkoṇṭu,
vayaṉaṅkōṭu eṉṉum taṉ ūrukku eṭuttucceṉṟu, taṉ maṉiviyuṭaṉ tāṉ peṟṟa maka ṉākavē
koṇṭu vaḷarttu varalāṉāṉ. am makaṉum pārp pārkkuriya neṟimippaṭiyē vaḷarntu kalipala
vuṇarntu aṟivumikap paṭitta nallāṇ makaṉākac ciṟappuṟṟaṉaṉ.
iḵtu iṅṅaṉamāka, orukāl maṟiyōtum pārppaṉar palar kūṭip pacu vēḷviyoṉṟu
ceyyaluṟṟaṉar. av vēḷviyiṟ koli ceyvataṟkākak kaṉṟīṉṟa kaṟaviyoṉṟik koṇarntiruntaṉar.
ak kayavaratu ceyali evvāṟō uṇarntukoṇṭa ak kaṟavi kataṟik kattik kaṇṇīrukuttu niṉṟatu.
ataṉik kaṇṭa iḷampūti yiṉ vaḷarppuc ciṟuvaṉ, ak kaṟaviyiṉpāl kaḻipōruḷ koṇṭu,
naḷḷiruṭ pōtil ak kaṟaviyi vēṟeṅkēṉum kikkoṇṭu pōkak karuttil eṇṇiṉāṉ.
' ac ciṟuvaṉ, tāṉ eṇṇiya vaṇṇamē, annāḷ iravil, kaṟaviyik kaḷaviṉāl paṟṟikkoṇṭu
oru kāṭṭu vaḻiyē pōvā ṉāyiṉaṉ. poḻutu viṭintatum, pārppaṉar tam vēḷvikkuriya
kaṟavi kāṇātu pōṉamikaṇṭu, ataṉit tēṭi nāṟpuṟamum tiriya luṟṟaṉar. avaruḷ oru cilar
ic ciṟuvaṉ celvatik kaṇṭaṟintu koḷḷavē, aṉivarum ik kāṭṭiṭattukku vantu,
icciṟuvaṉiyum kaṟaviyiyum vaḷintu paṟṟikkoṇṭaṉar. ic ciṟuvaṉip pala vāṟu vitaṉar;
vaḷintu paṟṟik kōl koṇṭu avaṉi niyap puṭittu varuttiṉar. an niliyil, ak kaṟavi
vekuṇṭu, ap pārppaṉaruḷ muṉṉaṇiyil niṉṟa vēḷvi yācāṉit tākkit taṉ kompiṉāṟ
kuttik kuṭar carintu vīḻac ceytuviṭṭu ōṭiviṭṭatu.
pārppaṉar taṉṉi mikavum aṭittu varuttiya vaḻiyum, ac ciṟuvaṉ, maṉaccōrvu koḷḷātu,
avarkaḷi nōkki, pārppaṉarkaḷē,
nōvaṉa ceyyaṉ miṉ; [1]noṭivaṉa kēṇmiṉ :
[2]viṭu nila maruṅkil paṭupul ārntu,
neṭunila maruṅkil makkaṭ kellām
piṟantanāḷ toṭṭum ciṟantataṉ tīmpāl
aṟantaru neñcōṭu aruḷ curan tūṭṭum
itaṉoṭu vanta ceṟṟam eṉṉi?
eṉṟu viṉaviṉaṉ; ataṟku av vētiyar kaḷ, nēriya kāraṇam urik kum nīrmi illāmiyāl,
"poṉṉaṇi [3]nēmi valaṅkoḷ cakkarakki
maṉṉuyir mutalvaṉ makaṉ emakku aruḷiya,
arumaṟi naṉṉūl aṟiyātu ikaḻntaṉi;
[4]terumaral uḷḷattuc ciṟiyi; nī av
āmakaṉ ātaṟku ottaṉi; aṟiyā
nī makaṉallāy"
eṉa ikaḻntu pēcat toṭaṅkiṉar.
"ivaṉ makkaḷiṉattu makaṉallaṉ; vilaṅkiṉattu āṉiṉ makaṉ " eṉṟu ap pārppaṉar ikaḻntu pēca,
"atu kuṟṟamaṉṟu" eṉṟaṟku ac ciṟuvaṉ,
[5]"āṉ makaṉ acalaṉ; māṉ makaṉ ciruṅki;
pulimakaṉ viriñci; [6]puriyōr pōṟṟum
narimakaṉ allaṉō kēcakampaḷaṉ;
īṅkivar numkulattu iruṭikaṇaṅkaḷ eṉṟu
[7]ōṅkuyar peruñciṟappu urittalum uṇṭāl,"
eṉa avar kūṟṟi maṟuttu, " āvoṭuvanta aḻikulamuṇṭō ?" eṉṟu avarkaḷ vāy aṭippuṇṇak
kaṭāviṉaṉ. yāvarum pēcum tiṟamiṉṟip pētuṟukiṉṟapōtu, vēṟoru pārppaṉaṉ, pēcutaṟku
vēṟu viraku teriyātu, ic ciṟuvaṉ piṟappip paḻikkat toṭaṅki, "ivaṉ taṉ piṟappu
varalāṟṟiṉi yāṉ aṟikuvēṉ; kēḷmiṉ" eṉak kūṟaluṟṟāṉ :
[8]"naṭavi varuttamoṭu nalkūr mēṉiyaḷ :
vaṭamoḻi yāṭṭi; maṟimuṟi eytik
kumari pātam koḷkiyiṉ vaṇaṅki,
[9]tamaril tīrnta cāliyeṉ pōḷ taṉi
'yātu niṉṉūr? īṅku eṉ varavu ' eṉa,
māmaṟi yāṭṭi varutiṟam urikkum:
‘vāraṇāci yōr māmaṟi mutalvaṉ,
āraṇa vuvātti arumpeṟal maṉiviyāṉ;
pārppārkku ovvāp paṇpiṉ oḻukik
[10]kāppuk kaṭikaḻintu kaṇavaṉi iḻantēṉ;
[11] eṟipayam uṭimiyiṉ iriyal mākkaḷoṭu
teṟkaṭkumari āṭiya varuvēṉ
poṉtērc ceḻiyaṉ koṟkiyam pērūrk
kāvatam kaṭantu kōvalar irukkiyiṉ ī
ṉṟa kuḻavikku iraṅkēṉāki
[12]tōṉṟāt tuṭaviyiṉ iṭṭaṉeṉ pōntēṉ;
celkati yuṇṭō tīviṉiyēṟku eṉṟu
[13]allaluṟ ṟaḻuta avaḷ makaṉ īṅku ivaṉ;
[14]collutal tēṟṟēṉ coṟpayam iṉmiyiṉ
pullal ōmpaṉmiṉ, pulimakaṉ ivaṉ
eṉṟu moḻintāṉ. itu kēṭṭa ac ciṟuvaṉ, ap pārppaṉari ikaḻntu nōkki nakittu,
“pārppaṉamākkaḷē, uṅkaḷ kulamuṟi kūṟuvēṉ; kēṇmiṉ :
mutumaṟi [15]mutalvaṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟiya
[16]kaṭavuṭ kaṇiki kātalañ ciṟuvar,
arumaṟi mutalvar antaṇar iruvarum;
[17]puri nūl mārpīr ! poyyuri yāmō?"
eṉṟu collit "tēva vēcimakkaḷāṉa vaciṭṭaṉ, akattiyaṉ eṉṟa iruvariyum mēṟkoṇṭu avar
vaḻiyē varum pārppaṉa mākkaḷākiya uṅkaḷ muṟimiyē nōkkiṉ, eṉṉip payanta, cālikku
oru kuṟṟamum illiyāmaṉṟō ?" eṉa ap pārppaṉar mukam cuḷittut tali kuṉiyumāṟu
pēci nakiyāṭi eḷḷiṉaṉ. itu kaṇṭa iḷampūti, icciṟuvaṉit taṉ maṉiyiṉiṉṟum nīkkiviṭ
ṭāṉ. avaṉpiṉ, avvūriṭattu oṭēntic cōṟu irantuṇpāṉāyi ṉaṉ. avvūril vāḻnta
kaṉmaṉap pārppaṉar, avaṉ cōṟu irak kum ōṭṭil, cōṟu iṭātu kalliyum maṇṇiyum
eṟiyaluṟṟaṉar.
avaṉ, piṉpu avvūriṉ nīṅki, teṉmaturi yaṭintāṉ. aṅkē cintātēvi kōyililuḷḷa
pīṭikiyil taṅki, cōṟu iran tuṇṭu vantaṉaṉ. avaṉēntiya ōṭṭil peṟṟa uṇavi avaṉ av
vūriṭattē iruntu paciyāl vāṭiya,
[18]kāṇār, kēḷār, kāl muṭap paṭṭōr,
[19]pēṇunar illōr, piṇinaṭuk kuṟṟōr,
yāvarum varuka eṉṟu [20]icittu uṭaṉūṭṭi,
avaruṇṭoḻinta miccilit tāṉ uṇṭu, ataṉiyē talikku aṇi yākavum koṇṭu
uṟaṅkiyuṟivāṉāyiṉaṉ.
kuṟippu :- ic ciṟuvaṉē, īṅkup pārppaṉarāl āmakaṉ eṉa iḻittuk kūṟappaṭṭāṉāyiṉum,
maṟupiṟappil, cāvaka nāṭṭil oru pacu vayiṟṟiṟ piṟantu ciṟakkiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, nam kati
iṉitu cel vatu vēṇṭi, ivaṉi iṉi āputtiraṉ eṉṟē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm.
-------
[1]. noṭivaṉa - colluvaṉavaṟṟi.
[2]. viṭu nilam - mēyccalukkāka aracaṉāl viṭappaṭṭa pulveḷi. paṭu pul - tāṉē
muḷittuc ceḻitta pul. ceṟṟam - pakimi.
[3]. nēmi - vaṭṭam. nēmiyiyuṭiya cakkaram eṉka. mutalvaṉ - tirumāl. makaṉ - piramaṉ.
[4]. terumaral uḷḷattu - taṭumāṟukiṉṟa neñcattiyuṭiya. vilaṅku makaṉ eṉa ikaḻvār, "
āṉmakaṉātaṟku ottaṉi " eṉṟaṉar. . makaṉallāy - āṟaṟivuṭiya makaṉ alli.
[5]. āṉmakaṉ - āṉukku makaṉ. māṉ makaṉ'. māṉukkumakaṉ. acalaṉ, ciruṅki, viriñci
mutaliya antaṇar muṟiyē āṉukkum, māṉukkum, pulik
kum piṟantavar eṉka.
[6]. puriyōr - uyarntōr. kēcakampaḷaṉ eṉpāṉ oru muṉivaṉ.
[7]. ōṅkuyar - mikavuyarnta . [8]. naṭavi - naṭantu cellum vaḻi.
[9]. tamar il tīrnta - taṉakku uriyavar illattiṉiṉṟum oḻukkak kēṭṭāl nīṅkiṉa.
[10]. kāppukkaṭi kaḻintu - koṇṭa kaṇavaṉatu kāvalik kaṭantu (viyapicāram ceytu).
[11]. eṟipayam uṭimiyiṉ - kaṇṭōr varuttuvar eṉṉum accat tāl. iriyal mākkaḷ -
nīṅkic cellum māntaruṭaṉ. āṭiya - āṭuva taṟku. ceḻiyaṉ - pāṇṭiyaṉ.
[12]. tōṉṟā pōntēṉ - piṟar kaṇṇukkut tōṉṟātapaṭi vantu viṭṭēṉ. tuṭavi - tōṭṭam.
iṭṭaṉeṉ - vittuviṭṭu.
[13]. allal uṟṟu - tuyar mikuntu.
[14]. collutal tēṟṟēṉ - colliṟṟilēṉ, coṟpayam iṉmi yiṉ - colvatāl oru payaṉum
illāmiyāl. pullal ōmpaṉmiṉ - tīṇṭa ṉ miṉ.
[15]. mutalvaṉ - piramaṉ.
[16]. kaṭavuṭkaṇiki - tilōttami. ciṟuvar iruvarum eṉka. iru par - vaciṭṭaṉum
akattiyaṉum.
[17]. puri nūl - pūṇu nūl. [18]. kāṇār - kuruṭar.
[19]. pēṇunar illōr - akatikaḷ.
[20]. icittu - aḻittu. uṭaṉūṭṭi - oruṅkē yiruntu uṇṇac ceytu.
-----------

14. pāttiramarapu kūṟiya kāti

itukāṟum tāṉ kūṟivantati mikka ārvattōṭu kēṭṭa maṇimēkaliyi nōkki, aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ
acciṟuvaṉāṉa āputtiraṉatu varalāṟṟi mēlum kūṟukiṉṟār.
maturi nakariluḷḷa, cintātēvi tirukkōyiṟ pīṭikiyil taṅkiyirunta āputtiraṉ,
maḻikkālattil orunāḷ naḷḷiravil iṉitu uṟaṅkik koṇṭirukkiyil, neṭuntolivu
naṭantu paciyāl melintu cilar vantu avaṉi eḻuppi, " aiyaṉē, vayiṟu kāy . perum
paciyāl mikavum varuntukiṉṟēm ” eṉṟu vaṇaṅki moḻin taṉar. avaṉō ēṟṟūṇ allatu
vēṟṟūṇ illātavaṉ; ataṉāl, avaṉ avar paci kaḷiyum vakiyaṟiyātu tikittāṉ. appōḻtil,
cintātēvi tōṉṟi, avaṉi viḷittu,
ēṭā ! [1]aḻiyal; eḻuntu itu koḷḷāy;
nāṭu [2]vaṟaṅkūriṉum ivvōṭu vaṟaṅkūrātu;
vāṅkunar kiyakam varuntutal allatu
tāṉ [3]tolivillāt takimiyatu
eṉṟu moḻintu taṉ kiyiṭat tirunta toru uṇavukkalatti avaṉ kiyil aṉpōṭu aḷittatu.
ataṉip peṟtataṉāl pēruvakik kaṭalmūḻkiya āputtiraṉ, attēviyip paravi,
[4]cintā tēvi | ceḻuṅkali [5]niyamattu
[6] nantā viḷakkē! [7]nāmicip pāvāy!
vāṉōr talivi! maṇṇōr mutalvi!
ēṉōr uṟṟa iṭarkaḷi vāy
eṉac collit toḻutu, taṉpāl pacittuvantavaratu pacippiṇi pōkkiṉaṉ. aṉṟumutal,
annakariṉum ataṉic cūḻavirunta nāṭ ṭilum pacippiṇi ilatāyiṟṟu. āputtiraṉatu
aṟappayaṉum ciṟantu mikuvatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl viṇṇulaka vēntaṉāṉa intiraṉatu pāṇṭu kampaḷam
tuḷaṅkat toṭaṅkiyatu. itaṉi yaṟinta av vintiraṉ, tirittu narittu mūttut taḷarnta
maṟiyōṉoruvaṉip pōla āputtiraṉ muṉtōṉṟi, " yāṉ intiraṉ; niṉ aṟattiṉ ciṟappi
aṟintēṉ; nī vēṇṭum varam yātu? kēḷ, taruvēṉ " eṉṟāṉ. avaṉi eḷḷi nakippāṉ pōla
āputtiraṉ vilāvaṟac cirittu,
[8]" īṇṭuc ceyviṉi [9]āṇṭu nukarn tiruttal
[10]kāṇtaku ciṟappiṉ num kaṭavuḷa rallatu
aṟañcey mākkaḷ [11]puṟaṅkāttu ōmpunar,
naṟṟavam ceyvōr [12]paṟṟaṟa muyalvōr
[13]yāvarum illāt tēvar naṉṉāṭṭukku
iṟiva ṉākiya peruntiṟal vēntē!
varunti vantōr arumpaci kaḷintu avar
[14]tiruntu mukam kāṭṭum eṉ teyvak [15]kaṭiñi
eṉakku ītu oṉṟē amiyum; niṉṉāl tarappaṭakkūṭiya uṇṭiyum uṭiyum peṇṭirum piṟavum
eṉakku vēṇṭā" eṉṟu viḷampiṉaṉ.
iccoṟkaḷik kēṭṭalum intiraṉukkuc ciṉam tōṉṟi yatu. " īttuvakkum iṉpa mikutiyāl
ivaṉ ivvāṟu moḻikiṉ ṟāṉ; iṉi, innāṭṭil ivaṉpāl uṇavu ēṟpār ilarāmāṟu maḻiyum
viḷi nalaṉum mikakūṇṭākkuvēṉ ”. eṉṟu niṉittu, niṉitta vaṇṇamē ceytaṉaṉ.
āputtiraṉum taṉpāl pacittup pōntu uṇavu koḷvōr ilarāṉami kaṇṭu ūrtōṟum uṇa viṉṟi
varuntuvōr uḷarō eṉat tēṭit tiriyalāṉāṉ.
paṉṉiraṇṭāṇṭukaḷāka maḻiyiṉmiyāl peruvaṟam kūrntirunta pāṇṭi nāṭṭil, ippōtu
peruvaḷam curantu pēriṉpam ceytataṉāl,
[16]' vīṭarum tūrttarum [17]viṭṭēṟ ṟāḷarum
[18] naṭavi mākkaḷum nakiyoṭu viki
[19] vaṭṭum cūtum vampak kōṭṭiyum
[20] muṭṭā vāḻkkiyum muṟimiya tāka,
pacittōrit tēṭit tiriyam āputtiraṉi, "yār ivaṉ eṉṟē yāvarum ikaḻnta ṉar." ataṉāl,
avaṉ,
[21] tiruviṉ celvam peruṅkaṭal koḷḷa
[22]orutaṉi varūum perumakaṉ pōlat
tāṉē tamiyaṉ varuvōṉ taṉ muṉ
[23] mānīr vaṅkam vantōr
cilar vantu vaṇaṅki, " cāvaka nāṭṭil peyaliṉmiyāl uyirkaḷ peritum varuntukiṉṟaṉa ”
eṉṟaṉar. atu kēṭṭatum, āputtiraṉ,
amararkōṉ āṇiyiṉ [24]aruntuvōrp peṟātu
[25]kumari mūtta eṉ pāt tiramēnti
aṅkan nāṭṭup pukuvateṉ karuttu eṉa
[26]vaṅka mākkaḷoṭu makiḻvuṭa ṉēṟi
[27] kālvici kaṭukak kaṭal kalak kuṟutaliṉ
[28] māl iti maṇipallavattiṭi vīḻttut
taṅkiyatu orunāḷ; tāṉ āṅku iḻintaṉaṉ.
iḻintu ammaṇipallavat tīviṭi avaṉ tirintukoṇ ṭirukki yil, vaṅkamākkaḷ, avaṉ vaṅkam
ēṟikkoṇṭaṉaṉ eṉap piṟaḻa vuṇarntu pōyviṭṭaṉar. tirintu pōnta āputtiraṉ vaṅkam
pōyviṭṭati yaṟintu maṉam aḻintu,
aṅku vāḻvōr yāvarum iṉmiyiṉ,
[29]maṉṉuyir ōmpum immāperum pāttiram
eṉṉuyir ōmputal yāṉō [30]poṟē eṉ;
[31]tavantīr maruṅkila taṉittuyar uḻantēṉ;
cumanteṉ pāttiram eṉ ṟaṉaṉ toḻutu,
kōmuki yeṉṉum poykikkaṇ appāttiratti eṟintu, " ōrāṇṭil orunāḷil itu
veḷippaṭuka; atupōtu, aruḷaṟam pūṇṭu āruyirōmpum periyōr uḷareṉil, avar kikkaṇ
itu ceṉṟu amarvatāka ” eṉak kūṟi, uṇṇā nōṉpāṟṟi uyir viṭṭāṉ. akkālattē ceṉṟu
yāṉ avaṉi viṉaviya pōtu, avaṉ eṉakku icceytiyik kūṟiṉaṉ. piṉpu, avaṉ cāvaka nāṭṭi
yāḷum pūmi cantiraṉ eṉṉum vēntaṉuṭiya kōl niḻa lil irunta maṇmukaṉ eṉṉum
muṉivaraṉiṭam irunta āviṉ vayiṟṟil piṟantāṉ.
----
[1]. aḻiyal - varuntātē. koḷḷāy - koḷvāyāka.
[2]. vaṟam kūriṉum - vaṟitāy viṭiṉum. vaṟam kūrā - vaṟumiyuṟātu.
[3]. tolivu - kuṟital. [4]. cintā tēvi - cintā viḷakkākiya tēvi
[5]. niyamam - kōyil.
[6]. nantā - aviyāta. kalveṭṭukkaḷ " nuntā viḷakku ' eṉpatum ituvē.
[7]. nāmicippāvāy - nāviṭattē yiruntu nalla coṟkaḷic col vikkum tēvi.
[8]. īṇṭuc ceyviṉi - ivvulakattē ceyyum aṟattiṉ payaṉi.
[9]. āṇṭu nukarntiruttal - tēvarulakattē nukarntukoṇ ṭiruttali.
[10]. kāṇṭaku ciṟappiṉ - kaṇṭukoṇṭirukkum ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya.
[11]. puṟaṅkāttu ōmpunar - piṟarip pātukāppavar.
[12]. paṟṟaṟa muyalvōr - iruvakippaṟṟum keṭa muyalpavar.
[13]. yāvarum - īvōrum koḷvōrumākiya evarum.
[14]. tiruntu mukam - uvakiyāl malarnta mukam. [15]. kaṭiñi - piccikkalam.
[16]. viṭar - tīyoḻukkamuṭiya kāmukar.
[17]. viṭṭēṟṟāḷar - piṟari ikaḻntu kaṭuñcol collum tīyavar.
[18]. naṭavimākkaḷ - vaḻiccellum tīyavar. naki - aṟpar.
[19]. vaṭṭu - uruṇṭi yuruṭṭiyāṭal. vampakkōṭṭi - payaṉil col kūṟit tirivōr
kūṭṭam.
[20]. muṭṭā vāḻkki - kuṟivillāta vāḻvu.
[21]. tiruviṉ celvam - kaṇṭōr matikkattakka peruñcelvam.
[22]. orutaṉi - taṉṉantaṉiyāka. perumakaṉ - aracaṉ, intiraṉumām. intiraṉ celvam
muṉivaṉ cāpattāl kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭateṉṟoru katiyuṇṭu.
[23]. mānīr vaṅkam - kaṭaliṟ cellum kalam.
[24]. aruntuvōr - uṇavu ēṟṟu uṇpōr,
[25]. kumari mūtta pāttiram - vaṟitē neṭunāḷ kiṭanta kalam.
[26]. vaṅkamākkaḷ - marakkalattukku uriyavar. [27]. kāl - kāṟṟu.
[28]. māl iti - marakkalattiṉ periya pāy.
[29]. maṉṉuyir - nilipeṟṟa uyirkaḷ. [30]. poṟēeṉ - virumpēṉāyiṉēṉ.
[31]. tavantir maruṅkil - tīviṉippakkattēṉāy. pāttiram cumantu eṉ - ēṟpōrillāta
pōtu ip pāttirattic cumattalāl peṟum payaṉ eṉṉi ?
--------------

15. pāttiraṅ koṇṭu piccipukka kāti

āputtiraṉi īṉṟa cāliyeṉpavaḷ viṭṭuppōṉa piṉ, avaṉi yā taritta pacu maṟupiṟappil,
cāvaka nāṭṭut tavaḷavariyiṭattē vāḻnta maṇmukaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉpāl poṟkōṭum poṟ
kompum uḷḷa pacuvāyp piṟantu vāḻntu vantatu. ataṉ vayiṟṟil poṟṟakaṭṭālāṉa muṭṭi
yoṉṟil āputtiraṉ ceṉṟu taṅki ṉaṉ. itaṉi mukkālamum uṇarnta ammuṉivaṉ muṉpē
aṟintu, itaṉpāl,
maḻivaḷam curappavum maṉṉuyi rōmpavum,
[1] uyirkāvalaṉ vantu oruvaṉ tōṉṟum;
[2] kuṭart toṭar māli pūṇpā ṉallaṉ;
[3] aṭarppoṉ muṭṭi akaviyiṉāṉ ...eṉṟāṉ.
piṉṉar.
piṇinōy iṉṟiyum piṟantaṟam ceyya,
maṇipallavat tiṭimaṉṉuyir nīttōṉ;
[4] taṟkāt taḷitta taki ā ataṉi,
[5] oṟkā uḷḷat toḻiyā ṉātaliṉ,
āṅkav [6]vāvayiṟṟu amararkaṇam uvappa,
[7] tīṅkaṉi nāval ōṅkumit tīviṉukku
orutāṉāki ulaku toḻat tōṉṟiṉaṉ.
ivaṉ piṟanta nāḷ puttatēvaṉ piṟanta nāḷātaliṉ,
maṇṇaka mellām māri yiṉṟiyum,
[8] puṇṇiya naṉṉīr pōtoṭu corintatu.
icciṟappik kaṇṭa cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattil vāḻum māta varkaḷ tiraṇṭu ceṉṟu kantiṟpāviyi
viṉaviṉar. avarkaṭku appāvi āputtiraṉatu tōṟṟattik kūṟi, ēṉic ceytikaḷi
aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷpāl kēṭkumāṟu viṭuttatu. avarkaṭku avvaṭi kaḷ, piṉvarum ceytiyik
kūṟiṉaṉ.
[9]maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ, maṇmuka ṉeṉṉum
[10] puṇṇiya mutalvaṉ tiruntaṭi vaṇaṅki,
'makkaḷi yillēṉ; mātavaṉ aruḷāl
peṟṟēṉ putalvaṉi' eṉṟu avaṉ vaḷarppa,
[11] aricāḷ celvam avaṉ pāl uṇmiyiṉ,
[12] niritār vēntaṉ āyiṉaṉ; avaṉ tāṉ,
[13] tuṟakka vēntaṉ, tuyppilaṉ kollō!
[14]aṟakkōl vēntaṉ, aruḷilaṉ kollō !
ivvaṇṇam kūṟippōnta aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ, maṇimēkaliyi nōkki,
[15]nalattaki yiṉṟi nalluyirk kellām
[16]alattaṟ kāli yākiyatu; āyiḻi,
veṇtiri tanta amuti vāṉōr
uṇ ṭoḻi miccili [17]oḻittu vittāṅku
[18]vaṟaṉōṭulakil vāṉ tuyar keṭukkum
aṟaṉōṭu oḻittal, āyiḻi, takātu
eṉṟu moḻintār. atu kēṭṭatum, maṇimēkali taṉṉic cūḻa irunta tāyaruṭaṉ avar
tiruvaṭiyil vīḻntu vaṇaṅki viṭi peṟṟu, pikkuṇik kōlattuṭaṉ, piccippāttiram
kiyil viḷaṅ kak kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattup perunteruvi aṭintāḷ. avaḷik kaṇṭa ūravarum
piṟarum,
utaya kumaraṉ uḷamkoṇ ṭoḷitta
[19] matumalark kuḻalāḷ vantu tōṉṟip
piccip pāttiram kiyiṉ ēntiyatu
[20] tippiyam eṉṟē cintinōy kūra,
maṇimēkali, maṉamakiḻcci mikavuṭiyaḷāy, "pattiṉip peṇṭir iṭum picciyi mutaṟkaṇ
ēṟṟal peruntakavākum" eṉṟaṉaḷ. avaṭkuk kāyacaṇṭiki eṉṉum viñciyar makaḷorutti, .
[21] "kuḷaṉ aṇitāmarik koḻumalar nāppaṇ,
orutaṉi ōṅkiya [22]tirumalar pōṉṟu,
[23]vāṉ taru kaṟpiṉ maṉiyuṟi makaḷiriṉ
tāṉ [24]taṉi yōṅkiya takimiya ḷaṉṟō
ātiri nallāḷ; avaḷ maṉi, immaṉi;
nī [25]pukal vēṇṭum, nēriḻi!" eṉṟaṉaḷ.
ivvāṟu muṉmoḻinta kāyacaṇṭiki mēlē ātiriyiṉ vara lāṟṟiṉik kūṟaluṟukiṉṟāḷ.
----
[1]. uyir kāvalaṉākiya oruvaṉ vantu tōṉṟum; uyirikkākkum oruvaṉ varuvaṉ.
[2]. kuṭarttoṭarmāli - kuṭarākiya māli.
[3]. poṉ aṭar muṭṭi akavayiṉāṉ - poṉ takaṭṭālāṉa muṭ ṭiyiṉ uḷḷiṭattāṉ.
[4]. taṟkāttaḷitta taki ā - taṉṉip pālūṭṭi vaḷartta aḻakiya ā.
[5]. oṟkā - olkā; taḷarāta.
[6]. ā vayiṟṟu - āviṉ vayiṟṟil. amararkaṇam - tēvar kūṭṭam.
[7]. tīṅkaṉi nāval - tīviya kaṉikaḷiyuṭiya nāval maram. tīvu -nāvalan tīvu.
[8]. puṇṇiya naṉṉīr - puṇṇiyap poruḷākiya nalla nīr. pū - vāṉa var coriyum pū.
[9]. maṇ - maṇṇulakam. vēntaṉ - pūmicantiraṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ.
[10]. puṇṇiya mutalvaṉ - puṇṇiyat talivaṉāṉa muṉivaṉ.
[11]. aricu - aracu.
[12]. niritār vēntaṉ - variciyuṟattoṭutta māliyaṇinta aracaṉ.
[13]. tuṟakka vēntaṉ - intiraṉ. tuyppu - aviyuṇavu.
[14]. aṟakkōl - aṟam tiriyāta ceṅkōl.
[15]. nalattaki - naṟpayaṉ; celvakkālam.
[16]. alattaṟkāli - vaṟumik kālam. ākiyatu - uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
[17]. oḻittuvittāṅku - payaṉpaṭuttātu ōriṭattē vaṟitē vit tatu pōla.
[18]. vaṟaṉ ōṭu ulakil - vaṟumi viriyap parantuḷḷa ulakil, vāṉ
tuyar - mikka tuṉpam. aṟaṉōṭu oḻittal - aṟam ceytaṟkuriya amuta curapiyākiya
ōṭṭiṉip payaṉpaṭuttātu vittiruttal.
[19]. matumalark kuḻalāḷ -tēṉiyuṭiya pūvaṇinta kūntaliyuṭiya maṇi mēkali.
[20]. tippiyam - viyapput taruvatu.
[21]. kuḷaṉ aṇi tāmari - kuḷattiṭattē aḻakuṟa malarnta tāmari. nāppaṇ - naṭuvē.
[22]. tirumalar - aḻaku ciṟanta tāmarippū.
[23]. vāṉ taru kaṟpu - maḻi peyvikkum kaṟpu.
[24]. taṉi ōṅkiya - nikaraṟac ciṟanta. takimiyaḷ - takutiyuṭiyaḷ.
[25]. pukal vēṇṭum - virumpic cellavēṇṭum.

16. ātiri picciyiṭṭa kāti

ātiri yeṉpavaḷ cātuvaṉ eṉṉum vaṇikaṟku maṉivi. avaṉ toṭakkattē, kaṇikimakaḷiriṉ
kāmavalippaṭṭut taṉ maṉiviyik kiviṭṭoḻukiṉaṉ; avaṉpāl kaḷḷuṇṭal, kava ṟāṭal mutaliya
tīcceyalkaḷ uḷavāyiṉa. avaṟṟāl avaṉ celva miḻantu vaṟiyaṉāyiṉaṉ. avaṉāṟ pēṇappaṭṭa
kaṇikiyarum avaṉik "kāṇamili" eṉak kiyutirtta ṉar. piṉṉar, 'avaṉ taṉ niliyiṉi
niṉintu varunti, vaṅkam pōkum vāṇikar tammuṭaṉ tāṉum ceṉṟaṉaṉ. vaḻiyil, avaṉ
ēṟicceṉṟa vaṅkam tīvaḷi yāl kaṭaliṟ kaviḻntuviṭṭatu. uṭikalappaṭṭa cātuvaṉ oṭi maram
oṉṟip paṟṟi, nākar vāḻum tīvoṉṟi yaṭintu uṇarvaṟṟuk kiṭantāṉ.
avaṉip pōla vēṟōrāṟṟāl uyir tappic ceṉṟōr, ātiripāl nikaḻntatu kūṟi, cātuvaṉ
iṟantāṉ eṉṟaṉar.
ātiri nallāḷ āṅkatu tāṉ kēṭṭu
[1] ūrīrē yō! oḷḷaḻal īmam
tārīrō eṉac [2]cāṟṟiṉaḷ kaḻaṟi,
cuṭalik kāṉil toṭukuḻip paṭuttu,
[3] muṭali viṟakiṉ muḷiyeri potti,
"mikka eṉ kaṇavaṉ viṉippayaṉ uyppap
pukkuḻip pukuvēṉ" eṉṟu avaḷ
tīyiṭi vīḻntāḷ. vīḻnta pōtu,
[4] paṭuttuṭaṉ vitta pāyaṟ paḷḷiyum,
uṭutta kūṟiyum oḷḷeri uṟā atu;
[5] āṭiya cāntamum, acinta kūntalil
cūṭiya māliyum [6]tol niṟam vaḻātu;
[7] virimalart tāmari orutaṉi yirunta
[8] tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ pōṉṟu iṉi tiruppa,
avaḷ peritum kalakka muṟṟu,
tīyum kollāt [9]tīviṉi yāṭṭiyēṉ,
yātu ceyvēṉ eṉṟu avaḷ ēṅkiṉaḷ;
ēṅkalum, vāṉattē meyyoli eḻuntu,
ātiri, kēḷ : uṉ arumpeṟaṟ kaṇavaṉi,
[10]ūrtiri koṇṭāṅku. uyppap pōki,
[11] nakka cāraṇar nākar vāḻmalip
pakkam cērntaṉaṉ; palyāṇṭu irāṉ;
cantiratattaṉ eṉumōr vāṇikaṉ
vaṅkam taṉṉuṭaṉ vantaṉaṉ tōṉṟum;
niṉ perun tuṉpam oḻivāy nī eṉa
urittatu. atu kēṭṭu maṉanteḷintu makiḻnta ātiri taṉ maṉiyakamaṭintu,
[12] kaṇmaṇi yaṉiyāṉ kaṭi tīṅ kuṟuka eṉap
[13]puṇṇiyam muṭṭāḷ; poḻimaḻi tarūum
[14]arumpeṟal marapiṉ pattiṉip peṇṭirum
virumpiṉar toḻūum [15] viyappiṉa ḷāyiṉaḷ.
ivvāṟu ātiriyiṉ ceyal vaki kūṟiya kāyacaṇṭiki nākar tīvaṭinta cātuvaṉ ceyalik
kūṟaluṟṟu, nākar tīvil malippakkattē uṇarvaṟṟuk kiṭanta cātuvaṉi, āṅkē vanta nākar
cilar kaṇṭu, tamakku avaṉatu uṭampu nalluṇavāmeṉṟu karuti yeḻuppiṉar.
eḻunta cātuvaṉ avar moḻiyi naṉkaṟintavaṉātaliṉ, ava ruṭaṉ iṉitē collāṭiṉaṉ. avarkaḷ
avaṉit toḻutu, "iṅkē em talimakaṉ uḷaṉ; avaṉpāl nī pōntaruḷ" eṉ ṟaṉar. cātuvaṉ
ataṟku icintu avaruṭaṉ nākar talivaṉpāl ceṉṟaṉaṉ.
kaḷḷaṭu [16]kuḻiciyum kaḻimuṭi nāṟṟamum
veḷḷeṉ [17] puṇaṅkalum viraviya irukkiyil,
[18]eṇkutaṉ piṇavō ṭiruntatu pōlap
peṇṭuṭaṉ irunta
nākar talivaṉic cātuvaṉ kaṇṭāṉ. avaṉ ivaṉatu varavu viṉāvi, nikaḻntatu kēṭṭu,
collāṭṭāl aṉpukoṇṭu, iccātuvaṉ,
[19]aruntutal iṉṟi ali kaṭal [20] uḻantōṉ;
varuntiṉaṉ; aḷiyaṉ. vammiṉ, mākkāḷ!
nampikku iḷiyaḷōr naṅkiyik koṭuttu,
[21]veṅkaḷum ūṉum vēṇṭuva koṭum
eṉṟu urittāṉ. cātuvaṉ :
[22]vevvuri kēṭṭēṉ; vēṇṭēṉ.
nākar talivaṉ mukañ cuḷittu:
peṇṭirum uṇṭiyum iṉṟeṉil mākkaṭku
uṇṭō [23]ñālattu uṟu payaṉ? uṇ ṭeṉil,
kāṇkuvam, yāṅkaḷum; kāṭṭuvā yāka.
cātuvaṉ :
mayakkum kaḷḷum maṉṉuyir [24] kōṟalum
[25] kayakkaṟu mākkaḷ kaṭintaṉar, kēḷāy :
piṟantavar cātalum iṟantavar piṟattalum
uṟaṅkalum viḻittalum pōṉṟatu uṇmiyiṉ,
nallaṟam ceytōr nallula kaṭitalum,
allaṟam ceyvōr arunara kaṭitalum
uṇṭeṉa vuṇartaliṉ, [26]uravōr kaḷintaṉar;
kaṇṭaṉi yāka.
nā. tali:- (nakittu)
uṭampuviṭṭōṭum uyir urukkoṇṭu ōr
iṭampukum eṉṟē emakku īṅku urittāy;
avvuyir evvaṇam pōyppukum? avvaki.
[27]cevvaṉam uri.
cātuvaṉ:- itu kēḷ,
[28]uṟṟati yuṇarum uṭal uyir vāḻvuḻi;
[29] maṟṟiya vuṭampē, maṉṉuyir nīṅkiṭiṉ,
[30] taṭinteri yūṭṭiṉum tāṉuṇa rāteṉiṉ,
uṭampiṭip pōṉatoṉ ṟuṇṭeṉa uṇar, nī.
[31] pōṉār tamakkōr pukkil uṇ ṭeṉpatu
yāṉō allēṉ, yāvarum uṇarkuvar;
uṭampīṇ ṭoḻiya uyirpala kāvatam
kaṭantu [32]cēṇcēṟal kaṉaviṉum kāṇkuvi;
āṅkaṉam pōki avvuyir ceyviṉi
[33] pūṇṭa yākkiyil pukuvatu teḷi, nī.
nā. talivaṉ:-- (vaṇaṅki)
kaḷḷum ūṉum kiviṭiṉ ivvuṭampu
uḷḷuṟi vāḻuyir [34]ōmputa lāṟṟēṉ;
[35] tamakkoḻi marapiṉ cāvuṟu kāṟum
emakkām nalla ṟam eṭutturi.
cātuvaṉ:
naṉṟu coṉṉāy naṉṉeṟi [36]paṭarkuvi
uṉ taṉakku ollum neṟiyaṟam urikkēṉ;
uṭikala mākkaḷ uyiruyn tīṅkuṟiṉ
[37] aṭutoḻil oḻintu avar āruyi rōmpi
[38] mūttu viḷimā oḻittu evvuyir māṭṭum
[39]tīttiṟam oḻika.
ivvaṟaṅkaḷik kēṭṭa nākar talivaṉ perumakiḻvu koṇṭu, vēṇṭum poṉṉum poruḷum mika
nalkiṉaṉ. avaṟṟip peṟṟa cātuvaṉ cantiratattaṉ eṉṉum vaṇikaṉatu vaṅkamēṟi innakari yaṭintu
taṉ maṉivi ātiriyuṭaṉ nallaṟam ceytu varuvāṉā yiṉaṉ; ivvā tiripāl picci
peṟuvāyāka eṉak kāyacaṇṭiki colli muṭittāḷ. avvaṇṇamē maṇimēkali ātiri illiṉ muṉ
“ puṉiyā ōviyam ” pōla niṉṟaṉaḷ. ātiri nallāḷum ava ḷatu piccippāttiram niṟiyac
cōṟu peytāḷ.
----
[1]. ūrīrēyō - ūril uḷḷa cāṉṟōrkaḷē. īmam - viṟakaṭukku.
[2]. cāṟṟiṉaḷ - colliṉaḷ.
[3]. muṭali viṟaku - muṟukkuṇṭu kiṭakkum viṟaku. eri potti - eri mūṭṭi.
muḷiyeri - mikka neruppu.
[4]. pāyaṟpaḷḷi - paṭukkiyiṭam, uṭaṉ paṭuttu vitta eṉa iyikka. kūṟi - puṭivi.
[5]. āṭiya - pūcikkoṇṭa.
[6]. tol niṟam - paḻiya niṟamum oḷiyum. vaḻā atu - keṭāmal.
[7]. viri - maṇam kamaḻum. [8]. tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ - tirumakaḷākiya ceyyavaḷ.
[9]. tīviṉiyāṭṭi - tīviṉi ceytavaḷ.
[10]. ūr tiri - oṉ ṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāka vūrntu cellum alikaḷ. uyppa - celutta.
[11]. nakka cāraṇar - uṭiyillāmal tiripavar . itaṉāl nākar “nakka cāraṇar nākar'
eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
[12]. kaṇ maṇi yaṉi yāṉ - kaṇṇiluḷḷa maṇiyoppavaṉāṉa kaṇavaṉ .
[13]. puṇṇiyam muṭṭāḷ - puṇṇiyam ceytalil kuṟiyāḷāy.
[14]. arumpeṟal marapu - peṟutaṟkariya muṟimi (kaṟpu).
[15]. viyappiṉaḷ - takutiyuṭiyaḷ. peṇṭirum: ummi, uyarvuc ciṟappu.
[16]. kuḻici - pāṉi. kaḻimuṭināṟṟam - mikka pulāl nāṟṟam.
[17]. uṇaṅkal - vaṟṟal. [18]. eṇku - karaṭi. piṇavu - peṇkaraṭi.
[19]. aruntutal - uṇavu. [20]. uḻantōṉ - tuṉpuṟṟāṉ.
[21]. veṅkaḷ - puḷippumikka kaḷ; virumpattakum kaḷḷumām.
[22]. vevvuri - koṭiya col.
[23]. ñālattu uṟupayaṉ - ulakattu uyir vāḻtalālākum payaṉ.
[24]. kōṟal - kollutal.
[25]. kayakkaṟumākkaḷ - kayaṅkutal illāta aṟivuṭiyōr.kaṭintaṉar vilakkiṉar.
[26]. uravōr - aṟivuṭiya periyōr. [27]. cevvaṉam - teḷivāka.
[28]. uyir vāḻvuḻi uṭal uṟṟati yuṇarum eṉka. uṟṟatu - uṭaliṟ paṭṭa tu.
[29]. uṭampu taṭintu, eriyūṭṭiṉum uṇarātu eṉka.
[30]. taṭintu - kuṟittu (veṭṭi).
[31]. pōṉār - pōṉa uyir. pukkil - pukumiṭam.
[32]. cēṇ cēṟal - neṭuntolivu pōtal.
[33]. pūṇṭa yākki - uyir niṟṟaṟkuk koṇṭa uṭampu.
[34]. ōmputal tēṟṟēṉ - kāttalic ceyyēṉ; iḻantu viṭuvēṉ eṉpatām.
[35]. tamakku oḻimarpiṉ nallaṟam eṉa iyikka. tamakkeṉṟē vari yaṟutta muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya
nalla ṟam.
[36]; paṭarkuvi - celvāy. [37]. aṭutoḻil - kollutal.
[38]. mūttuviḷimā - mutumiyuṟṟu iṟakkum uyirkaḷ.
[39]. tīttiṟam – kolittoḻil.
---------------

17. ulaka aṟavi pukka kāti

ātiriyiṭṭa picciyuṭaṉ ceṉṟa maṇimēkali pacinōy varutta varuntum makkaḷ palarkkum uṇavu
iṭṭu varalāṉāḷ. ata ṉāl, appāttirattup picci kuṟiyātu niṟintu niṉṟatu kaṇṭu,
kāya caṇṭiki,
[1] neṭiyōṉ mayaṅki nilamicit tōṉṟi
[2] aṭalaru munnīr aṭitta nāṉṟu,
kuraṅku koṇarn teṟinta [3] neṭumali yellām
[4] aṇaṅkuṭi yaḷakkar vayiṟu pukkāṅku
iṭṭatu āṟṟāk kaṭṭaḻaṟ kaṭumpacip
paṭṭēṉ, eṉṟaṉ paḻaviṉip payattāl;
aṉṉi, kēḷ nī. [5] āruyir maruttuvi! [6]
tuṉṉiya eṉ nōy tuṭippāy eṉṟaṉaḷ.
maṇimēkali aṅṅaṉamē cōṟiṭalum, kāyacaṇṭiki koṇṭirunta yāṉit tī eṉṉum pacinōy
nīṅkiṟṟu. avaḷ neñcu kuḷirntu, taṉakku an nōyuṇṭāṉa kāraṇattik kūṟaluṟṟāḷ.
veḷḷimaliyiṉ pakutiyākiya cēṭi maliyil uḷḷa kāñcaṉa purattu vittiyātara makaḷākiya
yāṉ, eṉ kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ potiyil maliyik kāṇṭaṟku vantukoṇ ṭiruntēṉ. vaḻiyil ōr
yāṟṟil eṉṉi yirutti eṉ kaṇavaṉ kaṉi koṇarac ceṉṟāṉ. aṅkē
*muḷ, ¨
viruccikaṉ eṉṉum mātavaṉ paṉṉīrāṇṭiṟ koru muṟi paḻuk kum nāvaṟkaṉi yoṉṟik
koṇṭuvantu tēkkiliyil vittu nīrāṭac ceṉṟāṉ. ataṉ uṇminili yuṇarātu cerukkic
ceṉṟa yāṉ akkaṉiyik kālāl eṟṟic citittēṉ. tirumpip pōnta viruccikaṉ eṉ
ceyalik kaṇṭu ciṉantu,
cīrtikaḻ nāvalil [7]patippiyamāṉatu,
īrāṟāṇṭil orukaṉi taruvatu
akkaṉi yuṇṭōr āṟīrāṇṭu
makkaḷ yākkiyiṉ varumpaci nīṅkuvar;
paṉṉīrāṇṭil orunāḷallatu
uṇṇā nōṉpiṉēṉ uṇkaṉi [8]citittāy;
[9] antarañ cellum mantiram iḻantu
[10] tantit tīyāl taṉittuya ruḻantu
munnālāṇṭil mutirkaṉi nāṉ īṅku
uṇṇum nāḷ uṉ uṟu paci kaḷika
eṉac cāpa miṭṭakaṉṟāṉ. piṉpu vanta eṉ kaṇavaṉ eṉ aṟiyā mik kiraṅki, varunti,
muṭivil,
[11]camput tīviṉuḷ tamiḻaka maruṅkil
[12] kampamillāk kaḻiperuñ celvar,
āṟṟāmākkaṭku āṟṟun tuṇiyāki,
nōṟṟōr uṟivatōr [13] nōṉakar uṇṭāl;
palanāḷāyiṉum [14] nilaṉoṭu pōki,
appatip pukuka,
eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭup pirintaṉaṉ. yāṉum innakari yaṭintu iṅku vativēṉāyiṉēṉ.
intiraviḻāk kālattē eṉ kaṇavaṉ pōntu eṉṉik kaṇṭu ekuvatu vaḻakkam. iṉṟu nī pōntu eṉ
pacit tuyar kaḷintu paṇṭiyiyalpu peṟuvittāy. iṉi yāṉ eṉ nakark kuc celvēṉ. nī
innakaril uḷḷa cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattu ulakavaṟavikkuc ceṉṟu, aṅkē,
[15] ūrūrāṅkaṇ uṟupaci yuḻantōr,
[16] ārum iṉmiyiṉ arumpiṇi yuṟṟōr,
[17] iṭuvōrt tērntāṅku iruppōr palarāl;
[18]. vaṭuvāḻ kūntal! ataṉ pāṟa pōkuka
eṉṟu colliviṭṭu akkāyacaṇṭiki pōyviṭṭaṉaḷ.
avaḷ colliyavaṇṇamē, maṇimēkali avvulaka aṟavikkuc ceṉṟu, campāpati kōyili yaṭintu
mummuṟi valam vantu vaṇaṅkip piṉ kantiṟpāviyik kitoḻutu, pacitta mākkaṭkup
pēruṇavu vaḻaṅki avar paci nōy tīrkkum perun toṇṭu purintu varalāṉāḷ.
----
[1]. neṭiyōṉ - tirumāl. mayaṅki - amparīṭaṉukku muṉivar iṭṭa cāpatti yēṟṟataṉāl
mayaṅki.
[2]. aṭalaru munnīr - taṭuttaṟkariya kaṭal.
[3]. neṭumali - neṭum pāṟikaḷ.
[4]. aṇaṅkuṭi yaḷakkar - muḻaṅku taliyuṭiya kaṭal. muḻaṅkukaṭali, varuttattāl
pulampuvatākak karutik kūṟutaliṉ, " aṇaṅkuṭi yaḷakkar " eṉṟāḷ. paṭṭēṉ - varuntiṉēṉ.
[5]. āruyir marut tuvi - āruyirkaṭku maruntākiya uṇavu aḷippavaḷ. uyirkaḷ eytum
nōyvaki yevaṟṟiṟkum periya nōy paciyātaliṉ, atu nīkkum uṇavi, " āruyir maruntu "
eṉṟum, ataṉit tarupava ḷātaliṉ, " āruyir maruttuvi " yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
[6]. tuṉṉiya - neruṅki nīṅkātu niṉṟa.
[7]. tippiyamāṉatu - teyvat taṉmiyuṭiyatu.
[8]. citittāy - kālāl iṭaṟik keṭuttāy.
[9]. antaram cellum - vāṉattūṭu cellum.
[10]. tantit tī - yāṉit tī yeṉṉum nōy.
[11]. camput tīvu - nāvalantīvu.
[12] . kampam - naṭukkam; īṇṭuk kēṭṭiṉ mēṟṟu. kaḻiperuñ cel var - mikka periya
celvamuṭiyōr.
[13]. nōṉ nakar - aṭuttarrit tāṅkum vaṉmi paṭitta nakaram. (kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam)
[14]. nilaṉoṭu pōki - nilattē naṭantu ceṉṟu.
[15]. ūrūrāṅkaṇ - ūrtōṟum.
[16]. ārum - pātukāppōr oruvarum.
[17].iṭuvōr - uṇavu iṭuvōr. tērntu - nōkki.
[18]. vaṭuvāḻ kūntal - vakirnta kūntaliyuṭiya maṇimēkaliyē.
-------------

18. utayakumaraṉ ampalam pukka kāti

maṇimēkali ivvāṟu picciyēṟṟaḷikkum pēraṟat tuṟavi yāy oḻukukiṉṟa ceyti,
cittirāpati eṉṉum mātaviyiṉ tāykku eṭṭiyatu. perumpuṇṇil ericerukiyatu pōl,
aḵtu avaḷ neñcil peruntuyaric ceytatu. ataṉāl avaḷ, ēṉi nāṭaka makaḷiri nōkki,
kōvalaṉ iṟantapiṉ [1] koṭuntuyar eyti
mātavi [2] mātavar paḷḷiyuḷ aṭintatu
[3] nakutak kaṉṟē, nalneṭum pērūr;
itu [4]tak keṉpōrkku eḷḷuri yāyatu;
[5] kātalaṉ vīyak kaṭuntuya reytip
[6] pōtal ceyyā uyiroṭu pulantu,
[7] naḷiyirum poyki yāṭunar pōla
[8] muḷiyerippukūum mutukuṭip piṟanta
pattiṉip peṇṭi rallēm; palartam
[9] kittūṇ vāḻkki kaṭaviyam aṉṟē;
pāṇmakaṉ paṭṭuḻip paṭūum [10]pāṉmiyiṉ,
yāḻiṉam pōlum iyalpiṉam; aṉṟiyum,
[11] naṟuntā tuṇṭu nayaṉil kāli
[12] vaṟumpūt tuṟakkum vaṇṭu pōlkuvam;
[13] viṉi yoḻi kālit tiruviṉ celvi
aṉiyē māki āṭavart tuṟappēm,
tāpatak kōlam tāṅkiṉam eṉpatu
yāvarum nakū um iyalpiṉatu aṉṟē !
mātavi īṉṟa maṇimē kalivalli,
[14] pōtaviḻ cevvi poruntutal virumpiya
utiya kumaraṉām ulakāḷ vaṇṭiṉ
[15] citiyā vuḷḷam cevvitiṉ arunta,
kikkoṇ ṭāṅkavaḷ ēntiya [16]kaṭiñiyip
[17] picci mākkaḷ piṟarkik kāṭṭi
maṟṟu avaṉ taṉṉāl maṇimē kali taṉip
poṟṟērk koṇṭu pōtē ṉākil,
[18] cuṭumaṇ ēṟṟi araṅku cūḻ pōki
[19] vaṭuvoṭu vāḻum maṭantiyar tammō
raṉiyē ṉāki araṅkak kūttiyar
[20] maṉiyakam pukā a marapiṉaṉ
eṉṟu vañciṉaṅ kūṟi, iḷaṅkōvākiya utayakumaraṉ irunta pō katti yaṭintāḷ. āṅkē,
[21] arava vaṇṭoṭu tēṉiṉa mārkkum.
[22] tarumaṇal ñemiriya tirunāṟu oruciṟip
pavaḻat tūṇattup pacumpoṟ ceñcuvart
tikaḻoḷi nittilac cittira vitāṉattu,
viḷaṅkoḷi paranta paḷiṅku cey maṇṭapattu,
[23] tuḷaṅkumā ṉūrtit tūmalarp paḷḷi
[24] veṇṭiri virinta veṇṇiṟac cāmari
koṇṭu iru maruṅkum [25]kōtiyar vīca,
utayakumaraṉ vīṟṟiruntāṉ. avaṉpāl, cittirāpati, maṇimēkali yiṉ iḷamic cevviyum,
āṭalpāṭalkaḷil niṟinta pulamiyum aḻakuṟak kūṟi, avaḷ ulakavaṟaviyil iruntu aṟam
ceyvataṉiyum eṭuttu moḻintāḷ. kaṭalil kalam kaviḻappeṟṟa oruvaṉukkup periya puṇi
kiṭittāṟpōṉṟa ic ceytiyik kēḷviyuṟṟa avaṉ, muṉpu am maṇimēkaliyik kaṇṭatumutal,
maṇimēkalā teyvam tōṉṟi, aracumuṟi kūṟi akaṟṟiyatu īṟāka naṭantavi yellām colli,
taṉ miya laṟivip pulappaṭuttiṉāṉ.
cittirāpati avaṉ kūṟiyatu kēṭṭuc ciṟunaki ceytu, “aracē, ivi yāvum kāmak
kaḷḷāṭṭil nikaḻum iṭiyīṭukaḷ. ivipōl vaṉa palavum tēvarkkum nikaḻntuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟi,
intiraṉ āyiraṅkaṇ peṟṟatum, tīkkaṭavuḷiṉ maṉiyāḷ, muṉimakaḷir eḻu varuḷ
aruntatiyoḻiya ēṉiyōr pōlat tōṉṟi, ak kaṭavuḷiṉ kāmattuyar tuṭittatum avaṉ aṟiya
urittāḷ. mēlum avaḷē kūṟaluṟṟu,
[26] vāṭṭiṟaṟ kurucil !
kaṉṉik kāvalum, [27] kaṭiyiṟ kāvalum,
taṉṉuṟu kaṇavaṉ cāvuṟiṟ kāvalum
niṟiyiṟ kāttup piṟarpiṟark kāṇātu,
koṇṭō ṉallatu teyvamum pēṇāp
peṇṭirtam kuṭiyil piṟantā ḷallaḷ;
nāṭavar kāṇa nallaraṅ kēṟi,
āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakum kāṭṭic
[28] curuppunāṇ karuppuvil aruppukkaṇi tūvac
[29] cerukkayal neṭuṅkaṇ curukkuvalip paṭuttuk
kaṇṭōr neñcam koṇṭakam pukkup
[30] paṇtēr moḻiyiṟ payaṉ pala vāṅki
vaṇṭiṉ tuṟakkum [31]koṇṭi makaḷirip
[32] pāṉmiyiṟ piṇittup paṭiṟṟuri yaṭakkutal
kōṉ muṟi yaṉṟō kumaraṟku,
eṉṟu av vatayakumaraṉ uḷḷam piṟaḻntu am maṇimēkaliyi viṭātu toṭarumāṟu tūṇṭiṉāḷ.
utayakumāraṉ, uṭaṉē, viripari pūṭṭiya neṭuntēr ēṟi, maṇi mēkali yirunta iṭatti
yaṭintāṉ. āṅkē, paciyāl vāṭu vōrkkup peruñcōṟu vaḻaṅkikkoṇṭirunta maṇimēkaliyik
kaṇṭu, iṭaṅkaḻi kāmattaṉāy, avaḷi aṇmi,
uṭampōṭu eṉ ṟaṉ uḷḷakam pukuntu eṉ
[33] neñcam kavarnta vañcak kaḷvi,
[34] nōṟṟūṇ vāḻkkiyiṉ nocitavam tāṅki
[35] ēṟṟūṇ virumpiya kāraṇam eṉ?
eṉa viṉavum eṇṇamuṭiyaṉāy, vāyviṭṭu,
nallāy, eṉ kol naṟṟavam purintatu? –
collāy eṉṟu tuṇintuṭaṉ kēṭpa,
maṇimēkali, "ivaṉ paṇṭippiṟappil eṉakkuk kaṇavaṉāṉa irākulaṉ; ivaṉi yāṉ vaṇaṅkalum, ivaṉ
eṉ kippaṟṟalum kuṟṟamākā" eṉa eṇṇiṉaḷ. eṉiṉum, maṉam naṭuṅki,
kēṭṭatu moḻivēṉ: [36]kēḷvi yāḷariṉ
[37] tōṭṭa ceviyi nī yākuvi yām eṉil,
eṉṟu muṉmoḻintu, piṉpu,
piṟattalum mūttalum piṇippaṭ ṭiraṅkalum
iṟattalum uṭiyatu; [38] iṭumpik koḷkalam;
makkaḷ yākki itu, eṉa uṇarntu
mikka nallaṟam virumputal purintēṉ;
[39] maṇṭamar murukkuṅ kaḷiṟaṉi yārkkup
peṇṭir kūṟum pēraṟi vuṇṭō ?
kēṭṭaṉi yāyiṉ, [40] vēṭṭatu ceyka
eṉṟu colliviṭṭu, am maṇimēkali arukirunta campāpati kōyiṟkuḷ nuḻintu, at
teyvattit toḻutu, maṇimēkalā teyvam kaṟpitta vēṟṟurukkoḷḷum mantiratti ōti, kāya
caṇṭikiyiṉ vaṭivukoṇṭu, piccippāttirattuṭaṉ veḷiyē vantāḷ. avaḷik kaṇṭa utayakumaraṉ
uṇmi yuṇarāṉāy, “ik kāyacaṇṭikipāl ip pāttirattit tantu maṇimēkali maṟin taṉaḷ "
eṉṟu niṉintu, tāṉum, ac campāpati muṉ ceṉṟu niṉṟu,
piccip pāttiram, [41]perumpaci yuḻanta
kāyacaṇ ṭikitaṉ kiyiṟ kāṭṭi
[42] māyiyiṉ oḷitta maṇimē kali taṉi
īṅ [43] kim maṇṇīṭ ṭiyāreṉa uṇarkēṉ;
āṅkavaḷ ivaḷ eṉṟu aruḷā yāyiṭiṉ,
paṉṉāḷāyiṉum [44]pāṭu kiṭappēṉ;
iṉṉum kēḷāy; [45] imiyōr pāvāy!
[46] pavaḷac cevvāyt tavaḷavāḷ nakiyum,
añcaṉam cērāc ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇum,
[47] murintu kaṭineriya varintacilip puruvamum,
[48] kuvimuṭ karuviyum, kōṇamum, kūr nuṉik
kavimuṭ karuviyu mākik kaṭikoḷak
[49] kalvip pākariṉ kāppuvali ōṭṭi
[50] valvāy yāḻiṉ melli tiṉ viḷaṅka,
[51] mutukkuṟi mutumoḻi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi,
[52] putukkōḷ yāṉi vēṭṭam vāytteṉa
mutiyā ḷuṉ ṟaṉ kōṭṭam pukunta
mativāḷ mukattu maṇimēkali taṉi
yoḻiyap pōkēṉ; [53] uṉ aṭi toṭṭēṉ;
itu kuṟi,
eṉak kuṟiyirantu niṟpāṉāyiṉaṉ.
---
[1]. koṭuntuyar - mikka tuṉpam.
[2]. mātavar paḷḷi - aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ uṟiyumiṭam.
[3]. nakutakkaṉṟu - piṟar eḷḷi nakittaṟkuriya toṉṟām.
[4]. takku - takkatu. eḷḷuri - ikaḻcciyuri.
[5]. kātalaṉ vīya - kōvalaṉ iṟakka.
[6]. pōtal ceyyā - nīṅkāta.
[7]. naḷiyirum poyki - mikka nīr niṟinta poyki.
[8]. muḷiyeri - mikka tī. mutukuṭi - paḻaṅkuṭi.
[9]. kit tūṇ vāḻkki kaṭaviyam - kiyiṭattatākiya uṇavip peṟṟuṇṭu vāḻum
takutiyuṭiyōm.
[10]. pāṉmiyiṉ - muṟimiyuṭiya.
[11]. naṟuntātu - naṟiya tēṉ. nayaṉ - tēṉākiya paci.
[12]. vaṟumpū - tēṉ illāta vaṟiya pū.
[13]. viṉi yoḻikāli - nalviṉippayaṉ tirum kālattē tāṉum nīṅki viṭum.
[14]. pōtu aviḻ cevvi - maṇattukkuriya camayam. maṇimēkaliyi valliyeṉ ṟataṉāl, avaḷ
maṇattukkuriya cevvi eytiya niliyi, .. pōtaviḻ cevvi" yeṉ ṟāḷ. utayakumaraṉām
vaṇṭu eṉka.
[15]. citiyā uḷḷam - vēṟoṉṟāl aḻiyāta uḷḷam.
[16]. kaṭiñi - piccikkalam. [17]. piccimākkaḷ - picciyeṭuttuṇpavar.
[18]. cuṭumaṇ - ceṅkal. araṅku - nāṭakavaraṅku.
[19]. vaṭu - kuṟṟam. kuṭik kuṟṟappaṭṭa makaḷir talimēl ceṅkal ēḻu ēṟṟi araṅkucūḻa
varuvittal paṇṭimarapu.
[20]. maṉiyakam pukā marapiṉaṉ - maṉiyakat tuṭpukēṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
[21]. arava vaṇṭu - ici yolikkum vaṇṭu. tēṉ, vaṇṭu, curumpu. miṇīṟu eṉpaṉa vaṇṭiṉ
vaki.
[22]. tarumaṇal ñemiriya - putu maṇal parappiya. tirunāṟu oru ciṟi - aḻaku tikaḻum
oru pakkattē .
vitāṉam - mēṟkaṭṭi. poṉṉāl cuvarum, pavaḻattāl tūṇum, muttāl vitāṉamum viḷaṅka,
paḷiṅkiṉāl ceyta maṇṭapam eṉka.
[23]. tuḷaṅkum māṉ - oḷiyāl acitaliyuṭiya ciṅkam. ūrti - īṇṭu irukki yeṉṉum
poruḷatu.
[24]. veṇṭiri virinta - veḷḷiya ali pōla virinta.
[25]. kōtiyar - makaḷir.
[26]. vāḷ tiṟal kurucil - vāṭpōril cīriya tiṟalpaṭitta araca kumaraṉē.
[27]. kaṭiyiṟ kāval - tirumaṇattāl piṟakkum kāval.
nallaraṅku - ēḻukōl akalamum, eṇkōl nīḷamum, orukōl kuṟaṭṭuyaramum vāytali
raṇṭumuṭiya nāṭakamēṭi.
[28]. curumpākiya nāṇ pūṭṭi, karumpākiya villi vaḷittu, arumpākiya ampu
toṭuttu eyya.
[29]. cerukkayal neṭuṅkaṇ - cerucceyyum kayal mīṉ pōlum uṇṭa kaṇṇāl. curukku vali
paṭuttu neñcam akappaṭuttukkoṇṭu - curukku vali vīci neñciṉi ataṉkaṇ
akappaṭuttikkoṇṭu.
[30]. paṇ tērmoḻiyiṉ - paṇ ṇicipōlum iṉiya coṟkaḷāl. payaṉ - poruḷ. vaṇṭiṉ -
vaṇṭupōla.
[31]. koṇṭi makaḷir - poruḷik kavarntu koḷḷutaliyuṭiya makaḷir.
[32], pāṉmiyiṉ - koḷḷutaṟkuriya muṟippaṭi. paṭiṟṟuri - poyyuri.
[33]. utayakumaraṉ neñcil niṉikkuntōṟum avaṉ maṉakkaṇṇil maṇimēkali taṉ muḻumēṉiyum
tōṉṟa niṟṟaliṉ, uṭampōṭu...kaḷvi" eṉkiṉṟāṉ. taṉ neñciṉi avaḷ kavarntēkiyataṉit tāṉ
aṟiyāmi yiṉ, "vañcak kaḷvi" eṉṟāṉ,
[34]. nōṟṟūṇ vāḻkki - viratam kāttup picci yēṟṟuṇṭu vāḻum vāḻkki .
nocital - nuṇukal. tavamuṭiyār toḻil tavat tiṉmēl niṉṟatu.
[35]. ēṟṟūṇ - irantuṇṭal.
[36]. 'kēḷviyāḷariṉ - nuṇṇiya nūṟporuḷik kēṭṭa cāṉṟōrka ḷāl.
[37]. tōṭṭa cevi - tuḷikkappaṭṭa cevi. pala nuṇporuḷik kēṭṭup payiṉṟōr ceviyik
' kēḷviyāl tōṭkappaṭṭa cevi,, yeṉ ṟal marapu. " kēḷviyāl tōṭkappaṭāta cevi "
eṉpatu tiruk . kuṟaḷ.
[38]. iṭumpik koḷkalam- tuṉpamellām koṇṭuvikkum kalam.
[39]. maṇṭu amar murukkum kaḷiṟu - neruṅkiya pōril muntuṟṟuc ceṉṟu porum
kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi.
[40]. vēṭṭatu - virumpiyatu.
[41]. perum paci - yāṉit tī eṉṉum kōyā luṇṭākiya paciyāl.
[42]. māyiyiṉ - māyamāka.
[43. im maṇṇiṭṭu yār - im maṇṇāṟ ceyta pāvikaḷuḷ yār.
[44]. pāṭukiṭappēṉ - varaṅkiṭappēṉ.
[45]. imiyōr pāvi - tēvarkaḷuṭiya talivi,
[46]. pavaḷac cevvāy - pavaḷampōl civanta vāy. tavaḷa vāḷ naki - veḷḷiya oḷiporuntiya
paṟkaḷ,
[47]. murintu - vaḷintu.
[48]. kuvimuḷ, kōṇam (tōṭṭi), kavimuḷ mūṉṟum yāṉiyi aṭakkum karuvi, kaṭi
koḷa - kāval koḷḷa.
vāḷ naki, kuvimuḷḷum; kaṇ kōṇamum; puruvam, kavi, muḷḷu mākak koḷka.
[49]. kalvippākar - kalviyākiya pākar. kāppu vali ōṭṭi - kātta lākiya valiyic
citittu.
[50]. " valvāy yāḻiṉ " eṉṟāṉ, valiya yāṉi yāḻicikku mayaṅki vaṇaṅkutal pōlat
taṉṉit taṉ yāḻicipōlum moḻiyāl vaṇak kiṉami tōṉṟa.
[51]. mutukkuṟi mutumoḻi - pēraṟivu cāṉṟa aṟivu moḻi.
[52]. putukkōḷ yāṉi - putitākakkoṇṭa yāṉi.
[53]. uṉ aṭi toṭṭēṉ - uṉ tiruvaṭi toṭṭuc cūḷ ceytēṉ.
---------

19. ciṟikkōṭṭam aṟakkōṭṭam ākkiya kāti

"maṇimēkali yoḻiyap pōkēṉ; uṉ aṭi toṭṭēṉ " eṉṟu campāpati muṉ vañciṉam kūṟiya
utayakumaraṉ kēṭka, aṅ kuḷḷa cittiraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟil niṉṟu,
[1]ēṭaviḻ tārōy ! emkō makaḷ muṉ
nāṭātu tuṇintu [2] nānal kūrntaṉi
eṉṟoru teyvam kūṟiṟṟu. ataṉik kēṭṭa utayakumāṉ maṉam kalaṅki, meyvarunti,
aṅkavaḷ taṉ tiṟam [3] ayarppāy eṉṟē
ceṅkōl kāṭṭiya teyvamum tippiyam;
[4] piyara valkul palarpaci kaḷiyak
kiyil ēntiya pāttiram tippiyam;
[5] mutti mutalvi aṭipiḻit tāy eṉac
cittiram uritta itū um tippiyam;
innili yellām [6] iḷaṅkoṭi ceytiyiṉ
piṉṉaṟivām,
eṉṟu eṇṇikkoṇṭē avviṭam viṭṭup peyarntu nīṅkiṉaṉ. mālippōtum vantatu.
iṉi, vēṟṟurukkoṇṭu niṉṟa maṇimēkali, palapaṭaniṉintu,
mātavi makaḷāy maṉṟam tiritariṉ,
kāvalaṉ makaṉō [7] kiviṭa līyāṉ;
[8] kāypaci yāṭṭi kāyacaṇ ṭikiyeṉa
ūrmuḻu taṟiyum uruvam koṇṭē,
[9] āṟṟā mākkaṭku āṟṟum tuṇiyāki
ēṟṟalum iṭutalum irappōr kaṭaṉ; avar
mēṟceṉ ṟaḷittal [10] viḻuttakit teṉṟē
nūṟporu ḷuṇarntōr [11] nuṉittaṉar,
eṉṟu karuti, vēntaṉatu āṇi piḻittōri oṟukkum ciṟik kōṭṭam pukuntu, āṅkē
paciyāl varunti melintu kiṭantōrk kup pēruṇavu tantu pacippiṇiyip pōkkiṉaḷ. avaḷ
pāttiram oṉṟē koṇṭu mikap palarkkup pacitīra uṇavaḷittu niṟkum ceya lic ciṟik
kōṭṭak kāvalar kaṇṭu peruviyappuk koṇṭu, ic ceytiyi aracaṟku uṇarttuvatu takkatu
eṉṟu eṇṇi avaṉ pāṟ ceṉṟaṉar.
akkāliyil, aracar perumāṉ taṉ maṉivi cīrttiyeṉpāḷuṭaṉ, viḷiyāṭṭukkaruti yoru
pūmpoḻiṟkuc ceṉṟiruntāṉ. aṅkē,
kompart tumpi [12]ku ḻalici kāṭṭap
[13] poṅkar vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya,
varikkuyil pāṭa mā mayil āṭum,
aḻakiya pūmpantar oṉṟu aracap perumakkaṭku iṉiya kāṭci payantatu. aracaṉ,
[14] puṇartuṇi nīṅkiya poyki yaṉṉamoṭu
maṭamayiṟ pēṭiyum tōkiyum kūṭi
[15] iruciṟi virittāṅku eḻuntuṭaṉ koṭpaṉa
oruciṟik kaṇṭu āṅku uḷ makiḻ veyti,
māmaṇi vaṇṇaṉum [16] tammuṉum piññiyum
āṭiya kuravi iḵtāmeṉa nōkkiyum,
[17] kōṅkalar cērnta māṅkaṉi taṉṉi,
[18] pāṅkuṟa irunta palpoṟi maññiyic
cempoṉ taṭṭil tīmpāl ēntip
piṅkiḷi yūṭṭum [19] ōr pāviyām eṉṟum,
[20] aṇimalarp pūmpoḻil akavayiṉ irunta
[21] piṇavukkuraṅ kēṟṟip perumatar maḻikkaṇ,
[22] maṭavōrk kiyaṟṟiya māmaṇi yūcal
[23] kaṭuva ṉūkkuvatu kaṇṭunaki yeytiyum
iṉpuṟṟuc ciṟantāṉ. kūttar, avinayappulavar, nāṭaka nūl vallavar, icippulavar, taṇṇumip
pulavar, kuḻal icippōr, pāṭṭici cuvippōr, muttukkōppavar, cāntam camippōr,
pūttoṭup pōr mutaliya palarum aracaṉic cūḻaviruntu iṉpuṟuttiṉar. aracaṉ,
avviṉpattāl ārāmi yeyti, mēlum ceṉṟu,
kuruntum [24] taḷavum tiruntu malarc ceruntiyum,
[25] murukuviri mulliyum [26]karuviḷam poṅkarum,
poruntupu niṉṟu tiruntu naki ceytu
[27]kuṟuṅkāl nakulamum neṭuñcevi muyalum
[28]piṟaḻntu pāy māṉum [29] iṟumpu akalā veṟiyum
aracikkuk kāṭṭi makiḻcci mikkāṉ. piṉpu,
entirak kiṇaṟum, [30] iṭumkaṟ kuṉṟamum,
vantu vīḻ aruviyum, malarppūm pantarum,
[31] parappu nīrp poykiyum, [32]karappu nīrk kēṇiyum,
[33] oḷittuṟi yiṭaṅkaḷum paḷikkaṟip paḷḷiyum
piṟavumākiya iṭaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭṭayarntu, kaṭiciyil,
[34] makata viṉiñarum marāṭṭak kammarum,
avantik kollarum yavaṉat taccarum
[35]taṇ tamiḻ viṉiñar tammoṭu kūṭik
koṇṭu iṉitu iyaṟṟiya kaṇkavar ceyviṉip
pavaḷat tiraḷ kāl paṉmaṇip [36]pōtikit
[37] tavaḷa nittilat tāmam tāḻnta
[38] kōṇac canti māṇviṉi vitāṉattut
[39] tamaṉiyam vēynta vakipeṟu vaṉappiṉ
cantaṉa meḻukiya pacumpoṉ maṇṭapatti yaṭintu iṉitirun tāṉ. akkālattē ciṟikkōṭṭak
kāvalar vantu, cēymikkaṇ niṉṟu vaṇaṅkip piṉ tirumuṉpu aṇuki, “ etirttu mēlvanta
cērariyum pāṇṭiyariyum,
[40] ārpuṉi teriyal iḷaṅkōṉ taṉṉāl
[41] kāriyāṟṟuk koṇṭa kāval veṇkuṭi
[42] vali keḻu taṭakki māvaṇ kiḷḷi !
[43]oḷiyoṭu vāḻi ūḻitō ṟūḻi
vāḻi yeṅkō! maṉṉavar peruntaki!
kēḷ, itu maṉṉō; keṭukaniṉ pakiñar;
mānakart tiriyum ōr [44] vampa mātar
aruñciṟik kōṭṭat [45] takavayiṟ pukuntu
[46] perumpeyar maṉṉa! niṉpeyar vāḻtti
[47] aiyap pāttiram oṉṟu koṇṭu, āṅku, [48]
moykoḷ mākkaḷ mocikka ūṇ curantaṉaḷ;
ūḻitō ṟūḻi ulakam kāttu
vāḻi eṅkō maṉṉava,
eṉṟu terivittu niṉṟaṉar. aracaṉ, viriyac ceṉṟu am maṭak koṭiyi iṅkē aḻittu varuka
eṉa, kāvalar ceṉṟu uritta lum maṇimēkali, aracaṉmuṉ pōntu avaṉi vāḻtti niṉṟāḷ.
vēntaṉ :
[49] tāṅkarum taṉmit tavattōy! nīyār?
[50] yāṅku ākiyatu iv vēntiya kaṭiñi?
maṇimēkali :
[51] virittār vēntē !. nī nīṭu vāḻi
[52] viñci makaḷyāṉ; [53]viḻavaṇi mūtūr
vañcam tirintēṉ; vāḻiyar peruntaki !
[54]vāṉam vāykka; maṇ vaḷam perukuka;
tītiṉ ṟāka kōmakaṟku caṅku; ītu
aiyak kaṭiñi; [55] ampala maruṅku ōr
teyvam tantatu; tippiya māyatu;
yāṉit tīnōy arumpaci keṭuttatu;
[56] ūṉuṭi mākkaṭku uyirmaruntu itu.
vēntaṉ :- iḷaṅkoṭi; yāṉ ceyaṟpālatu eṉ?
maṇimēkali:
ciṟiyōr kōṭṭam [57]cīttu, aruḷneñcattu
aṟavōrkku [58]ākkumatu; vāḻiyar
itukēṭṭu uḷḷam icinta aracar peruntaki, ac ciṟik kōṭṭattuḷḷārkkuc ciṟivīṭu
ceytu, ak kōṭṭattiyum iṭittut tūymi ceytu, aṟavōr vāḻum aṟakkōṭṭamākkiṉaṉ.

[3]. ayarppāy - maṟantiṭuka.

aṭi piḻit tāy - aṭiyil vaṇaṅkātu kuṟṟappaṭṭāy.
[6]. iḷaṅkoṭi - maṇimēkali.
[7]. kiviṭalīyāṉ - kiviṭāṉ. ' aracutuyilīyātu " (patiṟ. 12)
