[12]. kuḻalici kāṭṭa - vēyṅkuḻaliṉ ōciyic ceyya.
[13]. poṅkar - cōli. [14]. puṇar tuṇi - peṇ ṇaṉṉam.
[15]. iruciṟi - iraṇṭu ciṟakukaḷiyum. oru ciṟi - orupakkattē.
[16]. tammuṉ - palatēvaṉ. piñci - nappiṉṉi; aṉṉi, “meṉṟō ḷaññi ' (akam) yeṉa
vantāṟpōla.
[17]. kōṅkalar - kōṅkampū. [18]. pāṅku - arukē.
[19]. ōr pāvi - oru peṇ. " kōṅkalarukkup poṉ taṭṭum, māṅ kaṉikkuk kiḷiyum,
mayi lukkup peṇṇum uvami.
[20]. aṇimalarp pūmpoḻil akavayiṉ - aḻakiya pūkkaḷ niṟinta cōlikkuḷ.
[21]. piṇavukkuraṅku - peṇ kuraṅku. [22]. maṭavōrkku - makaḷirkku.
[23]. kaṭuvaṉ - āṇ kuraṅku. ūkkuvatu - āṭṭuvatu. "aiyaciṟi teṉṉi yūkki' (kali )
eṉṟāṟpōla.
[24]. taḷavu - oruvaki mulli. [25]. muruku - naṟumaṇam.
[26]. karuviḷam poṅkar - karuviḷam putar.
[27]. kuṟuṅkāl nakulam - kuṟukiya kālkaḷiyuṭiya kari.
[28]. piṟaḻntu - neṟimāṟi. [29]. iṟumpu - kuṟuṅkāṭu, veṟi - āṭu.
[30]. iṭuṅkaṟkuṉṟam - ceykuṉṟu,
[31]. parappunīrp poyki - nīr niṟinta paranta poyki.
[32]. karappu nīrkkēṇi - maṟitaliyuṭiya nīr niṟinta kiṇaṟu; itu antak kēṇi
yeṉappaṭum.
[33]. oḷit tuṟiyiṭam - oḷittirukkumiṭam.
[34]. makataviṉiñar - makatanāṭṭut toḻilāḷar.
[35]. taṇ tamiḻviṉiñar - taṇṭamiḻ nāṭṭut toḻilāḷar.
[36]. pōtiki - tūṇ muṭiyil irukkum pōtikik kaṭṭi; ita ṉip pōtikkaṭṭi yeṉpar.
[37]. tavaḷanittilam - veḷḷiya muttu.
[38]. kōṇac canti - kōṇamākiya canti. vitāṉam - mēṟkaṭṭi,
[39]. tamaṉiyam - poṉ. vaṉappu - aḻaku.
[40]. ārpuṉi teriyal - āttippūvāl toṭutta māli.
[41]. kāriyāṟṟukkoṇṭa - kāriyāṟṟiṉ kariyil veṉṟi peṟṟa. tōṟṟavaṉ " kāriyāṟṟut
tuñciya neṭuṅkiḷḷi " eṉpavaṉ.
[42]. valikeḻu taṭakki - valiporuntiya periya ki.
[43]. oḷi - aracarpāluḷḷa kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi yeṉpar.
[44]. vampamātar - putiyaḷāy vanta kāyacaṇṭiki.
[45]. akavayiṉ . uḷḷē . [46]. perumpeyar - mikka pukaḻ.
[47]. aiyap pāttiram. piccip pāttiram - amutacurapi.
[48].' moykoḷ mākkaḷ - neruṅkukiṉṟa makkaḷ. mocikka- uṇṇa..
[49]. tāṅkarum taṉmi - tāṅkutaṟku arumiyāṉa taṉmi.
[50]. yāṅku ākiyatu - evviṭattē peṟappaṭṭatu.
[51]. virittār - maṇam kamaḻum māli .
[52]. viñcimakaḷ - vittiyātarap peṇ; pala vittikaḷiyuṭiya peṇ.
[53]. viḻavaṇi mūtūr - viḻāvāl aḻaku koṇṭa paḻiya ūrākiya ik kāvirippūm
paṭṭiṉattil.
[54]. vāṉam vāykka - maḻi tappātu peyka.
[55]. ampalam - ūrampalam.
[56]. ūṉuṭimākkaḷ - veṟṟuṭal tāṅkum makkaḷukku. uyir maruntu - uṇavu.
[57]. cīttu - iṭittut takarttu.
[58]. ākkumatu - kōṭṭam amippatē ceyaṟpālatu.
-----------

20. utayakumaraṉi vāḷāl eṟinta kāti

maṇimēkaliyāl aracaṉ ciṟikkōṭṭattic cīttuc ceyta aṟak kōṭṭattē,
[1] poruḷpuri neñciṟ pulavōṉ kōyilum
aruḷ puri neñcattu aṟavōr paḷḷiyum
[2] aṭṭiṟ cāliyum [3]aruntunar cāliyum
uḷavāyiṉa. in nikaḻcci muṟṟum aṟintāṉ utayakumaraṉ. uṭaṉē
avaṉ, avaḷi, evvāṟēṉum koḷḷal vēṇṭumeṉṟu karuti,
[4] matiyōr eḷḷiṉum maṉṉavaṉ kāyiṉum,
[5] potiyil nīṅkiya poḻutiṟ ceṉṟu,
paṟṟiṉaṉ koṇṭu eṉ poṟṟēr ēṟṟi,
[6] kaṟṟaṟi vicciyum kēṭṭu avaḷ urikkum
[7] mutukkuṟi mutumoḻi kēṭkuvaṉ eṉṟē
maṉaṅkoṇṭu, maṇimēkali taṅkiyirunta ulakavaṟavikkuc ceṉ ṟāṉ. akkāli, kāyacaṇṭikiyiṉ
kaṇavaṉāṉa kāñcaṉaṉ, muṉivaṉ urittavāṟu paṉṉīrāṇṭu kaḻintum taṉ maṉivi tirumpa vārāmi
kaṇṭu, in nakarkkaṇ avaḷip palaviṭaṅkaḷilum tēṭit tirintu muṭivil, piccip pāttiram
ēntip palarkkum uṇavūṭṭi varuvatu kaṇṭu taṉ paḻimik kiḻamitōṉṟap pala uriyāṭiṉaṉ. utaya
kumaraṉum aṅkē uṭaṉiruntāṉ. maṇimēkali kāyacaṇṭiki vaṭi vil niṉṟāḷāyiṉum, avaḷ,
akkāñcaṉaṉpāl cellātu, utaya kumaraṉ mukanōkki, avaṉ uḷḷattiyum māṟṟa niṉintāḷ. ap
pōtu avvaḻiyē pōnta toṇṭukiḻavi yoruttiyik kāṭṭi,
[8] taṇṇaṟal vaṇṇam tirintuvē ṟāki
veṇmaṇa lākiya kūntal kāṇāy;
[9] piṟi nutal vaṇṇam kāṇā yō, nī,
narimiyiṉ tiritōl [10] takiyiṉ ṟāyatu;
[11] viṟalviṟ puruvam iviyum kāṇāy,
[12] iṟaviṉ uṇaṅkal pōṉṟu vē ṟāyiṉa;
[13] kaḻunīrk kaṇ, kāṇ, [14] vaḻunīr cumantaṉa;
[15] kumiḻmūkku, ivi,kāṇ, umiḻcī oḻukkuva;
nirimut taṉiya [16] nakiyum kāṇāy,
[17] curivit tēyppap piṟaḻntuvē ṟāyiṉa;
[18] ilavitaḻc cevvāy, kāṇā yō, nī,
[19] pulavup puṇ pōl pulālpuṟat tiṭuvatu;
[20] vaḷḷit tāḷpōl vaṭikātu ivi kāṇ,
uḷḷūṉ vāṭiya uṇaṅkal pōṉṟaṉa;
[21] iṟumpūtu cāṉṟa muliyum kāṇāy,
veṟumpi pōla vīḻntu vē ṟāyiṉa;
[22] tāḻntoci teṅkiṉ maṭalpōl [23]tiraṅki,
vīḻntaṉa, iḷa[24]vēyt tōḷum kāṇāy,
narampoṭu viṭutōl [25]ukirttoṭar kaḻaṉṟu,
tiraṅkiya viralkaḷ iviyum kāṇāy; -
vāḻit taṇṭē pōṉṟa [26]kuṟaṅkiṇi,
tāḻit taṇṭiṉ uṇaṅkal kāṇāy;
[27] āvak kaṇikkāl kāṇā yō, nī,
mēviya narampōṭu eṉpupuṟaṅ [28]kāṭṭuva;
[29] taḷiraṭi vaṇṇam kāṇā yō, nī,
[30] muḷimutir teṅkiṉ utirkāy uṇaṅkal,
pūviṉum cāntiṉum [31]pulāl maṟittu yāttu,
[32] tūciṉum aṇiyiṉum tollōr vakutta
vañcam teriyāy, maṉṉavaṉ makaṉ !
eṉṟu avaḷatu uṟuppukkaḷiṉ paṇṭi vaṉappum, iṉṟiya aḻi vum eṭuttu viḷakkiṉaḷ.
ic ceytiyik kāñcaṉaṉ kaṇṭu maṉattē poṟāmiyum ciṉamum poṅki mikat taṉ neñciṟkuḷ,
[33]taṟpā rāṭṭum eṉ coṟpayaṉ koḷḷāḷ;
piṟaṉ piṉ cellum; [34] piṟaṉ pōl nōkkum;
[35] matukkamaḻ alaṅkal maṉṉavaṉ makaṟku
mutukkuṟi mutumoḻi eṭuttuk kāṭṭip
[36] pavaḷak kaṭikiyil tavaḷavāḷ nakiyum,
kuvaḷic ceṅkaṇum [37]kuṟippoṭu vaḻā aḷ;
īṅku ivaṉ kātala ṉātaliṉ, ēntiḻi
īṅku oḻintaṉaḷ
eṉa niṉintu, avviṭattē orucār taṅkiyiruntāṉ. utayakumā ṉum, am maṇimēkali
yuritta mutukkuṟi mutumoḻikaḷic cevi yiṟ koḷḷātu,
vaḷi cēr ceṅki maṇimē kaliyē,
kāyacaṇṭikiyāyk kaṭiñi yēnti,
[38] māya viñciyiṉ maṉammayak kuṟuttaṉaḷ;
ampala maruṅkil ayarntu aṟi vuritta iv
[39] vampavaṉ taṉṉoṭu iv vikiruḷ oḻiyāḷ;
iṅku ivaḷ ceyti [40]iṭiyiruḷ yāmattu,
vantu aṟikuvaṉ,
eṉa uṭkoṇṭu, iṭiyiruḷ yāmattē mīṇṭuvarat tuṇintu tērēṟic ceṉṟāṉ.
aṟṟippōtu kaḻintatu; iṭiyiruḷ yāmamum. eytuvatā yiṟṟu. utayakumaraṉ araṇmaṉiyiṉ
nīṅki, maṇimēkali yirunta am palam nōkki vantāṉ. kāñcaṉaṉ maṇimēkali yirunta iṭattiṟ
kēkum neṟikkaṇ uṟakkamiṉṟik kiṭantāṉ; ataṉi yuṇarātu pōnta utayakumaraṉ,
[41] vēka ventī nākam kiṭanta
[42] pōkuyar puṟṟaḷi pukuvāṉ pōla,
[43] ākam tōynta cāntu [44] alar uṟutta
[45]ūḻaṭi yiṭṭu ataṉ uḷḷakam pukutalum,
āṅkumuṉ ṉirunta alartār viñciyaṉ,
" īṅku ivaṉ vantaṉaṉ ivaḷ pāl " eṉṟē,
veñciṉa aravam [46]nañceyiṟu arumpa,
taṉ peru vekuḷiyiṉ eḻuntu [47]pi viritteṉa,
[48] iruntōṉ eḻuntu perumpiṉ ceṉṟu, avaṉ
[49] curumpu aṟi maṇittōḷ tuṇiya vīci,
uḷḷē ceṉṟu, kāyacaṇṭikiyākiya maṇimēkaliyit tāṉē valitiṟ kikkoṇṭu cellak karutic
ceṉṟāṉ. appōḻtil āṅkirunta kantiṟpāvi,
aṇukal, aṇukal; viñcik kāñcaṉa!
maṇimēkali avaḷ; maṟinturu veytiṉaḷ;
kāyacaṇ ṭikitaṉ kaṭumpaci nīṅki,
vāṉam pōvuḻi vantatu kēḷāy
eṉṟu toṭaṅkik kāyacaṇṭiki vāṉavaḻiyē celluṅkāl vinta maliyiliruntu ataṉik kākkum
vintākaṭiki, taṉmēl kāyacaṇṭi kiyiṉ cāyi tākka, ataṉāl, avaḷi yiḻuttut taṉ
vayiṟṟil aṭakkikkoṇṭāḷ.
[50]kimmi koḷḷēl, kāñcaṉa ! itukēḷ :
ūḻviṉi vantu iṅku utaya kumaraṉi,
āruyir uṇṭatu; āyiṉum, aṟiyāy :
[51] vevviṉi ceytāy, viñcik kāñcaṉa !
avviṉi niṉṉiyum akalātu āṅkuṟum
eṉṟatu. itukēṭṭu viñciyaṉum varuntiya uḷḷattaṉāyt taṉ viñciyarulaki yaṭintāṉ.
---
[1]. poruḷ puri neñciṉ pulavōṉ - vāymi nāṉkiyum virumpu eṉṟa neñcamuṭiya
puttaṉ. poruḷ - meymmi .
[2]. aṭṭiṟ cāli - uṇavu camikkumiṭam;
[3]. aruntunar cāli - uṇavu uṇpōr cāli.
[4]. matiyōr - aṟivuṭiyōr. [5]. potiyil - potu iṭam; ampalam.
[6]. kaṟṟaṟivicci - kaṟṟaṟintuḷḷa vittikaḷ palavum.
[7]. mutukkuṟi mutumoḻi - aṟivup poruḷ niṟinta col,
[8]. taṇ aṟal - kuḷirnta karumaṇal. [9]. piṟi nutal - piṟipōlum neṟṟi.
[10]. taki - aḻaku.
[11]. viṟal vil puruvam - viṟal paṭitta villotta puruvam,
[12]. iṟaviṉ uṇaṅkal - iṟāmīṉiṉ vaṟṟal.
[13]. kaḻunīrk kaṇ - ceṅkaḻunīr malarpōṉṟa kaṇ.
[14]. vaḻunīr - pīḷi vaṭiyum kaṇṇī r.
[15]. kumiḻ - kumiḻampū. [16]. naki - pal,
[17]. curi vittu - curikkāyiṉ viti. [18]. ilavitaḻc cevvāy - ilavam pūviṉ
itaḻpōl civanta itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya vāy.
[19]. pulavuppuṇ - pulāl nāṟṟam nāṟum puṇ.
[20]. vaḷḷit tāḷ - vaḷḷik koṭiyiṉ ili.
[21]. iṟumpūtu - perumitam.
[22] tāḻntu ōci maṭal - tāḻntu vaḷintu vīḻntu toṅkum maṭal.
[23]. tiraṅki - tirintu.
[24]. vēyt tōḷ - mūṅkil pōlum tōḷ.
[25]. ukir - nakam,
[26]. kuṟaṅkiṇi - tuṭiyi raṇṭu.
[27]. āvak kaṇikkāl - ampup puṭṭil pōṉṟa kaṇikkāl.
[28]. taḷiraṭi - taḷir otta aṭi.
[29]. puṟaṅkāṭṭuva - puṟattē tōṉṟumpaṭi terikiṉṟaṉa.
[30]. muḷi - ularntu. [31]. pulāl maṟittu - pulāl nāṟātapaṭi maṟittu.
[32]. tūci - puṭivi. [33]. taṟpā rāṭṭum - taṉṉip pārāṭṭukiṉṟa.
[34]. piṟaṉ pōl - ayalāṉ pōla.
[35]. matukkamaḻ alaṅkal - tēṉ maṇakkum māli.
[36]. pavaḷak kaṭiki - pavaḷat tuṇṭam. [37]. kuṟippu - iṅkitam.
[38]. māyaviñci - māyavitti . [39]. vampalaṉ - putiyōṉ; ayalāṉ.
[40]. iṭiyiruḷ yāmam - naḷḷiravu.
[41]. vēka ventī - koṭitāyp paṟṟum nañcu.
[42]. pōkuyar puṟṟaḷi - mikavuyarnta puṟṟiṉ puḻi.
[43]. ākam - mārpu.
[44]. alar uṟutta - maṇattāl avaṉ iruppiṉik kāṭṭa.
[45]. ūḻaṭiyiṭṭu - muṟiyē aṭimēl aṭi vittu.
[46]. eyiṟu nañcu arumpa - pal viṭattik kakka.
[47]. pi viritteṉa - paṭatti virittāṟ pōla.
[48]. iruntōṉ - irunta kāñcaṉaṉ.
[49]. curumpu aṟi maṇit tōḷ - vaṇṭu olikkum māliyaṇinta aḻakiya tōḷ.
[50]. kimmi - ivaḷ kimpeṇ; ivaḷ kiyip paṟṟutalākiya iḻi ceyal.
[51]. vevviṉi - koli.

21. kantiṟpāvi varuvatu uritta kāti

campāpati kōyiliṉ uḷḷē kiṭanta maṇimēkali kāñcaṉaṉ ceytiyum, utayakumaraṉ vīḻcciyum,
kantiṟpāvi ak kāñcaṉaṟ kurittatum kēṭṭu, eḻuntu, taṉ uṇmi vaṭivukoṇṭu veḷip
pōntu, aracakumaraṉatu uṭalikkaṇṭu, tuyarmikuntu,
[1] tiṭṭi viṭamuṇa niṉṉuyir pōmnāḷ
[2]kaṭṭaḻal īmattu eṉ uyir cuṭṭēṉ;
uvavaṉa maruṅkil niṉpāl uḷḷam
[3] tavirvilē ṉātaliṉ, tali makaḷ tōṉṟi
maṇippal lavattiṭi eṉṉi āṅ kuyttup
[4]piṇippaṟu mātavaṉ pīṭiki kāṭṭi
eṉ piṟap puṇarnta eṉ muṉ tōṉṟi,
uṉ piṟap pellām [5]oḻiviṉ ṟurittaliṉ,
piṟantōr iṟattalum, iṟantōr piṟattalum,
[6] aṟantaru cālpum, maṟantaru tuṉpamum,
yāṉ niṉak kurittu niṉ [7]iṭarviṉi yoḻikkak
kāyacaṇ ṭikivaṭi vāṉēṉ, kātala!
[8] vivāḷ viñciyaṉ mēyakkuṟu vekuḷiyiṉ
vevviṉi yuruppa viḷintaṉiyō
eṉac collip pulampaluṟṟaṉaḷ. appōḻtil, kantiṟpāvi maṇimēkaliyi yaḻittu,
cellal, cellal; [9]cēyari neṭuṅkaṇ!
[10] alliyan tārōṉ taṉpāṟ cellal,
niṉakku ivaṉ [11]makaṉāt tōṉṟi yatū um,
maṉakkiṉi yāṟku nī [12] makaḷā yatū um
paṇṭum paṇṭum palpiṟap puḷavāl;
kaṇṭa piṟaviyē alla, kāriki!
[13]taṭumāṟu piṟavit tāḻtaru tōṟṟam
viṭumāṟu muyalvōy, [14] viḻumam koḷḷēl
eṉṟu urittatu. atukēṭṭa maṇimēkali, "ivvampalattu uṟi yum teyvamākiya nī, ceṉṟa
piṟappil ivaṉ tiṭṭiviṭattāl uyiriḻantatum, ippiṟappil iṉṟu īṅku viñciyaṉāl
veṭṭuṇṭu vīḻntatum aṟivāyaṉṟō . aṟintaṉiyāyiṉ, ivaṉ vāḷ vāy vīḻnta kāraṇam viḷampa
vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ ” eṉṟāḷ.
uṭaṉē at teyvam,“muṉṉip piṟaviyil, kāyaṅkari eṉṉum āṟṟaṅkariyil, nī niṉ kaṇavaṉ
irākulaṉōṭu iruntapōtu, aṅku uṅkaḷpāl vanta piramataruma muṉivaṉukku uṇavucamittal
vēṇṭi maṭiyaṉi viṭiyalil varap paṇittīr; avaṉ avvāṟē varuṅkāl, aṭitaḷarntu maṭikkalam
citiya vīḻntāṉ. ataṉi nōkkātu, muṉivarkkuc ceytaṟkuriya paṇikku muṭṭuppāṭu
uṇṭākkiṉāṉeṉa am maṭi yaṉi irākulaṉ ciṉantu taṉ kivāḷāl tuṇittuk koṉṟāṉ. ak
koliviṉiyē, avaṉi ippiṟappil viñciyaṉāl veṭ ṭuṇṭu vīḻac ceytatu, kāṇ" eṉṟu
colli, mēlum, viṉiyiṉ iyalpu ciṟitu kūṟaluṟṟa atteyvam,
talivaṉ kākkum, tamporuṭ ṭākiya
[16]avala vevviṉi yeṉpōr aṟiyār;
aṟañcey kātal aṉpiṉi ṉāyiṉum,
[17]maṟamcey tuḷateṉiṉ valviṉi yoḻiyātu;
āṅku avviṉi vantu aṇukuṅ kālit
[18] tīṅkuṟum uyirē ceyviṉi maruṅkiṉ
mīṇṭuvaru piṟappil mīḷiṉum mīḷum;
āṅku avviṉi kāṇ, āyiḻi ! [19]kaṇavaṉi
īṅku vantiv viṭarcey toḻintatu,
eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭu, ataṉmēlum, maṇimēkalikku nikaḻaviruppaṉa vaṟṟiyum muṉpē moḻiyat
toṭaṅki,
iṉṉuṅ kēḷāy : iḷaṅkoṭi nallāy!
maṉṉavaṉ makaṟku [20]varuntu tuyar eyti
mātavar uṇarttiya vāymoḻi kēṭṭu,
kāvalaṉ niṉṉiyum [21]kāval cey tāṅkiṭum;
īṭu ciṟi nīkki, irācamā tēvi
kūṭavikkum [22]koṭpiṉa ḷāki,
mātavi [23]mātavaṉ malaraṭi vaṇaṅkit
tītu kūṟa avaḷ taṉṉoṭum cērntu
mātavaṉ uritta vāymoḻi kēṭṭuk
[24] kātali niṉṉiyum kāval nīkkuvaḷ;
aricāḷ celvattu āput tiraṉ pāl,
[25] puriyōrp pēṇip pōkalum pōkuvi;
pōṉā lavaṉoṭum [26]poruḷuri porunti,
[27] mānīr vaṅkattu avaṉoṭum eḻuntu,
[28] māyamil ceyti maṇipal lavameṉum
tīvakattu [29]. iṉṉum cēṟalumuṇṭāl;
tīva tilakiyiṉ taṉ tiṟam kēṭṭuc
[30]cāvaka maṉṉaṉ taṉ nā ṭaṭintapiṉ āṅkat
[31]tīvamviṭṭu aruntavaṉ vaṭivāyp
pūṅkoṭi! vañci mānakar pukuvi;
āṅku an nakarattu [32]aṟiporuḷ viṉāvum
[33] ōṅkiya kēḷvi uyarntōr palarāl.
ivvāṟu palarāya camayakkaṇakkar tam tiṟam kēṭṭu, avarkaṭkut takuvaṉa kūṟic ciṟappeytuvāy;
ātalāl, tīviṉi vantu uṟuttalum cettōr piṟattalum uṇmiyeṉṟu tuṇintu niṉ maṉamayakkam
oḻivāyāka eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu.
ivvaṇṇam collip pōnta at teyvam, tāṉ kantiṟ pāviyāy niṉṟu makkaṭku varuvaturittu
varum tiṟattik kūṟat toṭaṅki,
eṉ tiṟam kēṭṭiyō : iḷaṅkoṭi nallāy!
[34] maṉperun teyva kaṇaṅkaḷi ṉuḷḷēṉ,
tuvatikaṉ eṉpēṉ; [35] toṉṟu mutir kantiṉ
mayaṉ eṉak koppā vakutta pāviyiṉ
nīṅkēṉ yāṉ; eṉ niliyatu kēḷāy;
māntar aṟivatu vāṉavar aṟiyār;
ōviyac cēṉaṉ eṉ [36] uṟu tuṇit tōḻaṉ
āvati innakarkku ārurit taṉarō ?
avaṉuṭaṉ yāṉ ceṉṟu āṭiṭa mellām
[37] uṭaṉuṟin tārpōl oḻiyā teḻutip
pūvum pukiyum [38]poruntupu puṇarntu
[39] nānaṉi varunteṉ nalampā rāṭṭaliṉ
maṇimē kali!yāṉ varuporuḷ ellām
tuṇivuṭaṉ urittēṉ; eṉ col [40]tēṟuka.
eṉṟu collavē, maṇimēkali, " yāṉ tēṟātoḻiyēṉ; iṉi eṉakku muṭivukāṟum
nikaḻaviruppaṉavaṟṟiyum kūṟuka" eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ. avvāṟē, avaṟṟiyum collat toṭaṅkiya at
teyvam, kāñcimānakar vaṟampaṭutalum, āṅkē maṇimēkali ceṉṟu, taṉ piccippāttirattāl
uyirōmputalum, mēlum aṅku nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷum, camayakkaṇakkar palaroṭu kēṭṭavaṟṟi
aṟavaṇavaṭi kaṭku urippatum, avar taṉ ceyal ceppuvatum, piṉpu maṇimēkali makatanāṭṭil
āṇmakaṉāyp piṟantu aruḷaṟam pūṇṭu, putta tēva ṉukku mutal māṇavaṉākutalum piṟavum
kūṟi, nī muṟpiṟappil cātu cakkaramuṉivaṟku amutaḷitta nalviṉip payaṉāl, maṇimēkalā
teyvam niṉṉi maṇipallavattukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu putta pīṭiki kaṇṭu paḻampiṟappi nī
yaṇarntukoḷḷumāṟu ceytatu” eṉṟum colli muṭittatu.
---
[1]. tiṭṭiviṭam - kaṇṇil nañcuṭiya pāmpu.
[2]. kaṭṭaḻal - mikka neruppu. [3]. tavirvilēṉ - nīṅkātu celuttiṉēṉ.
[4]. piṇippaṟu mātavaṉ - paṟṟaṟṟa mātavaṉākiya puttaṉ.
[5]. oḻiviṉṟu - kuṟiyāmal; ciṟitum viṭāmal.
[6]. aṟantaru cālpu - aṟattāl uṇṭākum iṉpa niṟivu.
[7]. iṭar viṉi - tuṉpattic ceyyum viṉi; kātaṟ ceyki.
[8]. vi vāḷ - kūriya vāḷ. [9]. mayakkuṟu vekuḷi . aṟivi mayakkum ciṉam.
[10]. cēyari keṭuṅkaṇ . civanta ariparanta neṭiya kaṇṇiyuṭiyāy.
[11]. alliyan tār - pūviṉ akavitaḻkaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa māli.
[12]. makaṉ - kaṇavaṉ. [13]. makaḷ - maṉivi.
[14]. taṭumāṟu piṟavi - makaṉumakaḷumāy māṟip piṟantu iṟantu varum piṟavi.
tāḻtaru - taṅkum.
[15]. viḻumam - tuṉpam.
[16]. avalavevviṉi - melivi yuṇṭupaṇṇum koṭiya viṉi. viṉiyiṉiṉṟum tammit
talivaṉākiya kaṭavuḷ kāppaṉ eṉpōr aṟiyā tār eṉka. iḵtu iṟivaṉoruvaṉuṇmi tuṇintu
viṉittuyar nīṅka aṉpu neṟiyil vaḻipaṭum civar mutalaryiṉārkku maṟuppu.
[17]. maṟam ceytuḷatu - pāvam ceyyappaṭṭatāyiṉ.
[18]. tīṅku - tuṉpam. [19]. kaṇavaṉi - utayakumaraṉi.
[20]. varuntu tuyar - piriveṇṇi varuntu taṟku ētuvākiya tuyaram.
[21]. kāval - ciṟi.
[22]. koṭpu - koḷki. [23]. mātavaṉ - aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ,
[24]. kātali - aṉpuṭiyaḷākiya.
[25]. puriyōr - uyarntōr; īṇṭuc caṅkattār. pōkalum pōkuvi - pōvāy.
[26]. poruḷuri - aṟavuri. porunti - kēṭṭu.
[27].. mānīr - kaṭal. [28]. māyam il ceyti - kuṟṟamillāta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya.
[29].. iṉṉum cēṟal - maṟupaṭiyum celvatu. maṇimēkali āputtira ṉuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭu,
avaṉ vaṅkattil vara, tāṉ vicumpu vaḻiyāka maṇi pallavam aṭintāḷ. uru -ām kāti kāṇka.
[30]. cāvka maṉṉaṉ - āputtiraṉ. [31]. tīvam - maṇipallavam.
[32]. aṟiporuḷ - aṟinta camayap poruḷ.
[33]. ōṅkiya - uyarnta. uyarntōr palarāl - uyarntōr palar uḷar.
[34]. maṉ - nilipeṟṟa. kaṇam - kūṭṭam.
[35]. toṉṟu mutir kantu - mikap paḻamiyāṉa tūṇ, mayaṉ . teyvat taccaṉ.
[36]. uṟu tuṇi - mikka tuṇivaṉ.
[37]. uṭaṉ uṟintār - uṭaṉiruntu kaṇṭavar,
[38]. poruntupu - porunta, [39]. nā naṉi varunta - nā mikat taṭippēṟa.
[40]. tēṟuka - teḷivāy.

22. ciṟicey kāti

poḻutu pularntatum, campāpatiyiyum kantiṟpāviyiyum vaḻipaṭa vantavar utayakumaraṉ
veṭṭuṇṭu kiṭappatik kaṇṭu; āṅku vāḻum muṉivarkaḷukku urittaṉar. avarkaḷ maṇi
mēkaliyi viṉavi nikaḻntataṟintu avaḷiyum utayakumaraṉ uṭaliyum ōriṭattē oḷittuviṭṭu,
naṭanta ceytiyi aracarkkuṇarttac ceṉṟaṉar. ceṉṟavaruḷ oruvar, aracaṉik kaṇṭu,
uyarntōṅ kucci [1] uvāmati pōla
[2] nivantōṅku veṇkuṭi maṇṇakam niḻaṟceya,
vēlum kōlum [3]aruṭkaṇ viḻikka;
tītiṉ ṟuruḷka nī ēntiya [4]tikiri;
niṉakkeṉa [5]varinta āṇṭuka ḷellām
maṉakkiṉi tāka : vāḻiya vēntē!
iṉṟē yalla; ippati maruṅkil
[6]kaṉṟiya kāmak kaḷḷāṭ ṭayarntu
pattiṉip peṇṭir pāṟceṉ ṟaṇukiyum
naṟṟavap peṇṭir piṉṉuḷam pōkkiyum
[7] tīviṉi yuruppa uyir [8]īṟu ceytōr
[9]pārāḷ vēntē ! paṇṭum palarāl;
eṉṟu moḻintu, ivaṟṟukku eṭuttukkāṭṭākap pārppaṉi marutiyeṉṉum pattiṉip
peṇṭirpāl karuttuc celutti uyiriḻanta kakantaṉ mintaṉ ceytiyum, vicāki yeṉṉum
naṟṟava makaḷ mītu kātaluṟṟa am mintaṉukku muṉ piṟantōṉ ceytiyum eṭuttuk kūṟi
aracaṉi vāḻtti niṉṟaṉar. atukēṭṭa vēntaṉ,
iṉṟē yalla eṉṟeṭut turittu,
[10]naṉṟaṟi mātavir? [11]nalampala kāṭṭiṉir;
iṉṟum uḷatō ivviṉi ? uriyum,
eṉṟu viṉaviṉaṉ; avaṉukku ammā tavar kūṭṭattu oruvar muṉ vantu, " tītiṉṟāka, ceṅkōl
vēntē!" eṉṟu toṭaṅki,
[12] muṭi poruḷ uṇarntōr [13]mutu nīrulakil
[14] kaṭiyappaṭṭaṉa aintuḷa, avaṟṟil
kaḷḷum poyyum kaḷavum koliyum
taḷḷā tākum kāmam; tampāl
āṅ [15]katu kaṭintōr [16]allavi kaṭintōreṉa
nīṅkiṉa raṉṟē niṟitava mākkaḷ;
nīṅkā raṉṟē, nīḷnila vēntē
[17]tāṅkā narakam taṉṉiṭi uḻappōr
eṉṟu toṭaṅki, maṇimēkaliyiṉ varalāṟum, avaḷ pāl utaya kumaraṉ karuttuc celuttiyuḻaṉṟa
tiṟamum, avaḷ kāyacaṇṭiki yiṉ vaṭivukoṇṭu tirinta vakiyum, viñciyaṉ ceytiyum
viriyak kūṟi,
...... tīviṉi urutta tākaliṉ,
[18] matimaruḷ veṇkuṭi maṉṉa ! niṉ makaṉ
utaya kumaraṉ oḻiyā ṉāka,
āṅkavaḷ taṉṉi ampalat tēṟṟi,
ōṅkiruḷ yāmattu ivaṉi āṅ kuyttuk
kāyacaṇ ṭikitaṉ kaṇava ṉākiya
[19] vāyvāḷ viñciyaṉ taṉṉiyum kūuy
viñci makaḷ pāl ivaṉ van taṉaṉeṉa
[20] vañca viñciyaṉ maṉattiyum kalakki
āṅkavaṉ taṉki vāḷāl ampalattu
īṅkivaṉ taṉṉi eṟintatu,
eṉṟu coṉṉār. atukēṭṭa aracaṉ neñcil taṉ makaṉ ceykiyi aruvaruttu, arukukiṉṟa
cōḻika ēṉātiyi nōkki,
yāṉ [21]ceyaṟpālatu iḷaṅkōṉ taṉṉit
tāṉ cey tataṉāl [22]takavilaṉ viñciyaṉ;
mātavar nōṉpum maṭavār kaṟpum
kāvalaṉ kāval iṉ ṟeṉiṉ iṉṟāl;
makaṉi muṟiceyta [23]maṉṉavaṉ vaḻi, ōr
[24] tuyarviṉi yāḷaṉ tōṉṟiṉaṉ eṉpatu,
vēntar tamcevi [25]uṟuvataṉ muṉṉam
īṅku ivaṉ taṉṉiyum īmat tēṟṟik
[26] kaṇiki makaḷiyum kāval ceyka
eṉṟu paṇittāṉ. maṇimēkaliyum ciṟiyiṭap peṟṟāḷ.
---
[1]. uvāmati - muḻut tiṅkaḷ.
[2]. nivantōṅku - mikavuyarnta, oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi.
[3] aruṭkaṇ viḻikka - aruḷākiya aṟamē taḻippikka.
[4]. tikiri - aracāṭci. aracāṭciyi uruḷi (tikiri) eṉ ṟamiyiṉ ataṟkēṟpa,
"uruḷka" eṉṟār. celka eṉpatu karuttu.
[5]. varinta - aṟutiyiṭṭa. [6]. kaṉṟiya - ceṟinta; aṭippaṭṭa.
[7]. tīviṉi uruppa - tīviṉi vantu tūṇṭa. [8]. īṟu - kēṭu.
[9]. pār - cōṇāṭu. [10]naṉṟu - aṟam.
[11]. nalam pala - naṟceytikaḷ pala.
[12]. muṭiporuḷuṇarntōr - "pāramārttika ñāṉikaḷ'.
[13]. mutu nīr ulaku - paḻimiyāṉa kaṭal cūḻnta ulakil.
[14]. kaṭiyappaṭṭaṉa - vilakkappaṭṭaṉa. avi, kaḷ, poy, kaḷavu. koli, kāmam.
[15]. atu - kāmam.
[16]. allavi - ēṉiya kaḷ mutaliya nāṉku.
[17]. tāṅkā narakam - poṟukkamāṭṭāta tuṉpam tarum narakam.
[18]. matimaruḷ veṇ kuṭi-muḻumati pōlum veṇkoṟṟakkuṭi.
[19]. vāy vāḷ - veṭṭutalil tappāta vāḷ.
[20]. vañca viñciyaṉ - koliyi maṉattē koṇṭirunta kāñcaṉaṉ. at tīviṉi, avaḷi ēṟṟi,
ivaṉi uyttu, viñciyaṉik kūuy, maṉattiyum kalakki, vāḷāl eṟintatu eṉa iyikka.
[21]. ceyaṟpālatu - ceytaṟkuriya kolit taṇṭatti.
[22]. takavilaṉ - aracu neṟiyaṟiyum takutiyillāṉ.
[23]. maṉṉavaṉ - maṉu vēntaṉ.
[24]. tuyar viṉi yāḷaṉ - tīviṉi yāḷaṉ (utaya kumaraṉ)
[25]. uṟuvataṉmuṉ - aṭivataṟku muṉpē .
[26]. kaṇiki - mātavi. makaḷ - maṇimēkali. taṉ makaṉ koli yuṇṭaṟkum, taṉ kuṭi paḻi
yeytu taṟkum kāraṇamātaliṉ, maṇimēkali yit tāymuṟi koṇṭu, 'kaṇiki makaḷ" eṉa aracaṉ
ikaḻntāṉ eṉka .
-------------

23. ciṟiviṭu kāti

taṉ putalvaṉ, utayakumaraṉ koliyuṇṭiṟantataṉāl, cōḻa vēntaṉ maṉivi irācamātēvikku
uṇṭāṉa tuyarattiṟku elli yilliyāyiṟṟu. aracaṉ kōyilil, aracarkkāyiṉum,
aracakumarark kāyiṉum, araciyarkkāyiṉum takuvaṉa kūṟit tuyarāṟṟuvikkum cāṉṟōr palar
iruntaṉar. avaruḷ vācantavi-yeṉpāḷum orutti. ivaḷ oru peru mutiyōḷ.
irācamātēvikku nērnta tuyaram tuṭikkum karuttiṉaḷāy avaḷ araciyiṉ kōyiṟku vantāḷ.
vantavaḷ, araciyiṉ aṭiyil vīḻnturikkum amitiyi vilakki,
aḻutu aṭi vīḻātu āyiḻi taṉṉit
toḻutu muṉ niṉṟu [1]tōṉṟa vāḻttik
[2]koṟṟam koṇṭu kuṭipuṟaṅ kāttuc
[3]ceṟṟa tevvar tēentama tākkiyum,
taruppiyiṟ kiṭatti vāḷiṟ pōḻntu .
[4] ceruppukal maṉṉar [5]celvuḻic celkeṉa
mūttu viḷital ik kuṭippiṟan tōrkku
[6]nāppuṭi peyarātu; nāṇuttaka vuṭittē.
eṉṉeṉap paṭumō niṉmakaṉ muṭintatu !
[7]maṉpati kākkum maṉṉavaṉ taṉmuṉ
tuṉpaṅ koḷḷēl eṉṟu avaḷ
colliviṭṭup pōyiṉaḷ. irācamātēviyum oruvāṟu tēṟi, taṉ makaṉ iṟattaṟkuk kāraṇamāy
niṉṟa maṇimēkaliyi vañcaṉi yāl muṭikkat tuṇintu, puṟattē meyyaṉpum teḷinta vuṇarvu
muṭiyāḷ pōl naṭittu, orunāḷ aracaṉi naṇṇi, avaṉ aṭi vīḻntu toḻutu,
piṟarpiṉ cellāp [8]pikkuṇik kōlattu
aṟivu [9]tirintōṉ [10]araciyal tāṉ ilaṉ;
karumpuṭit taṭakkik kāmaṉ [11]kiyaṟa
arumpeṟal iḷami [12]perumpiṟi tākkum
[13]aṟivu talippaṭṭa āyiḻi taṉakkuc
ciṟi [14]takkaṉṟu ceṅkōl vēntē!
eṉṟu collavē, aracaṉ avaḷatu vañcamaṟiyātu, ciṟanta aṟiviṉāl āyntu,
[15]ciṟappiṉ pālār makkaḷ; allār
maṟappiṉ pālār maṉṉarkku eṉpatu,
aṟintaṉi yāyiṉ ivvāyiḻi taṉṉic
ceṟinta [16]ciṟinōy tīrkka eṉṟu
aracikkut terivittu ēvalari nōkki maṇimēkaliyic ciṟi viṭumāṟu paṇittāṉ. uṭaṉē,
irācamātēvi, " aracē,
eṉṉōṭu iruppiṉum irukka, iv viḷaṅkoṭi
taṉ ōṭu eṭuppiṉum [17]takikkunar il"
eṉṟurittu, maṇimēkaliyi mellak koṇarntu taṉ akanakark kaṇṇē taṅkumāṟu ceytukoṇṭāḷ.
piṉpu, maṇimēkalikkup pittuṇṭāmāṟu maruntoṉṟit tantāḷ; maṟupiṟap puṇarntava
ḷātaliṉ, avaḷi am maruntu oṉṟum ceyyavilli. piṉporu kāl, araci, kallā iḷiñaṉ
oruvaṉi yaḻittu avaṉ kinnirampap poṉ koṭuttu, maṇimēkali mārpil puṇarkuṟi ceytu
paḻip puṇṭāmāṟu ceyya viṭuttāḷ; avaṉ varukikkuṟip paṟintatum maṇimēkali,
vēṟṟurukkoḷḷum mantiram ōti, āṇurukkoṇ ṭiruntāḷ. vantavaṉ, " aracar urimi makaḷir
irukkumiṭattē āṭavar kuṟukār; iṅkōr āṭavaṉ uḷaṉ; ītu ētō vañcam " eṉṟu añci
yōṭiviṭṭāṉ. vēṟoru nāḷ, maṇimēkali nōyāl uṇavukoḷvatilaḷ eṉap poy kūṟi, avaḷi,
araci, oru puḻukkaṟiyil uṇavu koṭātu aṭittuvittāḷ; āyiṉum, am maṇimēkali,
ūṇoḻi mantiram ōti, mēṉi varuntātu iṉi tiruntāḷ. ic ceykikaḷāl avaṭkuc
ciṟitum tīṅku nērātatu kaṇṭatum irācamātēvi mikavum accamuṟṟu, avaḷit tāḷ
toḻutu, taṉ makaṉ mēl vitta kātalāl tāṉ piḻiyākac ceytavaṟṟip poṟukka vēṇṭiṉaḷ.
maṇimēkali avvāṟē poṟuttu, "araci, niṉmakaṉ, ceṉṟa piṟappil irākulaṉ eṉṉum
peyaruṭaṉ vāḻntu tiṭṭi viṭattāl tīṅkuṟṟu iṟantāṉ; ap pōtu avaṉporuṭṭu yāṉ uyir
tuṟantēṉ. nī yātu ceytaṉi? ippiṟappil avaṉ poruṭṭu varuntum nī muṉpiṟappil avaṉuk
kāka aḻutāyalliyē.
pūṅkoṭi nallāy! poruntātu ceytaṉi.
uṭaṟkaḻu taṉiyō? uyirkkaḻu taṉiyō?
uṭaṟkaḻu taṉiyēl uṉ makaṉ taṉṉi
eṭuttup puṟaṅkāṭ ṭiṭṭaṉar yārē!
uyirkkaḻu taṉiyēl, uyir pukum [18]pukkil
[19] ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyāl [20]terintuṇarvariyatu;
avvuyirk kaṉpiṉi yāyiṉ, āytoṭi!
evvuyirk kāyiṉum iraṅkal vēṇṭum.
niṉmakaṉ iṟattaṟkuk kāraṇamākiyatu, muṟpiṟappil maṭikkalam citittāṉeṉṟu maṭiyaṉik
koṉṟa tīviṉiyē. atuvē iṉṟu viñciyaṉ vāḷāl ivaṉ veṭṭuṇṭu vīḻac ceytatu. itaṉi
yevvāṟu aṟintāyeṉiṉ, kūṟuvēṉ, eṉṟu tāṉ malar vaṉam pukuntatu mutal ulakavaṟaviyil
teyvam urittatu īṟāka nikaḻntavi muṟṟum, tēvi neñcu koḷḷumāṟu kūṟi muṭittāḷ.
ataṉmēlum, irācamātēvi nikaḻttiya tīṅkukaḷiyum, avaṟṟil tāṉ uyntu tīrnta vakiyiyum
colli,
[21] antaram cēṟalum [22]ayaluruk kōṭalum
cintiyiṟ koṇṭilēṉ, ceṉṟa piṟaviyiṉ
[23]kātalaṟ payantōy, kaṭuntuyar kaḷintu
tītuṟu vevviṉi tīrppatu poruṭṭāl,
eṉat taṉ koḷkiyi yurittāḷ. piṉpu, kāmam, koli, poy, kaḷavu mutaliya kuṟṟaṅkaḷik
kaṭital vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli, mēlum cila aṟaṅkaḷik kūṟaluṟṟu,
[24]ceṟṟam ceṟuttōr, muṟṟa uṇarntōr;
[25] mallal mā ñālattu vāḻvō reṉpōr,
[26] allal mākkaṭku illatu nirappunar;
tiruntēr elvaḷi ! [27]cellula kaṟintōr,
varunti vantōr arumpaci kaḷintōr;
tuṉpa maṟukkum [28]tuṇiporuḷ uṇarntōr,
maṉpatik kellām aṉpoḻiyār
eṉṟa ivvaṟaṅkaḷi irācamātēvikku ōti, avaḷuṟṟa varuttat tip pōkki, maṉam teḷivaṭiyac
ceytāḷ.
[29]ñāṉa naṉṉīr naṉkaṉam teḷittut
[30] tēṉā rōti cevi mutal vārttu
[31]makaṉ tuyar neruppā maṉamviṟa kāka
akañcuṭu ventī āyiḻi yavippa,
[32]tēṟupaṭu ciṉṉīr pōlat teḷintu
[33] māṟu koṇ ṭōrā maṉattiṉa ḷāki
āṅku avaḷ toḻutalum, āyiḻi poṟāaḷ
tāṉ toḻu tēttit [34] takuti ceytili;
[35] kātalaṟ payantōy; aṉṟiyum, kāvalaṉ
māperun tēvi," eṉṟu etir vaṇaṅkiṉa ḷeṉ.
---
[1]. tōṉ ṟa - tāṉ tēviyiṉ aṭi vīḻāmikkuriya ētu tōṉṟa.
[2]. koṟṟam - aracavurimi.
[3]. ceṟṟa tevvar - pakitta pakivar.
[4]. ceruppukal maṉṉar - pōri virumpum aracar.
[5]. celvuḻic celka eṉa viḷitaliṉṟi, vaṟitē mūttu viḷital eṉa iyikka. " nōṟṟōr
maṉṟa tāmē kūṟṟam, kōḷuṟa viḷiyār piṟar koḷa viḷintōr "- (akam. 61.) viḷital -
cātal.
[6]. nāppuṭi peyarātu - nā kūṟutaṟku eḻātu. nāṇuttaka vuṭittu - nāṇum
takutiyuṭittu.
[7]. maṉpati - makkaḷ.
[8]. pikkuṇik kōlattu – pikṣuṇiyiṉ vēṭattāl.
[9]. tirintōṉ - kalaṅki vēṟupaṭṭa utayakumaraṉ.
[10]. araciyal - aracarukkuriya takuti. [11]. kiyaṟa - ceyalaṟcoḻiya.
[12]. perumpiṟi tākkum - payaṉpaṭā vakiyiṟ keṭukkum.
[13]. aṟivu - tavañāṉam.
[14]. takkaṉṟu - takkataṉṟu.
[15]. ciṟappiṉ pālār - kalvi, aṟivu, oḻukkam eṉṟa ciṟappiṉ pakuti yuṭiyavarkaḷē.
makkaḷ - putalvar.
[16]. ciṟi nōy - ciṟit tuṉpam. [17]. takikkunar - taṭuppavar.
[18]. pukkil – pukumiṭam.
[19]. ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyāl - ceyyappaṭuvaṉa vākiya iruvaki viṉi yālum.
[20]. viṉiyāl uyir pukum pukkil terintuṇartal aritu eṉka.
[21]. antarañ cēṟal - vicumpāṟākac cellutal.
[22]. ayaluru - vēṟṟuruvam.
[23] kātalaṉ - irākulaṉ. avaḷē utayakumāraṉākap piṟantāṉ. kaṭuntuyar - makaṉi yiḻanta
mikka tuṉpam.
[24]. ceṟṟam ceṟuttōr - pakimi neṭitu niṟkum ciṉa mākiya kuṟṟatti aṟac
citittavar. [25]. mallal - vaḷam.
[26]. allal mākkaḷ - tuṉpamuṟum makkaḷ. nirappunar - nirampak koṭuppavar.
[27]. cel lulaku -eṭutta uṭampiṉ nīṅkic ceṉṟaṭiyum nallulakam.
[28]. tuṇiporuḷ - ' paramārtta tattuva ñāṉam."
[29]. ñāṉa nal nīr - ñāṉamākiya nalla nīr.
[30]. tēṉārōti cevi mutal - tēṉ poruntiya kūntaliyuṭiya irācamātēviyiṉ
ceviyiṉkaṇ. tēṉārōti - aṉmoḻit toki.
[31]. makaṉi yiḻanta tuyarākiya neruppu maṉam viṟakākap paṟṟi, uḷ ḷattic cuṭṭeḻunta
koṭiya tī eṉka. āyiḻi - maṇimēkali.
[32]. tēṟupaṭu ciṉṉīr-tēṟṟāṅkoṭṭiyāl teḷivikkap peṟṟa ciṟitākiya nīr.
ciṟitākiya nīric ciṉṉīr eṉṟal marapu.
[33]. māṟu koṇṭu - pakimi koṇṭu. ōrā - teḷiyāta.
[34]. takuti - takka ceyal. [35]. kātalaṉ - utaya kumaraṉ.

24. āputtiraṉāṭu aṭinta kāti

cittirāpati yeṉṉum mutukaṇiki, taṉ cūḻcciyāl utaya kumaraṉ ulakavaṟavi pukku
uyiriḻantāṉ eṉpatu uṇarntu neñcu naṭuṅki meyvarunti, irācamātēviyi aṭintu, avaḷ
tiru vaṭiyil vīḻntu vaṇaṅkit toḻutu,
[1]toṉṟu paṭu mānakart tōṉṟiya nāḷ mutal
yāṉuṟu tuṉpam yāvarum paṭṭilar;
māperun tēvi ! mātar yāriṉum,
[2] pūvili yīttavaṉ poṉṟiṉaṉ eṉṟu,
mātavi mātavar paḷḷiyuḷ aṭintatum,
[3] parantupaṭu maṉi toṟum pāttiram ēnti
[4] araṅkak kūtticeṉṟu aiyaṅ koṇṭatum,
nakuta lallatu [5]nāṭakak kaṇikiyar
takuti yeṉṉār, taṉmi yaṉmiyiṉ;
maṉṉavaṉ makaṉē yaṉṟiyum mātarāl,
innakar uṟūum iṭukkaṇum uṇṭāl,
eṉṟu coṉṉavaḷ, muṉporukāl, cōṇāṭṭi yāṇṭa neṭu. muṭikkiḷḷi yeṉpāṉ kaṭaṟ kāṉaṟ
pūmpoḻilil pīlivaḷi yeṉṟa oru nākanāṭṭu makaḷik kaṇṭu avaḷ nalattiṟ karuttip pōkkip
pētuṟṟu niṟkiyil, avaḷ maṟintaṉaḷ; avaḷit tēṭit tirinta avaṉmuṉ, tēvacāraṇaṉ oruvaṉ
tōṉṟa, avaṉi yaracaṉ vaṇaṅkik kēṭpa, avaṉ,
kaṇṭilēṉ; āyiṉum kāriki taṉṉip
[6]paṇṭaṟi vuṭiyēṉ; [7] pārttipa ! kēḷāy.
nāka nāṭu [8]naṭukkiṉ ṟāḷpavaṉ,
[9]vāki vēlōṉ, vaḷivaṇaṉ; tēvi,
vāca mayili vayiṟṟuḷ tōṉṟiya
pīlivaḷi yeṉpōḷ; piṟanta annāḷ .
iravikulat toruvaṉ iṇi muli tōyak
[10]karuvoṭu varum eṉak [11]kaṇiyeṭut turittaṉṉ,
āṅkap putalvaṉ varūu mallatu,
pūṅkoṭi vārāḷ; [12]pulampal; itu kēḷ;
eṉa ac cāraṇaṉ mēlum collaluṟṟu, innakaril intiraviḻā āṇṭutōṟum . nikaḻtal
vēṇṭum; tavaṟiṉ innakar kaṭalāṟ koḷḷappaṭum. itu maṇimēkalā teyvattiṉ vāymi moḻi;
intiraṉatu cāpamum uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭu nīṅkiṉaṉ; kiḷḷiyum taṉ nakari yaṭintāṉ.
aṉṟu mutal iṉṟuvari ip pūmpukār nakarattār intiraviḻā eṭuttu varukiṉṟaṉar. avar
maṉattē itupaṟṟiya accam iruntukoṇṭē varukiṟatu. iṉi, maṇi peyariyuṭiyāṭku
uṇṭākiya tuṉpam kāraṇamāka varumō eṉṟu añcukiṉṟēṉ; ātalāl, am maṇimēkaliyic ciṟi
viṭuttu eṉpāl taruka eṉa vēṇṭiṉaḷ.
ivvāṟu cittirāpati vēṇṭic coṉṉatik kēṭṭa irāca mātēvi,
kaḷḷum poyyum kāmamum koliyum,
[13]uḷḷak kaḷavu meṉṟu uravōr tuṟantavi
[14] talimiyāk koṇṭaniṉ talimiyil vāḻkki
[15] pulimiyeṉ ṟañcip pōnta pūṅkoṭi
niṉṉoṭu pōntu niṉmaṉip pukutāḷ;
eṉṉō ṭirukkum
eṉṟu iyampiṉaḷ. cittirāpati ēkkamuṟṟāḷ. inniliyil maṇimēkali ciṟippaṭṭami
cutamatiyāl aṟinta mātavi aṟavaṇa vaṭikaḷi yaṭintu avari vaṇaṅki, nikaḻntatu kūṟi,
maṇimēkali yiṉ ciṟivīṭu virumpi avaruṭaṉ irācamātēvipāl vantaṉaḷ aṭikaḷ varakkaṇṭa
irācamātēvi porukkeṉa eḻuntu irukki tantu avarkku vēṇṭiya ciṟappukkaḷic ceytu,
[16]yāṇṭu pala pukka num iṇiyaṭi varunta eṉ
[17]kāṇtaku nalviṉi nummiyīṅ kaḻittatu;
[18]nāttoli villi yāyiṉum, taḷarntu
mūttatu iv yākki; vāḻkapal lāṇṭu
eṉṟu mukamaṉ moḻintāḷ. kēṭṭa aṭikaḷ, makiḻvuṟṟu,
tēvi ! kēḷāy : [19] ceytava yākkiyiṉ,
mēviṉē ṉāyiṉum [20]vīḻkatir pōṉṟēṉ :
piṟantār mūttār piṇinō yuṟṟār
iṟantār eṉki iyalpē; itukēḷ :
pētimi ceyki yuṇarvē [21]aruvuru
vāyil ūṟē nukarvē vēṭki
paṟṟē [22]pavamē [23tōṟṟam viṉippayaṉ

piṟantōr aṟiyiṉ [24]perum pēṟaṟikuvar
…. …. ….
aricaṉ tēviyoṭu āyiḻi nallīr !
[25]puritīr nallaṟam pōṟṟik kēṇmiṉ,
eṉap palarkkum potuppaṭak kūṟi, maṇimēkaliyic ciṟappāka nōkki,
maṟupiṟap puṇarnta maṇimēkali, nī
[26]piṟavaṟaṅ kēṭṭa piṉṉāḷ vantu uṉakku
ittiṟam palavum ivaṟṟiṉ pakutiyum
[27]muttēr nakiyāy! muṉṉuṟak kūṟuval
eṉṟu urittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟār. piṉpu maṇimēkali yeḻuntu, am makaḷiri vaṇaṅki, "nīṅkaḷ
itukāṟum kēṭṭa nallaṟaṅkaḷi maṟavātu kaṭippiṭittoḻukumiṉ; yāṉ iṉimēl iṅku uṟi
vēṉāyiṉ, ivaḷ utayakumāṉ iṟattaṟku ētuvāyiṉaḷ eṉpa rātaliṉ, yāṉ āputtiraṉ nāṭu
celvēṉ. ataṉpiṉ, maṇipalla vam ceṉṟu pīṭiki toḻutu, vañcikkuc ceṉṟu pattiṉik
kaṭavuṭku nallaṟam ceyvēṉ. eṉakku iṭar uṇṭāmeṉṟu nīvir iraṅkutal vēṇṭā" eṉṟu
colliviṭṭu, antimāli vantatum, ulakavaṟaviyum campāti kōyilum, kantiṟpāviyum
vaṇaṅkip paravi, vāṉvaḻiyāka, āputtiraṉ nāṭṭiyaṭintu āṅku oru poḻilakattē taṅki
iḷippāṟiṉaḷ. ciṟitu pōtil, avaḷ aṅkē vāḻnta tarumacāvakaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉ oruvaṉik
kaṇṭu,
innakarp pēr yātu? innaka rāḷum
maṉṉavaṉ yār?
eṉṟāḷ. avaṭku, am muṉivaṉ,
nākapuram itu; nalnaka rāḷvōṉ
pūmi cantiraṉ makaṉ, puṇṇiyarācaṉ :
īṅku ivaṉ piṟanta annāḷ toṭṭum
ōṅkuyar vāṉattup peyal [28]piḻippaṟiyātu;
maṇṇum [29] maraṉum vaḷampala tarūum;
[30] uṇṇiṉṟu urukkum nōy uyirkku illi
eṉṟu urittāṉ.

[1]. toṉṟupaṭu mānakar - paḻami poruntiya kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉat tiṉ kaṇ.
[2]. pūvili īttavaṉ - mātavi kūttum pāṭṭum eṉṟa kalivaki nirampip pūtta kālattu
aṉṟip pariyam koṭuttuc cērnta kōvalaṉ . mātavar paḷḷi - aṟavaṇa vaṭikaḷ uṟiyum paḷḷi.
[3]. parantu paṭu maṉi - mikap palavāy virinta maṉikaḷ,
[4. araṅkak kūtti - araṅkamēṟik kūttāṭum pāṉmiyaḷāṉa maṇi mēkali.
[5]. nāṭakak kaṇikiyar - nāṭakamāṭum kaṇiki makaḷir .
[6]. paṇṭu aṟivu uṭiyēṉ - muṉpu kaṇṭa aṟivu uṭiyēṉ; muṉpu kaṇṭuḷḷēṉ eṉpatu.
[7]. pārttipa - aracē. [8]. naṭukkiṉṟu - muṟi piṟaḻviṉṟi.
[9]. vāki vēl - veṟṟimāli cūṭiya vēl. tēvi - maṉivi. tēvi yākiya vācamayili
yeṉpavaḷ.
[10]. karuvoṭu varum - karuppamuṟṟu varuvāḷ .
[11]. kaṇi - cōtiṭaṉ. [12]. pulampal - varuntātē.
[13]. uḷḷak kaḷavu - uḷḷattāl piṟar poruḷi maṟiyiṟ koḷḷak karututal ituvum
kuṟṟameṉpatu paṭa, " uḷḷak kaḷavu" eṉṟār.
[14]. talimiyāk koṇṭa talimiyil vāḻkki - uravōr vilak kiyavaṟṟit talimip
poruḷākak koṇ ṭoḻukutal kaṭippaṭṭa 'parattimi vāḻvu.
[15]. pulimi - pulittaṉmi yuṭiyatu.
[16]. yāṇṭu pala pukka num iṇiyaṭi - yāṇṭu pala ātaliṉ mutumi yuṟṟa num aṭikaḷ
iraṇṭum. -
[17]. kāṇtaku nalviṉi - aḻakiya nalviṉiyāṉatu.
[18]. nā tolivilli - aṟam urikkum umatu nā melivaṭintatilli.
[19]. ceytava yākkiyiṉ mēviṉēṉ - tavam ceytaṟkaminta iv yākkiyoṭu kūṭi
yiruntēṉ.
[20]. vīḻ katir - maṟiyum ñāyiṟu.
[21]. aruvuru - pētimi mutal viṉippayaṉ #mukak kūṟiya paṉṉiru nītāṉaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
" aruvuru eṉpatu avvuṇarvu cārnta, uyirum uṭampumākum eṉpa." (maṇi. 30: 84-5.)
[22]. pavam-" pavameṉappaṭuvatu karumavīṭṭam, tarumuṟiyituveṉat tāntām cārtal.' (maṇi.
30: 93-4.)
[23]. tōṟṟam - piṟappu. [24] perum pēṟu - pari niruvāṇam.
[25]. puritīr - kuṟṟamillāta. kuṟṟamāvatu - piṟavikkētuvāya tuṟakka iṉpan tarutal.
[26]. piṟa aṟam - maṟṟiya camaya vaṟaṅkaḷ.
[27]. muttēr naki - muttuppōlum, pal.
[28]. piḻippaṟiyātu - peyyā toḻital iṉṟām.
[29]. maraṉ - maraṅkaḷ.
[30]. uḷ niṉṟu urukkum - uṭaṟkuḷḷēyiruntu uyiri varuttum.

25. āputtiraṉōṭu maṇipallavam aṭinta kāti

puṇṇiyarācaṉākiya āputtiraṉ taṉ urimi maṉiviyuṭaṉ maṇimēkali yaṭintirunta
poḻiliyaṭintu, āṅkē vāḻntirunta tarumacāvakaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉ aṭivaṇaṅki,
aṟaṉum [1]maṟaṉum anittamum [2]nittat
tiṟaṉum tukkamum [3]celluyirp pukkilum
[4]cārpiṉ tōṟṟamum cārpaṟut [5]tuytiyum
[6]āriyaṉ amitiyum amivuṟak kēṭṭu
varukiyil, avaṉ irunta poḻilil maṇimēkali piccippāttira (mēnti niṟpatu kaṇṭu
viyantu,
[7]peṇṇiṇi yillāp peruvaṉap puṟṟāḷ,
kaṇṇiṇi yiyakkamum [8]kāmaṉō ṭiyaṅkā,
aṅkiyiṟ pāttiram koṇṭu aṟam kēṭkum
iṅku iṇiyillāḷ ivaḷ yār eṉṉa,
arukē caṭṭiyaṇinta kāvalaṉ oruvaṉ, aracaṉi vaṇaṅki, “aracē, ivaḷi yoppār nāvalantīvil
oruvarum ilar; orukāl yāṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉuṭiya keḻutakimi vēṇṭik kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam
ceṉṟirunta kālattu yāṉ ivaḷippaṟṟi yaṟintēṉ. āṅkē ivaḷ piṟappi yuṇarntu eṉakku
aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ urit tār eṉpatit taṅkaṭku muṉpē urittuḷḷēṉ; avaḷē appati yiṉ nīṅki
ippōḻtil iṅkē vantuḷḷāḷ ” eṉṟāṉ. eṉṟatum, maṇimēkali, aracaṉi nōkki, “ aracē,
niṉkip pāttiram, eṉkip pukuntatu;
[9]maṉperuñ celvattu mayaṅkiṉi yaṟiyāy;
appiṟap paṟintili yāyiṉum, [10]āvayiṟṟu,
ippiṟap paṟintili; eṉ cey taṉiyō?
maṇippallavam valam koṇṭā lallatu,
[11]piṇippuṟu piṟaviyiṉ peṟṟiyi yaṟiyāy;
āṅku varuvāy, araca, nī"
eṉṟurittu, vāṉvaḻiyē paṭarntu pakalōṉ kuṭapāl maṟivataṟ kuḷ maṇipallavatti yaṭintāḷ.
aṅkē avaḷ, puttapīṭikiyik kaṇṭu valamvantu niṟpa, aḵtu avaṭkup paḻam piṟappuṇarvi:
nalkiṟṟu. avaḷ, appiṟappil kāyaṅkariyil kaṇṭa piramatarum muṉivaṉi muṉṉilip
paṭuttikkoṇṭu,
[12]"perumakaṉ taṉṉoṭum peyarvōrk kellām
vilaṅkum narakarum pēykaḷu mākkum
[13] kalaṅkañart tīviṉi kaṭimiṉ; kaṭintāl,
tēvarum makkaḷum [14] piramarum āku tir;
ākaliṉ nalvaṉi ayarātu ōmpumiṉ;
pulavaṉ, muḻutum poyyiṉ ṟuṇarntōṉ,
ulakuyak kōṭaṟku oruvaṉ tōṉṟum;
annāḷ avaṉ aṟam kēṭṭōr, allatu
[15] iṉṉāp piṟavi iḻukkunar illi;
[16] māṟṟarum kūṟṟam varuvataṉ muṉṉam
pōṟṟumiṉ aṟam' eṉac [17]cāṟṟik kāṭṭi
[18]nākkaṭippāka vāyppaṟi yaṟintīr !
avvuri kēṭṭu num aṭitoḻu tētta
[19]vevvuri eṅkaṭku viḷampiṉir;
aṉṟiyum, appō til, yāṉ, puttaṉ tōṉṟāmuṉpē intiraṉ ippīṭiki avaṟku iṭṭa kāraṇam
eṉṉi? eṉṟu viṉaviṉēṉ; ataṟku, puttaṉ tōṉṟiya piṉ avaṉ piṟappit tāṉ aṟintu
koḷvataṟkāka intiraṉ itaṉi amittāṉ; mēlum ippīṭiki ap puttaṉiyallatu piṟarip
poṟātu eṉṟu kūṟiyatuṭaṉ av vintiraṉē, ip pīṭikiyip puttaṉākiya
[20]perumakaṟku amittup piṟantār piṟaviyit
[21]tarumapīṭiki cāṟṟuka eṉṟē
aruḷiṉaṉ; ātaliṉ, āyiḻi piṟaviyum
[22] iruḷaṟak kāṭṭum eṉṟu eṭutturitta
vāymoḻi eṉakku iṉṟum aṉṟēpōl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu", eṉṟu kūṟip pārāṭṭi yētti
niṉṟāḷ.
iḵtiṅṅaṉamāka, maṇimēkali kūṟiyavaṟṟik kēṭṭuk kalakka muṟṟa vēntaṉ, nērē taṉ tāy
amaracuntaripāṟ ceṉṟu taṉ piṟappu varalāṟu muḻutum kēṭṭu,
iṟanta piṟaviyiṉ [23]yāycey tatū um,
piṟanta piṟaviyiṉ [24]peṟṟiyum niṉintu,
ceruvēl maṉṉar [25]cevvi pārttuṇaṅka,
aricu vīṟṟiruntu [26]puriyōrp pēṇi,
nāṭakam kaṇṭu pāṭaṟ pāṉmiyiṉ
[27]kēḷvi iṉṉici kēṭṭut tēviyar
….. …… ……
matimukak karuṅkaṇ [28]ceṅkaṭi kalakkak
karuppu villi [29]yaruppukkaṇi tūvat
[30] tarukkiya kāmak kaḷḷāṭ ṭikaḻntu
[31] tūvaṟat tuṟattal naṉṟeṉac cāṟṟit
teḷinta nātaṉ eṉ cevi mutal iṭṭa vittu
[32]ētamiṉṟāy iṉṟu viḷintatu;
maṇimēkali tāṉ kāraṇamāka,
eṉṟu taṉ araciyaṟ cuṟṟattārkkut terivittaṉaṉ. ivaṟṟik kēṭṭōruḷ, caṉamittiraṉ eṉṉum
amiccaṉ, “ maṉṉaṉ maṉam vēṟāyiṉaṉ" eṉṟu naṉkaṟintu, aracaṉi aṭivaṇaṅki,
eṅkō vāḻi; eṉ col kēṇmati :
nuṅkōṉ uṉṉip peṟuvataṉ muṉṉāḷ,
paṉṉī rāṇṭip [33]patikeḻu naṉṉāṭu maṉṉuyir
[34] maṭiya maḻivaḷam karantu. iṅku.
īṉṟāḷ kuḻavikku iraṅkāḷākit
tāṉ taṉi tiṉṉum [35]takimiya tāyatu;
kāy [36]veṅ kōṭiyil kārtōṉṟiya teṉa,
nī tōṉṟiṉiyē; nirittār aṇṇal!
[37]tōṉṟiya piṉṉart tōṉṟiya uyirkaṭku
vāṉam poyyātu; maṇvaḷam piḻiyātu;
ūṉuṭi yuyirkaḷ uṟupaci aṟiyā;
nī oḻikāli, niṉnā ṭellām,
[38]tāy oḻi kuḻavi pōlak kūun;
[39] tuyar nili yulakam kāttaliṉṟi, nī
[40]uyarnili yulakam vēṭṭaṉi yāyiṉ,
iṟuti uyirkaḷ eytavum, iṟiva !
[41]peṟuti virumpiṉi yākuvi yaṉṟē?
taṉṉuyirk kiraṅkāṉ, piṟavuyi rōmpum
maṉṉuyir mutalvaṉ aṟamumītu aṉṟāl;
[42]matimāṟu ōrntaṉi, maṉṉava!
eṉṟu eṭutturittāṉ. itaṉālum, aracaṉ eṇṇam ciṟitum māṟātāyiṟṟu. ākavē, avaṉ,
maṇipallavam valam koḷḷutaṟ keḻunta
[43] taṇiyā vēṭki taṇittaṟku aritāl;
aracum urimiyum akanakarc [44]cuṟṟamum
orumati elli kāttal niṉ kaṭaṉ,
eṉṟu kūṟi viṭipeṟṟuc ceṉṟu, vaṅkamēṟi maṇipallavam ceṉṟu cērntāṉ. avaṉi
maṇimēkali etirkoṇṭaḻittuc ceṉṟu, at tīvakattuc cōliyi valamvantu puttapīṭikiyik
kāṭṭiṉaḷ. vēntaṉ ataṉikkaṇṭu valam vantu paṇintuniṟpa, ap pīṭiki, avaṉatu paḻampiṟappiṉ
nikaḻcciyi,
kiyakat teṭuttuk kāṇpōr mukatti
miyaṟu maṇṭilam pōlak kāṭṭa, aṟinta anta vēntaṉ,
eṉ piṟap paṟintēṉ; eṉ iṭar tīrntēṉ;
teṉ tamiḻ [45]maturic ceḻuṅkalip pāvāy!
māri naṭunāḷ [46] vayiṟukāy paciyāl
āriru ḷañcātu ampalamaṇintu āṅku
[47]irantūṇ vāḻkki eṉpāl vantōrkku
[48]aruntūṇ kāṇā taḻuṅkuvēṉ kiyil
nāṭuvaṟaṅ kūriṉum ivvōṭuvaṟaṅ kūrātu;
ēṭā! aḻiyal; eḻuntu itu koḷka eṉa
amuta curapi aṅkiyil tantu eṉ
[49] pavam aṟuvitta vāṉōr pāvāy!
uṇarvil tōṉṟi uripporuḷ uṇarttum
[50]maṇitikaḻ aviroḷi maṭanti niṉṉaṭi,
tēvarāyiṉum [51] pirama rāyiṉum
[52]nā mācu kaḻūum nalamkiḷar tiruntaṭi,
piṟanta piṟavikaḷ [53]pēṇuta lallatu,
maṟantu vāḻēṉ, maṭanti!
eṉac cintātēviyi muṉṉilippaṭuttu vaṇaṅki, maṇimēkali yuṭaṉ, ap pīṭikikkut
teṉmēṟkil uḷḷa kōmuki yeṉṉum poykik karikkuc ceṉṟu, oru puṉṉimarattiṉ nīḻalil
iruntaṉaṉ. ivviruvaratu varavum, puṉṉiniḻalil porunti yiruppatum uṇarnta tīvatilaki
yeṉpāḷ, avar muṉ pōntu, aracaṉic ciṟappāka nōkki,
"aruntuyir maruntu muṉ aṅkiyiṟ koṇṭu
peruntuyar tīrtta apperiyōy ! vantaṉi;
annāḷ niṉṉi [54]yayarttup pōyiṉar,
piṉ nāḷ vantu niṉ [55] peṟṟimi nōkki,
[56]niṉkuṟi yiruntu tammuyir nīttōr,
oṉpatu ceṭṭikaḷ uṭaleṉpu ivikāṇ;
āṅkavar iṭavuṇṭu avaruṭaṉ vantōr,
ēṅki [57]meyvittōr eṉpum, ivikāṇ;
[58]ūrtiri tokutta uyarmaṇal putippa,
[59]āymalarp puṉṉi yaṇiniḻal kīḻāl
aṉpuṭi āruyir aracaṟ [60]karuḷiya
eṉpuṭi yākki iruntatu, kāṇāy,
[61]niṉṉuyir koṉṟāy; niṉṉuyirk kiraṅkip
piṉṉāḷ vanta piṟaruyir[62] koṉṟāy;
kolivaṉ alliyō ! koṟṟava ṉāyiṉi !"
eṉṟivvāṟu puṇṇiyarācaṉukkuc colli vantavaḷ, piṉpu maṇi 'mēkaliyi nōkki,
"palar toḻupāttiram kiyiṉ ēntiya
[63]maṭavaral nallāy! niṉ ṟaṉ mānakar,
kaṭalvayiṟu pukkatu; kāraṇam kēḷāy"
eṉṟu toṭaṅki, “ nākanāṭṭaracaṉ pīlivaḷi yeṉpāḷ, cōḻa ṉukkup piṟanta taṉmakaṉuṭaṉ
ittīvakam pōntu pīṭikiyi valaṅ koṇ ṭēttiniṟpa, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattiṟkuc cellum kam
paḷac ceṭṭiyiṉ vaṅkam vantu niṟkak kaṇṭu avaṉpāl makaṉit tantu, aracaṉpāl
cērppikkumāṟu koṭuttaṉaḷ. ac ceṭṭi avvāṟē koṇṭu celluṅkāl, avaṉatu marakkalam
kaṭalil aṉṟu iravē kaviḻntu viṭṭatu. itaṉi yavaṉ tappic ceṉṟu, aracaṟ kuṇartta, avaṉ
makaṉi yiḻanta tuyaram poṟātu, kāṉalum, kaṭalum, kaṭaṟkariyum tēṭit tirintaṉaṉ. ataṉāl,
annakaram intira viḻā āṟṟātu maṟantoḻintatu. uṭaṉē, maṇimēkalā teyvam an nakarattik
kaṭal koḷḷumāṟu cāpamiṭṭāḷ; ataṉāl atu kaṭaṟku iriyāyiṟṟu; aracaṉāṉa
neṭumuṭikkiḷḷiyum taṉippaṭṭup pōyiṉaṉ eṉṟu colli, ic ceytiyit taṉakku at
teyvam kūṟiyatākavum terivittu, in nikaḻcciyil, " niṉ tāyarum aṟavaṇa vaṭikaḷuṭaṉ
vañcimānakar ceṉṟaṭintaṉar; iṉi, niṉkula mutal vaṉ oruvaṉi am maṇimēkalā teyvam
muṉṉāḷil ātaravu cey taḷittatum, avaṉ piṉpu aṟavaracāṇṭatum piṟavum aṟavaṇaṉpāl
kēṭṭaṟikuvi" eṉṟu terivittuviṭṭu, attīvatilakiyum avarkaḷi viṭṭu nīṅkiṉaḷ.
piṟaku, puṇṇiyarācaṉ maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ taṉ muṉpiṟappiṉ uṭampu putiyuṇṭirunta
iṭamaṇuki, akaḻntu nōkka, aṅkē, ataṉ eṉpukkūṭu tōṉṟakkaṇṭu maṉaṅ kalaṅkiṉaṉ. avaṉi
maṇimēkali pārttu,
"eṉṉuṟ ṟaṉiyō ! [64]ilaṅku itaḻttārōy!
niṉṉāṭu aṭintuyāṉ niṉṉi īṅkaḻittatu,
maṉṉā ! niṉṟaṉ maṟupiṟap puṇartti,
[65]antarat tīviṉum akaṉ perun [66]tīviṉum
niṉpeyar [67] niṟutta; nīḷnila māḷum,
aracar tāmē aruḷaṟam pūṇṭāl,
poruḷu muṇṭō piṟapuri[68] tīrttaṟku?
aṟameṉap paṭuvatu yāteṉak kēṭpiṉ,
maṟavātu itukēḷ : maṉṉuyirk kellām,
uṇṭiyum uṭiyum uṟiyuḷu mallatu,
kaṇṭa tu il,"
eṉak kaṭṭurittāḷ. itik kēṭṭa av vēntaṉ,
"eṉ nāṭ ṭāyiṉum piṟar nāṭṭāyiṉum,
naṉṉutal ! uritta nallaṟam ceykēṉ :
eṉ piṟap puṇartti eṉṉi nī [69] paṭittaṉi;
[70]niṉ tiṟam nīṅka lāṟṟēṉ yāṉ ",
eṉṟāṉāka, iccoṟkaḷik kēṭṭatum, maṇimēkali muṟuvalittu,
[71]"puṉkaṇ koḷḷal; nī pōntataṟ kiraṅki, niṉ
maṉperu nāṭu [72]vāyeṭut taḻikkum;
[732]vaṅkat tēkuti; vañciyuṭ celval",
eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭut tāṉ vāṉvaḻiyāka vañci mānakarkkuc ceṉṟāḷ.

26. vañcimā nakar pukka kāti

tāy kaṇṇakiyiyum, tanti kōvalaṉiyam tāṉ kāṇa vēṇṭumeṉṟu eḻunta vēṭki maṇimēkali
yuḷḷatti yalippa, avaḷ vañci nakariṉ puṟattē, avarkaḷuṭiya paṭimam niṟuviya kōyiṟkuḷ
nuḻintu taṉ vēṭki tīra,
vaṇaṅki niṉṟu [1]kuṇam pala ētti,
[2]“aṟpukkaṭaṉ nillātu, naṟṟavam paṭarātu,
kaṟpuk kaṭaṉ pūṇṭu num [3]kaṭaṉ muṭittatu
aruḷal vēṇṭum,"
eṉṟu aḻutu, kaṇṇakip paṭimattiṉ muṉ niṉṟāḷ. appōtu, kaṇṇaki,
"em [4]iṟikkuṟṟa iṭukkaṇ poṟātu,
[5]vemmiyiṉ maturi vevvaḻaṟ paṭunāḷ
maturāpati yeṉum māperun teyvam,
itu nīr muṉ cey viṉiyiṉ payaṉāl;
eṉṟu tam paḻampiṟappil nikaḻnta nikaḻcciyi viḷaṅkavurittu, appiṟappil, taṉ kaṇavaṉ
caṅkamaṉip parataṉ eṉpāṉ paṟṟik koli purivittāṉāka, avaṉ maṉivi nīli eṉpāḷ iṭṭa
cāpamē it tuṉpattukku ētuvāyiṟṟu eṉa am maturāpati vaṟpuṟuttic colliyum,
cīṟṟam koṇṭu ceḻunakar citittēṉ;
mēṟcey nalviṉi yiṉ [6] viṇṇavarc ceṉṟēm;
avviṉi yiṟutiyiṉ [7]aṭuciṉap pāvam,
evvaki yāṉum eytuta loḻiyātu;
[8]umpar ilvaḻi impariṟ [9]palpiṟappu
yāṅkaṇum iruviṉi yuyttu umippōla,
nīṅkarum piṟavik kaṭaliṭi nīntip
piṟantum iṟantum [10]uḻalvōm; piṉṉar,
maṟantum maḻimaṟā makata naṉṉāṭṭukku
oruperun [11]tilakameṉṟu uravōr urikkum
[12] karavarum perumik tapiliyam patiyil,
aḷapparum [13] pāramiti aḷaviṉṟu niṟittut
[14] tuḷakkamil putta ñāyiṟu tōṉṟi,
[15]pōti mūlam porunti vantaruḷi.....
eṇṇarum [16] cakkaravāḷam eṅkaṇum
aṇṇal [17] aṟakkatir virikkuṅkāli ....
tuṉpak katiyil [18] tōṟṟara viṉṟi,
aṉpuṟu maṉattōṭu avaṉ aṟam kēṭṭut
[19]tuṟavi yuḷḷam tōṉṟit toṭarum
[20] piṟavi nītta peṟṟiya mākuvam;
attiṟa māyiṉum anēka kālam
ettiṟat tārkkum [21] iruttiyum ceykuvam."
ivvāṟu tām ceytaṉavum ceyyaviruppaṉavum kūṟiya kaṇṇakiyār, mēlē maṇimēkalikkuc cila
kūṟaluṟṟu,
[22-naṟikamaḻ kūntal naṅki ! nīyum,
muṟimiyiṉ inta mūtūrakattē,
avvavar camayattu [22]aṟiporuḷ kēṭṭu,
[23] meyvaki yiṉmi niṉakkē viḷaṅkiya
piṉṉarp periyōr [24]piṭakaneṟi kaṭavāy,
iṉṉatu ivviyalpu,"
eṉa eṭutturittu, mēlum, "nī iḷiyaḷ vaḷiyaḷ eṉṟu karuti niṉakku yāvarum
meypporuḷi yuṇartta muṉvārār; ātaliṉ, nī vēṟṟuruk koḷka" eṉṟu paṇittaṉuppiṉaḷ.
maṇimēkaliyum oru mā tavaṉ vaṭivu koṇṭāḷ.
mātavaṉ vaṭiviṟ puṟappaṭṭa maṇimēkali,
[25]tēvakulamum [26]teṟṟiyum paḷḷiyum
pūmalarp poḻilum poykiyum [27]miṭintu,
naṟṟava muṉivarum [28]kaṟṟaṭaṅkiṉarum,
naṉṉeṟi kāṇiya [29]toṉṉūṟ pulavarum,
eṅkaṇum viḷaṅkiya [30] eyiṟ puṟavirukkiyiṉ
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉum ceṅkōl vēntaṉ
iruntu aracupuriyum vañcimā nakarkkuṭ pukuntaṉaḷ.
----
[1]. kuṇam pala ētti - kuṇaṅkaḷip palappaṭac collip paravi.
[2]. aṉpuk kaṭaṉ - kaṇavaṉ iṟappiṉ uṭaṉ iṟattalum, mūḻ kalum. tavam - kimmi nōṉpu.
[3]. kaṭaṉ muṭittatu - maturiyi erittuk kaṇavaṉik kūṭiyatu.
[4]. iṟi - kaṇa vaṉāṉa kōvalaṉ. [5]. vemmiyiṉ - vekuḷit tīyāl.
[6]. viṇṇavarc ceṉṟēm - viṇṇavarāki, viṇṇulaku ceṉṟēm.
[7]. aṭu ciṉap pāvam - maturiyi erittaṟku ētuvāyirunta vekuḷi yāl tōṉṟiya
pāvam.
[8]. umpar il vaḻi - viṇṇulakattē nalviṉi nukartal illā viṭattu.
[9]. pal piṟappu yāṅkaṇum - ellāp piṟappiṉum.
[10]. uḻalvēm-varuntuvōm.
[11]. tilakam'- mōṉatu, uravōr - aṟivuṭiyōr.
[12]. karavarum perumi - maṟikka muṭiyāta perumi.
[13]. - pāramiti - --ṭampu, uṟuppu, poruḷ mutaliyaṉa kuṟivāp peṟṟut tāṉ
mutaliya pattum uṭiya rātal. tāṉam, cīlam, poṟi, vīriyam, tiyāṉam, uṇarcci, upāyam,
aruḷ, valimi, ñāṉam eṉṟa ippattum tacapāramiki eṉappaṭum.
aḷaviṉṟu - kuṟiviṉṟi.
[14]. tuḷakkamil - naṭukkamillāta. [15]. pōtimūlam - aracamaram.
[16]. cakkaravāḷam - ulakam muppattāṟum taṉṉuḷ aṭakkiyakōḷam.
[17]. aṟakkatir - aṟamākiya oḷikkatir. [18]. tōṟṟarvu - piṟattal.
[19]. uḷḷam tuṟavi tōṉṟi - uḷḷattē tuṟavu uṇarvu tōṉṟu talāl.
[20]. piṟavi nītta peṟṟiyam - piṟavittoṭarpaṟṟa niruvāṇameyti yavarākuvam.
[21]. irutti - citti. [22]. naṟi - tēṉ.
[22]. aṟiporuḷ - aṟitaṟkuriya camaya nūṟporuḷ.
[23]. meyvaki yiṉmi - avi poyyāntaṉmi.
[24]. piṭakaneṟi - puttaṉaruḷiya ākamamākiya piṭaka nūl neṟi. ip piṭakam, cuttam,
viṉayam, apitaṉ mam eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
[25]. tēvakulam - kōyil. [26]. teṟṟi - mēṭi; tiṇṇiyumām. ¨
[27]. miṭintu - ceṟintu.
[28]. kaṟṟu aṭaṅkiṉar - kaṟṟaṟkuriyavaṟṟik kaṟṟu, avaṟṟuṭ kaṇṭa taṟkut taka niṟpavar.
[29]. toṉṉūṟpulavar - paḻiya nūlkaḷik kaṟṟuṇarnta pulavar.
[30]. eyiṟpuṟa virukki - puṟamatil irukki.

27. camayak kaṇakkar tam tiṟam kēṭṭa kāti

vañci nakarkkuṭ ceṉṟa maṇimēkali aṅkē irunta camayak kaṇakkar palariyum kaṇṭu avaravar
camayattiṟaṅkaḷik kēṭkalā ṉāḷ, avaruḷ vitika mārkkattu aḷavi vātiyi nōkki, " niṉ
kaṭippiṭiyātu?" eṉa viṉava, avaṉ,
vētavi yātaṉum kiruta kōṭiyum
[1]ētamil cimiṉi eṉum ivvāciriyar
pattum eṭṭum āṟum paṇpuṟat ta
ttam vakiyāl tām pakarn tiṭṭaṉar;
kāṇṭa l, karutal, uvamam, ākamam,
[2]āṇṭiya aruttā pattiyōṭu iyalpu,
aitikam, [3] apāvam, mīṭci, oḻivu,aṟivu
[4]eyti uṇṭāmneṟi eṉṟu ivi tammāl
poruḷiṉ uṇmi [5]pulaṅkoḷal vēṇṭum eṉṟum
ivi yovvoṉṟiṉ ilakkaṇamum muṟiyē kūṟi, piramāṇā pācaṅkaḷ eṭṭu uḷa eṉṟum, avi,
[6] cuṭṭuṇarvoṭu, tiriyakkōṭal, aiyam,
tērātu teḷital, kaṇṭuṇarāmi,
eytum ilvaḻakku, uṇarntati yuṇartal,
niṉippu eṉa nikaḻva
eṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpukaḷiyum teriyak kūṟiṉaṉ. ataṉ mēlum, avaṉē,
[7] pāṅkuṟum ulōkāyatamē, puttam, -
cāṅkiyam, niyā yikam vicēṭikam,
mīmāñ cakamām : camayavāciriyar
tām, pirukaṟpati, ciṉaṉē, kapilaṉ,
akkapātaṉ, kaṇātaṉ, cimiṉi;
meyp pirattiyam, aṉumāṉam, cāttam,
uvamāṉam, aruttā patti, aṭāvam
[*]iviyē ippōtu iyaṉṟuḷa aḷavikaḷ
eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. avaṉi nīṅkiyatum, īcaṉē iṟivaṉ eṉak koḷḷum civavāti maṇimēkali muṉ
nērpaṭṭāṉ. avaṉi viṉaviya maṇimēkalikku, avaṉ,
[8] irucuṭarōṭu iyamāṉaṉ aimpūta meṉṟu
eṭṭuvakiyum uyirum yākkiyu māyk
[9] kaṭṭi niṟpōṉum [10]kaliyuruvi ṉōṉum
paṭittu viḷiyāṭum paṇpi ṉōṉum
[11] tuṭittut tuyar tīr tōṟṟat tōṉum
taṉṉil vēṟutāṉ oṉṟilōṉum
aṉṉōṉ iṟivaṉākum,
eṉṟāṉ; piṟaku, piramavāti tōṉṟi, “ pērulakamellām tēvaṉ iṭṭa muṭṭiyākum "
eṉṟāṉ; viṇavavāti, " kaṭalvaṇaṉ, nāraṇaṉē iṟivaṉ " eṉṟāṉāka, vētavāti mēlvantu colla
luṟṟāṉ :
kaṟpam ki, cantamkāl, eṇ kaṇ,
teṟṟeṉ niruttam cevi, cikki mūkku,
uṟṟa viyākaraṇam mukam peṟṟuc
----------
[*] camayam āciriyar aḷavi
'ulōkāyatam - pirukaṟpati pirattiyakkam
puttam - ciṉaṉ pirattiyakkamum aṉumāṉamum cāṅkiyam - kapilaṉ pirattiyakkamum
aṉumāṉamum ākamamum
ivvāṟē piṟavaṟṟiyum ōṭṭikkoḷka. cāttam - ākamam
----
[12]cārpil tōṉṟā āraṇa [13]vētakku
ātiyantam illi; atu neṟi,
eṉṟa avvētiyaṉ uri, meyttiṟam, vaḻakku eṉa varikkum evvakiyālum icivataṉṟu eṉa
uṭkoṇṭa maṇimēkali, āṅkē tōṉṟiya ācīvakaṉik kaṇṭu, "niṉ iṟivaṉ yāvaṉ? niṉ nūṟporuḷ
yātu ?" eṉa viṉaviṉaḷ. avaṉ,
elliyil poruḷkaḷil eṅkum eppoḻutum,
[14]pullik kiṭantu pulappaṭukiṉṟa
[15]varampil aṟivaṉ iṟi; nūṟporuḷkaḷ aintu,
eṉṟu kūṟi, avvintum, uyirum, nilam, nīr, tī, kāṟṟu eṉum nālvaki yaṇukkaḷumām
eṉṟu vakuttum,
avvaṇu uṟṟum kaṇṭum uṇarttiṭap
[16]peyvaki kūṭip pirivatum ceyyum :
nilam nīr tīk kāṟṟeṉa nāl vakiyiṉa
malimaram uṭampeṉat tiraḷvatum ceyyum :
vevvēṟāki virivatum ceyyum;
avvaki yaṟivatu uyireṉappaṭumē
eṉa uyiriyum, ivvāṟē ēṉi nālvaki yaṇukkaḷiyum virittum kūṟiṉaṉ. aṅṅaṉam
kūṟumiṭattu, aṇukkaḷiṉ potuvi yalpi,
tītuṟṟu yāvatum citivatu [17]ceyyā;
putitāyp piṟantoṉṟu oṉṟiṟ pukutā; mu
tu nīraṇu nilavaṇuvāyt tiriyā;
oṉṟu iraṇṭākip piḷappatum ceyyā;
aṉṟiyum [18]avalpōl parappatum ceyyā,
eṉṟum, uyirkaḷ ceṉṟeytum piṟappum vīṭupēṟum kūṟaluṟṟu,
[19]karumm piṟappum karunīlap piṟappum
pacumm piṟappum cemm piṟappum
poṉṉ piṟappum veṇṇ piṟappum
eṉṟu ivvāṟu piṟappiṉum mēvip
paṇpuṟu variciyiṟ [20]pāṟpaṭṭup piṟantōr,
[21]kaḻiveṇ piṟappiṟ kalantu vīṭaṇukuvar
eṉṟum, uyirkaḷ,
peṟutalum ikaḻtalum iṭiyū ṟuṟutalum,
[22] uṟumiṭat teytalum tukkacuka muṟutalum,
[23]peritavi nīṅkalum piṟattalum cātalum
[24] karuviṟ paṭṭa poḻutē kalakkum;
iṉpamum tuṉpamum iviyum aṇuveṉat takum;
muṉṉuḷa ūḻē piṉṉum uṟuvippatu
maṟkali nūliṉ vaki itu"
eṉṟum kūṟi muṭittāṉ. avaṉi viṭṭu, nikaṇṭavātiyi nōkki, maṇimēkali, "niṉṉāl
pukaḻum talivaṉ yār ? niṉ nūṟporuḷ yāvi?” eṉṟāḷ. nikaṇṭavāti,
intirar toḻappaṭum [25]iṟivaṉ em iṟivaṉ;
tanta nūṟporuḷ [26]taṉ māttikāyamum,
[27]ataṉmāttikāyamum kālā [28]kāyamum
tītil cīvaṉum paramāṇukkaḷum
nalviṉiyum tīviṉiyum avviṉiyāl
ceyvuṟu [29]pantamum vīṭum ittiṟatta
eṉat taṉ nūṟporuḷit tokuttum, piṉpu ovvoṉṟiyum virittum kūṟi muṭivil, vīṭu
pēṟṟiyalpi,
cīrcāl nalviṉi tīviṉi yavi ceyum
varuvaḻi yiraṇṭiyum māṟṟi muṉ cey
aruviṉip payaṉ aṉupavit [30]taṟuttiṭutal;
atu vīṭākum,
eṉṟaṉaṉ. avaṉpiṉ cāṅkiyaṉ tōṉṟit taṉ cāṅkiya nūṟ karutti yurikkat toṭaṅki,
taṉiyaṟivaritāyt tāṉ mukkuṇamāy
maṉanikaḻ viṉṟi [31] māṇpami potuvāy
ellāp poruḷum tōṉṟutaṟ kiṭameṉac
collutal mūlap pakuti
eṉṟum, cittam, māṉ, putti, vāṉ, kāṟṟu, tī, nīr, maṇ eṉṟa ivi oṉṟiliruntu oṉṟu
veḷippaṭum eṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ kūṭṭattiliruntu maṉamum, maṉattu āṅkāramum, kaṉmēntiriya
ñāṉēntiriya vikāraṅkaḷum tōṉṟi ulakāy nikaḻum eṉṟum,
vanta vaḻiyē ivi ceṉ ṟaṭaṅki
antamil piraḷayamāy [32]iṟumaḷavum
oṉṟāy eṅkum parantu nittiyamāy
aṟitaṟ keḷitāy mukkuṇa maṉṟip
[33]poṟiyuṇarvikkum potuvu maṉṟi
epporuḷum tōṉṟutaṟ kiṭamiṉṟi
apporuḷellām aṟintiṭaṟku uṇarvāy
oṉṟāy eṅkum parantu nittiyamāy
niṉṟuḷa uṇarvāy nikaḻtarum puruṭaṉ
[34]pulamār poruḷkaḷ irupat tintuḷa
eṉṟum, avi nilam mutalāka uyireṉum āṉmā īṟāka irupat tintum niralē kūṟi
muṭittāṉ. maṇimēkali, uṭaṉē arukil niṉṟa vicēṭikaṉi "niṉ vaḻakkiṉi urippāyāka,"
eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ. anta vicēṭikaṉ, poruḷ, kuṇam, toḻil, cāmāṉi yam, vicēṭam, camavāyam
eṉa nūṟporuḷ āṟākum eṉṟum
avaṟṟuḷ,
poruḷ eṉpatu,
kuṇamum toḻilu muṭittāy, ettokip
poruḷukkum ētuvām; apporuḷ oṉpatām;
ñālam, nīr, tī, vaḷi, ākāyam, tici,
kālam. āṉmā, maṉam; ivaṟṟuḷ nilam,
oli ūṟu niṟam cuvi nāṟṟamoṭu aintum
payil kuṇam uṭittu; niṉṟa nāṉkum
[35]cuvi mutal orō kuṇam avikuṟi vuṭiya,
eṉa ovvoṉṟiṉ iyalpukaḷiyum virittuk kūṟiṉaṉ. avaṉpiṉ, pūtavāti tōṉṟi,
[36]tātakip pūvum kaṭṭiyum iṭṭu
maṟṟum kūṭṭa [37]matukkaḷi piṟantāṅku
uṟṟiṭum pūtattu uṇarvu tōṉṟiṭum;
avvuṇarvu avvappūtat taḻivukaḷiṉ
vevvēṟu piriyum; [38] paṟiyōciyiṟ keṭum;
uyiroṭum kūṭṭiya uṇarvuṭip pūtamum,
uyirillāta uṇarvil pūtamum
avvap pūtavaḻi avi piṟakkum;
[39]meyvaki ituvē;
eṉṟum, yām kāṭciyaḷavi yaṉṟik karuttu mutaliya aḷavikaḷik koḷḷēm eṉṟum, ip
piṟappē, immiyum immip payaṉu mām; maṟumiyum viṉippayaṉ tuyttal uṇṭu eṉpatum
veṟum poyyē eṉṟum kūṟiṉaṉ. ivaṟṟik kēṭṭu maṇimēkali māṟu vēṟu kūṟātu
nakittaṉaḷ; avaṉ, nakippatu eṉṉi ? eṉṟāṉāka, tāṉ paḻam piṟappuṇarnta ceyti kūṟiṉaḷ.
ataṉik kēṭṭatum
avaṉ nakittu,
[40]teyvamayakkiṉum kaṉāvuṟu tiṟattiṉum
[41]miyaluṟuvār maṉam vēṟām vaki
aiyamaṉṟi illi,
eṉṟāṉ. avaṉi maṇimēkali nōkki, "kāṭciyē yaḷaviyāyiṉ
niṉ
tanti tāyari aṉumāṉattāl alatu
inta ñālattu evvaki aṟivāy?
meyyuṇar viṉṟi meyp poruḷ uṇarvariya
aiyamallatu itu collap peṟāy,
eṉat tāṉ koṇṭirunta mātavaṉ vaṭivilē niṉṟu tāṉ uṉṉiya poruḷi urittu, iṅkē
kāṭṭiya camaya nūṟporuḷ kaḷi aṟintāḷ.

[1]. ētam il - kuṟṟayillāta.
[2. āṇṭiya aruttāpatti - īṇṭuk kūṟiyatoṉṟikkoṇṭu āṇṭu uḷḷati yaṟitaṟkuriya
aruttāpattiyaḷavi.
[3]. apāvam - iṉmi . [4]. eytiyuṇṭām neṟi - campavam.
[5]. pulaṅkoḷal - aṟital.
[6]. cuṭṭuṇarvu - uḷḷa taṉ uṇmiyi yuḷḷavāṟu kāṇṭal.
[7]. pāṅku uṟu - pakutikaḷāṉa.
[8]. irucuṭar - ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum. iyamāṉaṉ - uyir.
[9]. kaṭṭi niṟpōṉ - camittu niṟpavaṉ.
[10]. kaliyuruviṉōṉ - kalikaḷi uruvāka uṭiyavaṉ.
[11]. tuṭittu - aḻittu. tuyar - piṟaviyāl uḷatākum iḷippu.
[12]. cārpil tōṉṟā - ōriṭattil tōṉṟāta.
[13]. vētakku - vētattukku. āraṇattukku aṅkam āṟu uṇṭu : avi, kaṟpam. cantam,
niruttam, cikki, kaṇitam, ilakkaṇam, "vēta mākiya puruṭaṉukkuk kaṟpam ki, cantam kāl,
kaṇitam kaṇ, niruttam cevi, cikki mūkku, ilakkaṇam mukam, " eṉpar.
[14]. pullikkiṭantu - porunti yiruntu.
[15]. varampil aṟivaṉ - elliyillāta aṟivuṭiya maṟkali tēvaṉ.
[16]. peyvaki cu.ṭi. - peyyum kūṟupāṭṭāl nilavaṇu mutaliya nāl vaki yaṇukkaḷōṭum
kūṭi.
[17]. citivatu ceyyā - aḻiyā.
[18]. avalpōl - avalippōla.
[19]. karumpiṟappu mutalākak kūṟuvaṉa piṟappuvaki. ivi muṟiyē oṉ ṟiṉoṉṟu ciṟantaṉa.
karumpiṟappu. karunīlappiṟappu. pacumpiṟappu. cempiṟappu, poṉ piṟappu, veṇ piṟappu
eṉa varuvaṉa.
[20]. pāṟpaṭṭu - pakutikkaṇ uṟṟu.
[21]. kaḻiveṇ piṟappu - mika veṇmiyāṉa ( tūya) piṟappu.
[22]. uṟumiṭattu - eytu taṟkuriya viṭattu.
[23]. avi peritu nīṅkal - avaṟṟiṉ muḻutum nīṅkal.
[24]. karuviṟ paṭṭapoḻutē - karuppattilē karuvākiyapōtē.
[25]. iṟivaṉ - arukaṉ. intirar - nūṟuvaki intirar.
[26]. taṉmāttikāyam - eṅkum uḷatāki eññāṉṟum poruḷkaḷi naṭattuvatu (maṇi. 27:
187-8).
[27]. ataṉmāttikāyam - poruḷkaḷaṉittiyum niṟkacceyvatu.
[28]. kālākāyam-kuṟiyatum neṭiyatumāya viṉi nikaḻcci nikaḻvippatu.
[29]. pantam - kaṭṭu.
[30]. aṟuttiṭutal - toṭarātavāṟu ceytu koḷḷutal.
[31]. māṇpu ami potuvāy - māṭcimippaṭa aminta cāmā ṉiya tattuvamāy.
[32]. iṟumaḷavum - aḻiyuṅkāṟum.
[33]. poṟiyuṇarvikkum potu - poṟikaṭku viṭayamākum cāmā" ṉiyattaṉmi ."
[34], pulamār poruḷ - tattuvapporuḷ.
[35]. cattam mutal nāṟṟam iṟutiyākiya aintaṉuḷ nīr cattam mutaliya nāṉkum. tī cattam
mutaliya mūṉṟum. kāṟṟu cattam mutaliya iraṇṭum. viṇ cattam oṉ ṟum kuṇamākak koṇṭaṉa
eṉpatu.
[36]. tātakippū - āttippū. kaṭṭi - karuppukkaṭṭi. maṟṟum - āvā rampaṭṭi (āviram
paṭṭi) vēlaṅkoḻuntu mutaliyaṉa.
[37]. matukkaḷi - matuviṉiṭattē kaḷippu. piṟantāṅku - piṟantatu. pōla,
[38]. paṟiyōciyiṉ - paṟiyōci niṟutta niṟpatupōla.
[39]. meyvaki - meynnūṟ kūṟum poruṭ kūṟupāṭu.
[40]. teyvamayakku - teyvamēṟutal,
[41]. miyaluṟuvār, aṟivu mayaṅkuvōr.
------------

28. kaccimā nakar pukka kāti

iṉi, avvañci mānakarattē maṇimēkali taṉ tāyariyum aṟa vaṇavaṭikaḷiyum kāṇa virumpi
avarit tēṭikkoṇṭu puṟañcēri kaṭantu, kalavi nīrum, cāntukaḻi nīrum, virinīrum eṉṟa
palavaki nīrē eṅkum pāytalāl, tāmari, kaḻunīr, āmpal mutaliya pūkkaḷ malā, vaṇṭāṟṟa
viḷaṅkiya akaḻiyiyum, palvakip poṟiyamittu, viṉi māṇpu ciṟantu niṉṟa matiliyum,
kaṭantu ceṉṟu, koṭinilavum vāyil vaḻiyāka nakarkkuḷḷē ceṉṟaṉaḷ. aṅkē, kāvalāḷar, mīṉ
viliñar, uppuppakarukar, kaḷviṟpōr, [1] kāḻiyar, [2]kūviyar eṉpavar mutalākap pala
toḻilāḷarum vaṇikarum nirampak kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. avar kūṭṭattik kaṭantu celpavaḷ, kaṇṭu
perumakiḻvu koṇṭu, intira vikāram pōl eḻilpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiya oru mātavap paḷḷiyik
kaṇṭāḷ.
nalantaru paṇṇum [3]tiṟaṉum vāyppa
[4]nilam kalam kaṇṭam nikaḻak kāṭṭum
pāṇar eṉṟivar palvaki maṟukum,
[5]vilaṅkaram porūum [6]veḷvaḷi pōḻnarōṭu
ilaṅku maṇi viṉiñar [7] irīiya maṟukum,
[8]vēttiyal potuviyal eṉṟiv viraṇṭiṉ
kūttiyalpu aṟinta kūttiyar maṟukum,
[9]pālvēṟāka eṇvakip paṭṭa
kūlam [10]kuvi iya kūlamaṟukum,
[11]mākatar cūtar vētāḷikar maṟukum,
pōkam purakkum potuvar mali maṟukum,
[12] kaṇ nuḻikallā nuṇṇūṟ kiviṉi,
vaṇṇa aṟuviyar vaḷam tikaḻ maṟukum,
poṉṉuri kāṇpōr naṉmaṉi maṟukum,
paṉmaṇi pakarvōr [13]maṉṉiya maṟukum,
maṟiyōr [14]aruntoḻil kuṟiyā maṟukum,
araciyal maṟukum, amicciyal maṟukum,
eṉip peruntoḻil cey ēṉōr maṟukum,
[15]maṉṟamum, [16]potiyilum, cantiyum, [17]catukkamum,
[18]putuk kōḷ yāṉiyum [19]poṟṟārp puraviyum,
[20] katikkuṟa vaṭippōr kaviṉpeṟu vītiyum,
cēṇōṅ karuvi tāḻnta cey kuṉṟamum,
[21]vēṇavā mikukkum viri marikkāvum,
viṇṇavar, taṅkaḷ vicumpiṭam maṟantu
naṇṇutaṟku otta [22]naṉṉīriṭaṅkaḷum,
cāliyum kūṭamum [23] tamaṉiyappotiyilum
[24]kōlam kuyiṉṟa koḷki yiṭaṅkaḷum
aṅku uṟinta mātavarkaḷil, kōvalaṉ tantiyākiya mācāttuvāṉum tavattiṟam pūṇṭiruntāṉ.
avaṉik kaṇṭatum, maṇimēkali avaṉ tiruvaṭi paṇintu, tāṉ amuta curapi peṟṟatu mutal,
camayak kaṇakkar tiṟam kēṭṭatum, avar kūṟiyavaṟṟik koḷḷātu, putta tēvaṉatu nallaṟam kēṭka
nayantu aṟavaṇaṉit tēṭi vantatum īṟāka nikaḻn tavi yaṉittum colli muṭittāḷ.
mācāttuvāṉ, iṉit taṉ varalāṟu kūṟaluṟṟu,
tūyōy! niṉṉi eṉ
nalviṉip payaṉ kol nāṉ kaṇṭatu;
tiyal! kēḷ : niṉtātiyum tāyum
ceyta tīviṉiyiṉ ceḻunakar kēṭuṟat
tuṉpuṟa [25]viḷintami kēṭṭuc cukataṉ
aṉpu koḷ aṟattiṟku [26]arukaṉēṉātaliṉ,
maṉittiṟa vāḻkkiyi māyameṉ ṟuṇarntu
tiṉittaṉiyāyiṉum celvamum yākkiyum
niliyā eṉṟē nilipeṟa uṇarntē
[27] maliyā aṟattiṉ mātavam purintēṉ;
eṉṟavaṉ, tāṉ inta vañcimānakarkku vanta kāraṇam kūṟat toṭaṅki,
kuṭakkōc cēralaṉ [28] kuṭṭuvar peruntaki
[29]viṭarccili poṟitta vēntaṉ muṉṉāḷ,
[30]tuppaṭu cevvāyt tuṭiyiṭiyāroṭum –
ippoḻil pukuntāṅku irunta elliyuḷ,
ilaṅkit tīviluḷḷa camaṉoḷi eṉṉum malikkuc ceṉṟu ataṉi valaṅkoṇṭu pōnta tarumacā raṇar,
ip poḻiṟkaṇ vantu kaṟ ṟalattil iruntaṉar. avarkaḷik kaṇṭa aracaṉ avarkaṭku vēṇ ṭum
uṇavum vēṟu pala ciṟappum tantu iṟiñci niṉṟāṉ. avarkaḷ makiḻntu,
piṟappiṉ tuṉpamum [31]piṟavā iṉpamum
[32] aṟattaki mutalvaṉ aruḷiya vāymi
iṉpa vāramutu iṟivaṉ cevimutal
tuṉpam nīṅkac
corintaṉar. annāḷil, avvēntaṟkuk kātaṟ pāṅkaṉāka - irunta vaṉ, niṉ tanti
kōvalaṉukku oṉpatu talimuṟi muṉṉavaṉā vāṉ; avaṉ peyarum kōvalaṉ eṉpatē. avaṉum avar
kūṟiya aṟaṅkaḷik kēṭṭu, taṉ muṉṉōr paṭitta celvattiyē yaṉṟit tāṉ paṭittataṉiyum
kūṭṭi ēḻutāḷil iravalarkku īntu, puttaṉukku oru ciṟanta cittiyam amittaṉaṉ; ataṉik
kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkutaṟkāka yāṉ vantēṉ. īṅkuṟiyum mātavarkaḷ kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattik kaṭal
koḷum eṉṟataṉāl, iṅkē iruntoḻintēṉ.
iṉṉum kēḷāy : naṉṉeṟi mātē!
tīviṉi [33]yuruppac ceṉṟa niṉ tātiyum
tēvariṉ tōṟṟi muṟceyviṉip payattāl,
āṅkat tīviṉi iṉṉum tuyttup
pūṅkoṭi! muṉṉavaṉ pōtiyiṉ nallaṟam
tāṅkiya tavattāl tāṉ tavam tāṅkik
[34]kātali taṉṉoṭu kapiliyam patiyil
nātaṉ nallaṟam kēṭṭu vīṭueytu meṉṟu
[35]aṟputak kiḷavi aṟintōr kūṟac
coṟpayaṉ uṇarntēṉ; tōki ! yāṉum,
annāḷ āṅkavaṉ aṟaneṟi kēṭkuvaṉ
eṉṟāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, maṇimēkalikku, " niṉakku uḷavākum nikaḻcci kaḷi niṉakkuk
kantiṟpāvikkaṇ niṉṟa tuvatikaṉ urittuḷāṉ aṉṟō ? avaṉ urittaṉaṉ eṉṟa ceytiyi aṟavaṇaṉ
collakkēṭṭuḷḷēṉ; avvaṟavaṇaṉē, niṉakku etu nikaḻcci nikaḻumiṭam kāñci eṉṟāṉ. avaṉ
kāñcikkuc ceṉṟa nāḷil niṉ tāyarākiya mātaviyum cutamatiyam uṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar, eṉṟu
colliviṭṭu,
aṉṉati yaṉṟiyum, aṇiyiḻi! kēḷāy :
poṉṉeyiṟ kāñci nāṭu kaviṉaḻintu,
maṉṉuyirmaṭiya maḻivaḷam [36]karattaliṉ,
annakar mātavarkku aiyamiṭuvōr,
iṉmiyiṉ innakar eytiṉar, kāṇāy;
[37] āruyir maruntē! annāṭ [38]ṭakavayiṉ
[39]kāreṉat tōṉṟik kāttal, niṉ kaṭaṉ
eṉa vaṟpuṟuttiṉaṉ. atukēṭṭa maṇimēkali vañcinakariṉ mēṟkup pakkattiṉiṉṟu viṇṇiṭattil
ōṅki, vaṭaticiyil kāñci nōkkic ceṉṟu, atu vaḷaṅkuṉṟip poliviḻantu kiṭappatik
kaṇṭu varunti, naṭunakarellikkaṇ iṟaṅki, toṭukaḻaṟ kiḷḷiyiṉ piṉṉava ṉāṉa iḷaṅkiḷḷi
eṭutta puttacittiyattip paravittoḻutu, annakariṉ teṉmēṟkilirunta pūmpoḻili
yaṭintiruntāḷ. ataṉit tarumatavaṉam eṉpar. avaḷ varavi yaṟinta kañcuki yoru vaṉ,
aracaṉpāl virintu ceṉṟu, vaṇaṅki niṉṟu,
kōvalaṉ maṭanti [40]kuṇa vatam purintōḷ;
nāvalantīvil tāṉ naṉi [41]mik kōḷ,
aṅkiyiṉ ēntiya amuta curapiyoṭu
taṅkātu, ippatit [42] tarumata vaṉattē
vantu tōṉṟiṉaḷ māmaḻi pōla
eṉṟu moḻintāṉ. kēṭṭa vēntaṉ mantiriccuṟṟam cūḻntu varap puṟappaṭṭut taṉakkuk
kantiṟpāvi yurittavi yaṉittum uṇmiyātal kaṇṭu viyanta uḷḷattaṉāy vantu maṇimēkaliyik
kaṇṭu,
ceṅkōl kōṭiyō, ceytavam piḻittō,
[43]koṅkaviḻ kuḻalār kaṟpukkuṟi paṭṭō,
[44]nalattaki nallāy! naṉṉāṭellām
[45]alattaṟ kāli yākiyatu; aṟiyēṉ;
mayaṅkuvēṉ muṉṉar ōr māteyvam tōṉṟi
[46] uyaṅkātoḻi : niṉ uyar tavattāl ōr
kāriki tōṉṟum; avaḷ peruṅ [47]kaṭiñiyiṉ
[48]āruyirmaruntāl akalnilam uyyum;
āṅkavaḷ aruḷāl amararkōṉ ēvaliṉ
[49]tāṅkā māriyum tāṉ naṉi poḻiyum;
aṉṉāḷ inta akaṉakar pukunta
piṉṉāḷ nikaḻum pēraṟam palavāl;
kārvaṟaṅ kūriṉum nīrvaṟaṅ kūrātu;
[50]pāraka vītiyil paṇṭiyōr iḻitta
kōmuki eṉṉum [51]koḻunīr ilañciyoṭu
māmaṇi pallavam vantatu īṅkeṉap
poykiyum poḻilum puṉiyum eṉṟu aṟintu at
teyvatam pōyapiṉ ceytiyām amittatu
ivviṭam
eṉṟu avviṭattik kāṭṭiṉaṉ. atukaṇṭu makiḻnta maṇimēkali, aracaṉikkoṇṭē
puttapīṭikiyiyum, tīvatilakiyiyum maṇi mēkalā teyvattiyum vaṇaṅki vaḻipaṭutaṟkuriya
kōyil eṉṟa ivaṟṟi iyaṟṟuvittu, avaṟṟiṟkup pūciyum viḻāvum ceyvittu,
amutacurapiyip pīṭikiyil vittu vaḻipaṭṭu ētti, " pacippiṇi yāl varuntum ellā
uyirkaḷum varuka" eṉṟaṉaḷ. eṉṟalum,
kāṇār kēḷār kālmuṭa māṉōr
[52] pēṇā mākkaḷ [53]pēcār [54]piṇittōr
[55]paṭiva nōṉ piyar pacinōyuṟṟōr
[56] maṭi.nal kūrnta makkaḷ yāvarum
paṉṉūṟāyiram vilaṅkiṉ tokutiyum
maṉṉuyi raṭaṅkalum vantoruṅ [57]kīṇṭi,
aruntiyōrk kellām āruyir maruntāyp,
peruntavar [58]kippey picciyiṉ payaṉum,
nīrum nilaṉum kālamum karuviyum
[59]cīrpeṟa vittiya vittiṉ viḷivum
perukiya teṉṉap peruvaḷam curappa
[60]vacittoḻil utavi vaḷan tantatu
eṉa maṇimēkaliyip pārāṭṭikkoṇṭu cella, mātaviyum cutamatiyum eṉṟa ivaruṭaṉ
aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ am maṇimēkaliyiṉ aṟac cāliyi vantaṭintaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ tiruvaṭi vaṇaṅki,
nīrāl viḷakki, irukkiyil irutti, nalluṇavu iṉi tūṭṭi,
[61] pācilittiriyalum [62]paḷitamum paṭittu
[63]vāyvatāka eṉ [64] maṉap pāṭṭu aṟam
eṉa maṇimēkali iṟiñci niṉṟāḷ.
---
[1]. kāḻiyar - piṭṭuvāṇikar. [2]. kūviyar - appavāṇikar.
[3]. paṇ - pāli. kuṟiñci, marutam, cevvaḻi mutaliya ici. tiṟam - ivaṟṟiṉ kiḷi.
[4]. nilam - eḻuttu, aci, cīr eṉṟa iṭam, kalam - yāḻ. kaṇ ṭam - kural.
[5]. vilaṅku aram - vāḷaram. [6]. vaḷi pōḻnar - caṅkaṟuppavar.
[7]. irīiya - iruntu vāḻum.
[8]. vēttiyal - vēntarkkāṭum kūttu. potuviyal - ēṉiyel: lārkkumāṭum kūttu.
[9]. vēṟupālāka - vēṟu vēṟu pakutiyāka. eṇ vakikkūlam - nel, pul, varaku, tiṉi,
cāmi, iṟuṅku, tōri, mūṅkil nel.
[10]. kuvi iya - kuvitta.
[11]. mākatar - iruntēttuvōr. cūtar - niṉṟēttuvōr, vētā ḷikar - palavakit
tāḷattiṟkēṟpa āṭuvōr. potuvar - potumakaḷir.
[12]. kaṇ nuḻikallā - kaṭpārvi cella muṭiyāta. aṟuviyar - uṭi viṟpavar. [13].
maṉṉiya - perukavuḷḷa. [14]. aruntoḻil - ariya "vitikak kiriyikaḷ"
[15]. maṉṟam - ūr maṉṟam; naṭunāṭṭār, itaṉi " maṉṟa veḷi " eṟu iṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa.
[16]. potiyil - potuviṭam. [17]. catukkam - nāṉku teru kūṭumiṭam.
[18]. putukkōḷyāṉi - putuvatākap piṭikkappaṭṭa yāṉi.
[19]. poṟṟār - poṉṉāl ceyta kiṇkiṇi māli.
[20]. katikku uṟa-naṭiyil mēmpāṭu eyta. vaṭippōr payiṟṟuvōr.
[21]. vēṇavā - mikka avā. vēṭki + avā-vēṇavā.
[22]. naṉṉīriṭam - poyki mutalāyiṉa.
[23]. tamaṉiyap potiyil - poṉṉālākiya ampalam.
[24]. kōlam - ōviyam. kuyiṉṟa - amiyacceyta, koḷkiyiṭam tavaccāli.
[25]. viḷintami - iṟantatu. [26]. arukaṉēṉ - pakkuvamuṭiyēṉ.
[27]. maliyā . māṟupaṭāta'. [28]. kuṭṭuvar - cērar kuṭiyiṉar.
[29]. viṭarccili poṟitta vēntaṉ - imayavarampaṉ neṭuñcēra lātaṉ. viṭar - īṇṭuppāṟi.
cili - viṟpoṟi.
[30]. tuppu aṭu - pavaḷattiyotta.
[31]. piṟavā iṉpam - piṟavāmiyāl varum iṉpam.
[32]. aṟattaki mutalvaṉ - aṟattāl takutipeṟṟa puttaṉ.
[33]. uruppa - varuttic celutta. [34]. kātali - kaṇṇa ki.
[35]. aṟputakkiḷavi - aṟputamāṉa coṟkaḷ. [36]. karattaliṉ - illātoḻitalāl.
[37]. āruyir maruntu - niṟinta uyirkaḷi varuttum pacippiṇi pōk kum uṇavākiya
maruntu tarupavaḷē.
[38]. nāṭṭakavayiṉ - nāṭṭiṟkuḷ. [39]. kār eṉa - maḻimukilpōla.
[40]. kuṇavatam - naṟkuṇa nalviratam : kuṇameṉṟē koṇṭu vatam eṉpatip pakutipporuḷ
vikutiyākka lumoṉṟu. “kuṇavatamkoḷutta . lāmō" eṉṟa cintāmaṇip pāṭṭukku
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urippatu. kāṇka.
[41]. mikkōḷ - mēmpaṭṭāḷ. [42]. tarumatavaṉam - tarumattik koṭukkum vaṉam.
[43]. koṅkaviḻ kuḻalār - tēṉmalarum kuḻaliyuṭiya kaṟpuṭimakaḷir .
[44]. nalattaki - naṟkuṇamum aḻakum uṭiya.
[45]. alattaṟkāli - vaṟumikkālam.
[46]. uyaṅkātoḻi - varuttaṟka. [47]. kaṭiñi - amutacurapi yeṉṉum kalam.
[48]. āruyir maruntu - uṇavu. nilam - nilattumakkaḷ.
[49]. tāṅkā māri - itukāṟum maḻipeytu uyirkaḷit tāṅkāta maḻi.
[50]. pārakavīti - nilattiṭattu.
[51]. koḻunīr ilañci - mikka nīrporuntiya poyki,
[52]. pēṇāmākkaḷ - piṟarāl pātukākkappaṭāta makkaḷ; ātaravaṟṟavar
[53]. pēcār - ūmi. [54]. piṇittōr - nōyuṟṟōr.
[55]. paṭivanōṉpiyar - tavavaṭivum nōṉpumuṭiyavar.
[56]. maṭinalkūrnta - uṭukkum uṭiyillāta. [57]. īṇṭi - niṟintu.
[58]. kippey picci - kiyiṭattē iṭappaṭum picci.
[59]. cīrpeṟa - naṉku amiya. nīr mutaliyaṉa cīrpeṟa vittiyavittiṉ -viḷivum,
picciyiṉ payaṉum perukiya teṉṉavaḷam curappa eṉka.
[60]. vaci - maḻi . [61]. pācilit tiriyal - paciya iliyākiya veṟṟili.
[62]. paḷitam - paccik karuppūram. itu pāḷitam eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.. " aṭaku pulāl
pākupāḷi tamumuṇṇāṉ kaṭal pōluṅ kalviyavaṉ" eṉ puḻippōla,
[63]. vāyvatāka - tappātu payaṉ vaḻaṅkuka.
[64]. maṉappāṭṭu aṟam - maṉattiṭattē koṇṭiyaṟṟum aṟam.

29. tavattiṟam pūṇṭu tarumam kēṭṭa kāti

taṉ aṭikaḷil vaṇaṅki niṉṟa maṇimēkaliyi nōkki, aṟa. vaṇavaṭikaḷ, 'nākanāṭṭaracaṉ makaḷāṉa
pīlivaḷi tāṉ peṟṟa makaṉik kampaḷac ceṭṭiyiṭam tantu cōḻaṉiṭam cērppikkac colla, am
makaṉi aḻittup pōnta aṉṟiravē, avaṉatu vaṅkam kaṭalil kaviḻntatu. tappik kariyaṭinta ac
ceṭṭi, kāvi rippūm paṭṭiṉatti yaṭintu cōḻaṉiṭam nikaḻntatu kūṟi ṉaṉ.. aracaṉ makaṉ
piri vāṟṟātu avaṉit tēṭik kaṭaṟkariyilum kāṉaṟcōliyilum alintu tiriyavē,
pūmpukār nakaram intira viḻāvi! āṟṟātu nekiḻttatu. itu niṟka,
taṉ viḻāt tavirtaliṉ [1]vāṉavar talivaṉ,
niṉṉuyirt [2] tanti neṭuṅkulat tutitta
maṉṉuyir [3]mutalvaṉ makara [4]vēliyuḷ,
[5]muṉṉiya vaṅkam [6] muṅkik kēṭuṟap
poṉṉiṉ ūci pacuṅkam paḷattut
tuḷḷiya teṉṉat toṭukaṭal uḻantuḻi,
eḻunāḷ elli [7] iṭukkaṇ vanteytā
vaḻuvāc cīlam [8]vāymiyiṟ koṇṭa
pāṉmiyiṉ, taṉātu pāṇṭu kampaḷam tāṉ
[9]naṭukkuṟṟa taṉmi nōkki,
ātimutalvaṉ pōti mūlattu
nāta ṉāvōṉ naḷi nīrp parappil
[10] evvamuṟ ṟāṉ taṉatu evvam tīr eṉa
urittāṉ. atukēṭṭa maṇimēkalā teyvam pōntu avaṉukku vēṇṭum utaviyiṉic ceytatu.
itaṉi muṉṉaṟiyāta avaṉukku,
aṟavaracāḷavum aṟavāḻi yuruṭṭavum,

appāl yāṉum niṉ tāyarum niṉ poruṭṭu in nakari yaṭintōm," eṉṟu colli

[2]. tanti - kōvalaṉ.

cīlatti.

pāṇṭu kampaḷam - veḷḷikkampalamākiya irukki,
[9]. naṭukkuṟṟataṉmi - cīlamum vāymiyum koṇṭōr irup piṉi yuṇarttaṟku intiraṉatu
kampalam aciyum eṉpavākaliṉ, īṇṭu atu naṭuṅkiṟṟu; attaṉmiyi avvintiraṉ nōkkiṉāṉ
eṉka. pōtimūlattu nātaṉāvōṉ - pōticattuvar; puttaṉātaṟkuriyavaṉ.
[10]. evvamuṟṟāṉ - tuṉpamuṟṟāṉ. taṉatu - avaṉatu.
[11]. aivakic camayam- aḷavi vātamutalākavuḷḷa aintum,(1) ācīvaka vātam nikaṇṭa vātaṅkaḷum.
(2) cāṅkiyam, (3) vicēṭikam, (4) pūtam, (5) āka aivakic camayam.
[12. cevvitaṉmiyiṉ - ovvoṉṟum meyyāṉa tāka illāmi yāl. |

[15]. pakkam - itu mēṟkōḷ, piratiññi eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
[16]. aṭukkaḷi - aṭuppu. [17]. eṉal - eṉṟu tuṇital.
[18]. capakkam - tuṇiyappaṭṭa karuttu veḷippaṭṭa pakkam.

30. pavattiṟa maṟukeṉap pāvi nōṟṟa kāti

tāṉam tāṅkic cīlam mēṟkoṇṭu paḻampiṟappuṇarnta maṇi mēkali, putta taruma caṅkatti

[1]. aṟivu - pari niruvāṇattiyaṟiyum aṟivu. vaṟitāy- illiyātalāl,
[2]. niṟikālattu - takutiyāl amiyuṅkālattil.
[3]. muṭitayaṅku amarar - taliyil muṭiviḷaṅkum tēvar.
[4]. tuṭitalōkam - aṟuvakit teyvalōkattuḷ oṉṟu. oḻiya vaṟitāka.
kutamaputtar cuttōtaṉarkkup putalvarāyp piṟappataṟku muṉ irunta ulakam itu eṉpar.
[5]. māraṉ - maṉattit tīyavaḻiyiṟ celutti mayakkum tēvaṉ.
[6]. kuṟṟa mūṉṟu - kāmam, vekuḷi, mayakkam. [7]. vāmaṉ - puttaṉ.
[8]. ēmakkaṭṭuri - iṉpattip payakkum poruḷuri.
[9]. eṇṇil puttarkaḷ - aṟamkuṉ ṟiya kālaṅkaḷilellām palappala puttarkaḷ tōṉṟi aṟam
valiyuṟuttiṉar eṉpar; ataṉāl puttarkaḷ eṇṇilar eṉka.
[10]. niliyila - niliyillātavi. vaṟiya - payaṉillātavi.
[11. uliyā vīṭṭiṟku - aḻivillāta iṉpa vīṭu.
[12]. nāl vaki vāymi - tuṉpam, tuṉpattōṟṟam, tuṉpanīkkam, tuṉpa nīkka neṟi.
[13]. aivakik kantam - " uruvu, nukarcci, kutippē' pāvaṉi, uḷḷa aṟivu ivi
aiṅkantamāvaṉa 1 (maṇi. 30: 189-90).
[14]. āṟuvaki vaḻakku - uṇmi vaḻakku, iṉmi vaḻakku, uḷḷatu cārnta uṇmi vaḻakku,
illatu cārnta iṉmi vaḻakku, uḷḷatu cārnta iṉmi vaḻakku, illatu cārnta uṇmi vaḻakku.
[15]. nayam nāṉku: oṟṟumi nayam. vēṟṟuminayam. puriviṉminayam iyalpunayam.
[16]. nālvaki viṉāviṭi - (piṉṉark kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.)
[17]. niṉmiti - uṇṭākkutal. [18]. ūḻpāṭu - muṭivupaṭutal.
[19]. iṟṟu - ittaṉmittu.
[20]. āyiṉ - ārāyntu pārkkumiṭattu. aṟiyār naraku aṟikuvar eṉka .
[21]. muyaṟ kōṭu - muyalukkuk kompu. [22]. kuṟaḷi - kōṭ collutal.
[23]. veḵkal - piṟar poruḷik kavara virumpal.
[24]. pollākkāṭci - mayakkam; uṇmiyaṟiyāmi.
[25]. uruppaṉa - tōṉṟu vaṉa. (urukkoḷvaṉa eṉpatu coṟ poruḷ.)
[26]. payaṉ - meypporuḷ.
[27]. uḷḷakkavaliyiṉ - uḷḷattē nīṅkāta tuṉpat tuṭaṉ.
[28]. colliya pattu - uṭampilum, collilum uḷḷattilum tōṉṟuvaṉa eṉa mēlēyuritta
pattukkuṟṟam.
[29]. cīlam - ainteṉṟum, patteṉṟum, eṭṭeṉṟum kūṟuvar.
kollāmi, poyyāmi, kaḷḷāmi, kāmamiṉmi, karavāmi. eṉa aintu; piṟavum ivipōlvaṉa.
[30]. makiḻcci - iṉpam.
[31]. purivu - toḻil, pulaṉ koḷātatu - nukarappaṭātatu.
[32]. mēvutal - poruntutal. [33]. ārāmi - nirampāmi.

[36]. uṟappuṇar uḷḷam - mikakkūṭiya viññāṉam.
[37]. iṭumpi purital - tuṉpam ceytal.

[39]. vīḻkatir - maṟiyum ñāyiṟu.
